These findings establish path signatures not as semantic generalizers, but as rigorous mathematical lenses that explicitly encode discrete lexical structures and word order, offering an interpretable alternative to opaque deep learning for lexical tracking.

</details>


### [36] [SimXAI: A Simple Drop-in Layer Increases Interaction Quality and Understanding in Conversational XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41856)
*Van Bach Nguyen,Qianli Wang,Nils Feldhus,Jörg Schlötterer,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以大语言模型为核心的可解释人工智能对话接口，在保持解释忠实性的同时，更好地处理依赖上下文的追问，并提升用户任务完成表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式可解释人工智能系统通常采用意图识别、对话管理和模板生成等分离模块。尽管这类流程能够忠实调用底层解释方法，却不易调整，也难以理解结合对话上下文的后续问题。研究旨在简化对话层，同时保留对现有可解释人工智能资源的可靠访问。

Method: 研究提出一种轻量级、以大语言模型为核心的对话层，由单个能够调用工具的大语言模型取代传统的多个对话模块。它并非独立的智能体，而是连接用户与外部可解释人工智能资源的模块化接口。研究还构建评测套件，考察单轮与多轮请求解析、解释忠实性及上下文理解能力。

Result: 在已有及新建数据集上，该方法取得了较好的功能选择准确率和较高的解释忠实性，并更有效地处理依赖上下文的请求。在一项用户研究中，研究者仅将一个代表性传统系统的对话层替换为该方法；用户随后以更高准确率、较少轮次完成任务。

Conclusion: 以大语言模型为核心的模块化对话层能够改进传统的对话式可解释人工智能设计，在维持解释忠实性的同时提升上下文处理能力和整体对话质量。

Abstract: Conversational explainable AI (ConvXAI) agents often use GUS-style architectures with separate modules for intent recognition, dialogue management, and template-based response generation. While faithful to the underlying XAI methods, these pipelines are hard to adapt and often struggle with context-dependent follow-up questions. We present SimXAI, a lightweight LLM-centric conversational layer that replaces these modules with a single tool-calling LLM that bridges access to existing XAI resources. Rather than a standalone agent, SimXAI serves as a modular interface that enables conversational access to external XAI resources. We further introduce an evaluation suite for single- and multi-turn parsing, faithfulness, and contextualization. Across existing and new datasets, SimXAI achieves strong function-selection accuracy and high faithfulness while better handling context-dependent requests. In a user study replacing only the conversational layer of TalkToModel, a representative GUS-based agent, users completed tasks more accurately and in fewer turn. Overall, SimXAI improves GUS-based design while preserving faithfulness and improving conversational quality.

</details>


### [37] [Text as an Index for Zero-Shot Cross-Domain Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41883)
*Yin Yi,Haiping Ma,Siyu Song,Ziwen Wang,Shangshang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出零样本跨领域知识追踪方法：利用题目文本为目标领域题目建立索引，再结合学生历史作答与行为动态预测下一次作答；在三个公开数据集的留一领域实验中，整体表现优于所比较的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新学习平台往往提供题目文本，却缺少可用于训练的学生作答记录，因此需要在没有目标领域训练数据的情况下迁移知识追踪能力。现有方法依赖题目语义进行迁移，但不同平台的题目语义存在差异，削弱了直接迁移的可靠性。

Method: 方法将行为序列建模与领域内历史证据构建分开。掌握动态编码器仅根据作答正误学习行为变化，不使用题目标识或文本嵌入；领域内题目寻址模块通过冻结的文本编码器和无监督的领域内投影，为题目构造固定地址。随后，文本寻址核依据题目地址汇总学生此前相关作答，形成针对当前题目的历史正确率摘要。跨片段延续机制使模型能访问更早的作答，而不必延长行为编码器的输入序列；证据量读出则保留归一化时被丢弃的核权重总量，并将其对数压缩后与行为状态、历史正确率摘要共同用于预测。所有可训练参数仅在源领域学习，目标领域推断时使用公开题目文本和学生已观测历史。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上进行的留一领域实验显示，该方法在所有目标领域的平均曲线下面积和准确率均为所比较基线中的最高。消融实验表明，文本寻址和跨片段延续均有贡献。

Conclusion: 研究表明，跨平台迁移不必直接把题目文本当作可迁移的预测表示；可将文本用于领域内题目寻址，再与独立建模的作答动态及历史证据结合，从而实现无需目标领域训练的知识追踪。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) predicts a student's next response from their history. New platforms often have question texts but no response logs for training, motivating zero-shot cross-domain KT. Existing transfer and cold-start methods use question semantics, but cross-platform semantic discrepancies limit transferability. We propose TIZSKT, which separates behavioral sequence modeling from domain-local historical evidence construction and uses text as an index rather than a directly transferable predictive representation. The Mastery-Dynamics Encoder learns correctness dynamics without question identifiers or text embeddings. Domain-Local Question Addressing builds fixed question addresses using a frozen text encoder and an unsupervised per-domain projection. The Text-Addressing Kernel uses these addresses to aggregate the current student's previous outcomes into a question-specific historical correctness summary. Cross-Segment Continuation retains access to earlier responses without extending the behavioral encoder's input sequence. Evidence-Volume Read-Out preserves the total kernel mass discarded by normalization and combines its log-compressed value with the behavioral state and historical correctness summary to predict the next response. All trainable parameters are learned from source domains; target inference uses public question texts and the current student's observed history without target-domain training. Leave-one-domain-out experiments on three public datasets show that TIZSKT achieves the highest mean AUC and accuracy among evaluated baselines on all target domains. Ablations confirm the contributions of text addressing and cross-segment continuation.

</details>


### [38] [Aligning MLLMs with the Latent Structure of Human Cognition via Behavior-Derived Semantic Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41951)
*Ning E,Changde Du,Yizhuo Lu,Huiguang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出一种将人类行为反映出的认知维度融入多模态大语言模型的方法，使模型的相似性判断及其理由更贴近人类，并提升了模型表征与人脑活动的对应程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽已在多种推理任务中表现出色，但其内部表征和判断依据是否符合人类认知结构仍不清楚。研究旨在弥合模型判断与人类相似性判断之间的差异。

Method: 研究以一个由约四百七十万次人类“找出异类”判断构建的语义空间为基础，将物体表示为六十六个可解释维度。针对每组三个物体，推断人类最可能依据的关键维度，并将其转化为与该维度对应的语言理由，再训练模型同时给出选择和理由。

Result: 实验显示，该方法提升了模型与人类相似性判断的一致性，同时保持了在标准多模态基准上的竞争力。研究还通过独立的人类脑成像数据集进行表征相似性分析，发现模型与人脑表征的对齐程度有所提高。

Conclusion: 研究表明，可利用大规模人类行为数据提取认知结构，并将其作为训练依据，以更数据驱动的方式增强多模态大语言模型与人类判断及表征的对齐。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive performance across a wide range of reasoning tasks, yet it remains unclear whether their internal representations and decision criteria align with the latent structure of human cognition. In this work, we propose a novel framework to align MLLMs with the multidimensional mental representations underlying human similarity judgements. We build on a previously established behavior-derived semantic embedding space, in which objects are represented along 66 interpretable dimensions derived from 4.70 million human Odd-One-Out (O1O) judgements. For each triplet, we infer the most salient decision dimension and convert it into a dimension-grounded linguistic rationale. This enables MLLMs to learn both the final choice and a justification consistent with the inferred behavioral criterion. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach improves model consistency with human similarity judgements while retaining competitive performance on standard multimodal benchmarks. Furthermore, using searchlight Representational Similarity Analysis (RSA) on an independent fMRI Natural Scenes Dataset (NSD), we observe increased representational alignment between model and human. Ultimately, our findings point to a data-driven route for incorporating human cognitive structure into MLLMs alignment. The code is available \href{https://github.com/ening-open/cogalign}{here}.

</details>


### [39] [TurnTurk-GPT: Text-Based Turn-Taking Prediction for Turkish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42010)
*Ahmet Tuğrul Bayrak,Fatma Nur Korkmaz,Nur Yağmur Tuncer,Mustafa Sertaç Türkel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出并评估一种面向土耳其语对话的文本轮次结束预测模型；它优于多语种基线，但对未见说话人的优势不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮次交替预测是对话系统理解何时应接话的关键。土耳其语的黏着形态可能在词尾提供重要线索，而现有多语种检测器及零样本模型在土耳其语真实对话中的表现和局限仍需系统评估。

Method: 研究以土耳其语生成式预训练模型为基础，使用轮次预测目标，在合成对话和真实对话上微调。评估采用近似流式语音识别结果的文本：去除标点并统一为小写，在词级决策点判断轮次是否结束；通过录音不重叠和说话人不重叠的数据划分、嵌套阈值选择及配对集群自助法检验，与规则基线、零样本土耳其语模型和多语种轮次结束检测器进行比较。

Result: 该模型在录音不重叠和说话人不重叠评估中均优于多语种检测器；相较零样本模型，仅在测试说话人也出现在训练数据时表现更好，在未见说话人上两者无显著差异。遮蔽轮次末词的词尾会使平均精确率最多下降百分之四十四，而遮蔽句中词的词尾仅下降百分之二，表明土耳其语动词词尾形态是重要线索。仅用合成对话训练的模型表现最弱；说话人停顿时，零样本模型和多语种基线的表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 实验显示，针对土耳其语训练的轮次预测模型能够超过通用多语种检测器，但其相对零样本模型的优势受说话人是否见于训练集影响。土耳其语词尾形态对轮次结束判断尤为重要，合成数据单独使用不足以获得最佳效果。研究同时公开模型与评估流程，便于后续复现和比较。

Abstract: This paper presents TurnTurk-GPT, a text-based turn-taking predictor for Turkish obtained by fine-tuning a Turkish GPT-2 model with the TurnGPT objective on synthetic and real dialogue, and evaluates it on real Turkish conversation against rule baselines, a zero-shot Turkish language model and a widely used multilingual end-of-turn detector. Evaluation uses word-level decision points on unpunctuated, lower-cased text approximating the output of streaming speech recognisers, recording-disjoint and speaker-disjoint folds, nested threshold selection and paired cluster bootstrap tests. TurnTurk-GPT outperforms the multilingual detector under both recording-disjoint and speaker-disjoint evaluation. It outperforms the zero-shot language model only when the test speakers occur in training; on unseen speakers the two are statistically indistinguishable. Removing the suffixes of the turn-final word reduces AP by up to 44%, against 2% for a sentence-internal word, locating the Turkish end-of-turn cue in verb-final morphology. Synthetic dialogue alone yields the weakest trained model, and when a speaker pauses the zero-shot and multilingual baselines remain close to chance. The model and the evaluation protocol are publicly released.

</details>


### [40] [Asynchronous Flow Map Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42018)
*Chanhyuk Lee,Wonjung Kim,Patrick Pynadath,Jaehoon Yoo,Satoshi Hayakawa,Ruqi Zhang,Jinwoo Kim,Seunghoon Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出异步流映射语言模型，通过学习词元之间的生成顺序，将顺序信息融入单次前向计算，实现一次并行生成全部词元，并在数独求解和数学文字题代码生成任务上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词元的预测顺序会显著影响生成质量。自回归模型和掩码扩散模型可在多步生成过程中利用不同顺序，但如何将顺序信息有效用于单步并行语言建模，仍不明确。

Method: 提出异步流映射语言模型，学习异步流的流映射。异步流通过逐词元的时间安排控制各词元的去噪进度，从而编码预测顺序。论文构建了同时支持预设安排与可学习、可随状态调整安排的一般异步流，并证明这些流能够定义有效的流映射；此外，还提出相应的流映射蒸馏方法，使模型将顺序信息内化到隐藏计算中。

Result: 模型能够在一次前向计算中并行生成所有词元。在数独求解和数学文字题代码生成任务中，论文报告其单步性能达到新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 研究表明，预测顺序不仅能用于多步迭代生成，也可通过异步流映射融入单步并行模型。该方法为兼顾生成效率与质量提供了新途径，但摘要未提供具体数据或实验细节，无法进一步评估其优势幅度及适用范围。

Abstract: The order in which tokens are predicted in language modeling has a profound impact on the quality of generated sequences. Autoregressive and masked diffusion models iteratively fill in tokens and can exploit such orderings across generation steps, but how to leverage an ordering for one-step parallel language modeling is unclear. We introduce asynchronous flow map language models (aFMLMs), which incorporate prediction order into one-step modeling by learning the flow maps of asynchronous flows. These flows specify an ordering through a tokenwise time schedule that controls the relative denoising progress of tokens. We formulate a general class of asynchronous flows that admit both prescribed and learned, state-adaptive schedules, show that they define valid flow maps, and develop new methods for their flow map distillation. The resulting models can generate all tokens in a single forward pass by internalizing the ordering encoded in the flow into their hidden computations. On Sudoku solving and code generation for GSM8K math word problems, aFMLM establishes new state-of-the-art one-step performance.

</details>


### [41] [CRoS: Causality Reward on Scrambled CoTs Improves Accuracy and Reasoning Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42054)
*Xiao Yu,Justin Chih-Yao Chen,Zaid Khan,Hyunji Lee,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种通过打乱推理步骤顺序、但保留各步骤内容来评估思维链因果作用的方法，并将其用于训练奖励；实验显示，该方法在未见任务准确率、推理质量和思维链忠实度方面优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习通常只依据最终答案是否正确进行奖励，无法确保思维链真正促成了答案。已有因果性评估方法通过删除步骤或注入错误来扰动思维链，但这些操作会改变步骤中的信息，因此测得的高因果性可能只是模型对信息变化敏感，而非推理过程本身具有因果作用。将这类指标用作奖励，还可能诱导模型生成信息量较低的思维链，并削弱探索和自我纠错。

Method: 论文提出“乱序思维链因果奖励”方法：改变推理步骤的排列顺序，同时保留每一步的内容，再观察最终答案的变化，以此评估思维链对答案的因果贡献。该设计旨在减少传统扰动造成的信息变化混杂，并将所得因果性指标用于模型训练奖励。

Result: 在推理健身房基准上使用四十亿参数的宝可梦三模型进行实验。面对五个未见过的逻辑与游戏数据集，该方法准确率为百分之四十六点八，高于最强基线的百分之四十三点八。其思维链质量指标在从一到一百二十八的各个采样规模下均稳定优于基线，平均得分为百分之四十三点四，对比基线为百分之四十一点四。思维链忠实度方面，提示承认得分为百分之三十四点零，高于基线的百分之二十点二。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在评估思维链因果性时，保留推理步骤内容并仅改变步骤顺序，可以减轻信息变化造成的混杂。将这种指标作为训练奖励，有望同时提升模型的泛化准确率、推理质量与思维链忠实度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become the standard method for training language models to reason. However, RLVR typically relies solely on outcome correctness, which does not guarantee that the CoT causally contributes to the final answer. Without this connection, outcome rewards may fail to reinforce high-quality reasoning. Existing causality metrics assess CoT causality by perturbing the CoT, such as deleting reasoning steps or injecting errors, and measuring changes in the final answer. However, these perturbations inevitably alter information in each step. Consequently, high causality scores may reflect sensitivity to information changes introduced by perturbation rather than true causality. Such confounding effects further induce undesirable reasoning behaviors when these metrics are used as training rewards, including generating less informative CoTs and reducing exploration and self-correction. To address this problem, we propose CRoS (Causality Reward on Scrambled CoTs), a novel CoT causality metric that perturbs the order of reasoning steps without altering content, thereby preserving information within each step. On Reasoning Gym with Gemma-3-4B, CRoS outperforms existing causality and outcome rewards in out-of-domain accuracy, CoT quality, and CoT faithfulness. Specifically, CRoS achieves 46.8% accuracy on five unseen logic and games datasets, outperforming the strongest baseline (43.8%). It also achieves better CoT quality, consistently outperforming baselines in CoT-Pass@K from K=1 to 128, averaging 43.4% vs. 41.4%. For CoT faithfulness, CRoS achieves a hint acknowledgment score of 34.0% vs. 20.2%.

</details>


### [42] [PersonaChatBench: Evaluating Chat Assistants with Diverse Simulated Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42060)
*Zheyuan Deng,Taoran Li,Jingwen Zhang,Yuante Li,Dachuan Zhao,Fulei Zhang,Yunkai Zhang,Zhikun Xu,Yajie Liao,Haichao Zhang,Haodi Hu,Cong Wang,Chen Yang,Siyang Wu,Defu Cao,Xu Hu,Haoming Li,Haixin Wang,Robert George,Weizhi Zhang,Yuchu Wang,Zhejian Peng,Yueying Li,Wanting Jiang,Shushu Liang,Zhiyuan Yu,Yifan Wang,Jintao Huang,Yijun Wang,Yuexing Hao,Xiaomin Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一套评测框架，通过固定任务、改变用户人格与需求，衡量对话助手的任务能力及其对不同用户的适应程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测常以单一、配合度高的用户为中心，难以揭示助手面对不同背景、偏好和沟通方式时表现是否稳定。总体任务分数也可能掩盖助手未能满足特定用户需求的问题。

Method: 研究提出“人格对话基准”，将同一任务与多个模拟用户配对；每位用户由一张私有人格卡引导，体现不同需求、偏好和互动行为。在任务保持不变、用户条件变化的设置下，研究评估了涵盖六个领域的二十种语言模型，并分别考察任务结果、对用户明确表达需求的满足程度，以及模拟器对设定用户的还原忠实度。

Result: 模型的任务表现和需求满足度会随用户人格及领域而变化。较强的任务能力并不必然意味着能够满足个体需求；模型满足需求方面的相对优势也无法稳定地跨领域延续。该基准能够呈现汇总分数容易掩盖的用户间差异。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估助手服务不同用户的一致性提供了基础，有助于识别哪些用户受益较多、哪些需求仍未得到满足，并为改进助手适应能力、使其更好地服务日常应用中的多样化用户提供方向。

Abstract: Conversational assistants are expected to help people with diverse backgrounds, preferences, and communication styles. Yet evaluations centered on a single cooperative user provide limited insight into how reliably an assistant serves users with different personas. We introduce PersonaChatBench, a framework for evaluating task competence and responsiveness to diverse user needs. PersonaChatBench pairs the same underlying task with multiple simulated users, each guided by a private persona card that specifies distinct needs, preferences, and interaction behaviors. By holding the task fixed while varying the user, the framework examines how assistant performance changes across user conditions. We evaluate 20 language models across six domains, separately assessing native task outcomes, fulfillment of expressed persona needs, and simulator fidelity. Our results show that task performance and need fulfillment vary across personas and domains. Strong task performance does not necessarily translate into satisfying individual needs, and models' relative strengths in meeting those needs are not consistent across domains. PersonaChatBench makes these differences measurable, revealing gaps obscured by aggregate scores and helping identify which users an assistant serves well and where adaptation is needed. It provides a basis for evaluating consistency across users and guiding the development of assistants that better accommodate diverse needs in everyday applications.

</details>


### [43] [RangeSWA: Training-Free Length Extrapolation for Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42084)
*Guangfu Guo,Xiaoqian Lu,Linsey Pang,Weiran Yao,Haolin Chen,Long Cheng,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需重新训练的长文本外推方法，通过改进注意力机制与位置处理，缓解连续扩散语言模型生成长文本时的重复和质量下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散语言模型用于文本生成具有潜力，但生成长度超过训练长度时，容易出现文本重复、语义漂移和生成质量退化。研究旨在提升模型对长文本的处理能力，同时避免额外训练。

Method: 该方法结合局部滑动窗口注意力、远程分块摘要和动态内容检索，以兼顾局部细节与远距离上下文；并通过位置重映射、条件注意力校正，以及随去噪阶段调整窗口的策略，缓解长度外推带来的退化。

Result: 在多种连续扩散语言模型上的实验表明，该方法能够降低长文本生成的困惑度和文本重复率。摘要未提供具体数值或模型间比较结果。

Conclusion: 该方法通过无需训练的注意力与位置机制调整，增强了连续扩散语言模型的长文本生成表现。现有摘要支持其在降低困惑度和重复率方面有效，但尚不足以判断其适用范围、计算开销及在其他质量指标上的表现。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion language models show promising potential in text generation tasks, but are prone to problems such as text repetition, semantic drift, and generation quality degradation when the generation length exceeds the training length. To address these challenges, we propose RangeSWA, a training-free length extrapolation method. The method combines local sliding-window attention, remote block summaries, and dynamic content retrieval to preserve local details and support long-range context access. In addition, generation degradation caused by length extrapolation is mitigated through positional remapping, conditioning attention correction, and denoising-stage-adaptive window scheduling. We conducted experiments on multiple continuous diffusion language models, and the results show that RangeSWA can effectively reduce generative perplexity and text repetition rates in long-text generation.

</details>


### [44] [Subquadratic Attention with a Hierarchical Tree-Structured Indexer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42112)
*Leon Lufkin,Cutter Dawes,Alexander Speicher,Kamesh Krishnamurthy,Tomás Figliolia,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于树形层级索引的稀疏注意力方法，通过递归压缩键信息并逐层路由查询，使注意力计算随序列长度呈次二次增长，同时保留较强的检索与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力的计算随序列长度平方增长，限制了大语言模型处理超长上下文。现有方法各有不足：固定状态的滑动窗口或线性注意力难以匹配注意力的表达能力，而部分稀疏注意力方法的索引开销仍呈二次增长，因而只带来常数级加速。

Method: 提出 Zyphra 层级注意力：在键值缓存上构建递归压缩键摘要的树结构；处理新查询时，从树顶向下遍历，以路由方式筛选出少量键，再对其执行稀疏注意力。逐步增加树的深度后，路由与最终注意力的总成本随序列长度呈次线性增长，使整体注意力计算达到次二次、但仍超线性的复杂度。该方法可通过使用数十亿级词元进行短期、低成本的散度蒸馏，应用于已有预训练模型。

Result: 摘要称，该方法在长上下文检索、复杂推理、数学及智能体编程评测中保持了模型表现，并且对树结构几何较为稳健，最多测试至六层深树。性能模型显示，其计算复杂度为次二次；当上下文超过一百万词元时，预计可显著优于其他方法，并有望支持数亿至数十亿词元的有效上下文。

Conclusion: 层级树索引为稀疏注意力提供了一条降低索引开销的路径，有望缓解超长上下文处理中的计算瓶颈。论文报告的扩展潜力主要来自性能模型；摘要未提供具体基准数值，因此实际收益仍需结合完整实验评估。

Abstract: The quadratic scaling of attention is a fundamental bottleneck limiting the long-context capabilities of large language models (LLMs). A variety of approaches have attempted to circumvent this, but they either fail to match attention's expressiveness (e.g., sliding-window or linear attention variants with fixed-size state) or only achieve constant-factor speedups (e.g., sparse attention methods whose indexers themselves scale quadratically). In this paper, we derive an asymptotically subquadratic sparse attention method based on a hierarchical, tree-structured indexer, which we call Zyphra Hierarchical Attention (ZHA). ZHA constructs a tree of recursively compressed key summaries over the KV cache, which future queries traverse from the top down to retrieve a sparse subset of keys. Crucially, by iteratively growing the tree's depth, the total cost including routing and final attention grows sublinearly in sequence length, resulting in overall subquadratic (but superlinear) scaling for attention. We demonstrate that ZHA can be cheaply applied to existing pretrained models with only a short KL distillation training stage over a few billion tokens, and maintains performance on long-context retrieval and challenging reasoning, mathematical, and agentic coding evaluation tasks. Moreover, we show that our method is highly robust to tree geometry including very deep trees of up to 6 levels. Finally, we develop a detailed performance model and show that our approach scales subquadratically, dramatically outperforms competing methods beyond 1 million context, and ultimately has the potential to extend effective context windows to the hundreds of millions or billions of tokens.

</details>


### [45] [DecisionRank: On-Policy Distillation for Decision-Model Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42128)
*Yanran Wu,Dian Yu,Chongyang Bai,Jieyi Zhang,Rui Liu,Tianyi Chen,Shike Mei,Hang Cui,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Luke Simon,Chunwei Liu,Xi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向检索排序的决策模型训练方法，通过在线策略蒸馏把生成式大模型的排序偏好转化为对文档分数的直接监督，从而兼顾排序效果与推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型可直接预测选择或分数，避免生成式模型逐词解码带来的延迟。但生成式教师以文档编号序列表达排序偏好，决策学生则依据首个词元位置的分数确定排序，两者输出形式不匹配，使教师排序知识难以有效迁移。

Method: 提出决策排序方法，采用基于项目空间的在线策略蒸馏。学生先按自身文档分数排序，生成一份排序列表作为自身轨迹；生成式教师再以该列表为输入，提供条件反馈，直接监督学生分数。同时结合离线排序监督，训练学生模型。

Result: 该方法在通用及推理密集型检索基准上取得领先表现，涵盖特雷克深度学习检索、异质信息检索和推理密集型检索等基准；在四个基准上，项目空间在线策略蒸馏均优于其他训练目标。仅有17亿参数的模型在特雷克深度学习检索基准的2019至2023年测试集上超过了参数量为270亿的生成式教师。参数量为80亿时，该模型也超过生成式列表排序模型，并在特雷克深度学习检索和推理密集型检索基准上分别快24.5倍和16.7倍。

Conclusion: 将学生自身排序结果作为教师反馈的条件输入，能够有效弥合生成式排序知识与决策模型分数之间的形式差异。实验表明，该训练方案可提升检索排序性能，并在大幅降低推理延迟的同时超越规模更大的生成式模型。

Abstract: Decision models target structured tasks by directly predicting choices or scores, avoiding autoregressive decoding latency. However, transferring a generative LLM's ranking knowledge to a decision model remains challenging: the teacher expresses preferences through a ranked list of document ID sequences, while the student determines the ranking using scores from the first-token position. To bridge this gap, we introduce DecisionRank, a decision model trained through item-space on-policy distillation (OPD). Item-space OPD constructs the student's rollout by sorting its document scores into a ranked sequence. A generative teacher takes the resulting ranking list as inputs and provides conditional feedback to directly supervise those scores. Combined with offline ranking supervision, DecisionRank achieves state-of-the-art performance on both general and reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmarks such as TREC DL, BEIR, and BRIGHT. Across four benchmarks, item-space OPD consistently outperforms alternative training objectives. Even at 1.7B parameters, DecisionRank surpasses its 27B generative teacher on TREC DL19--23. At 8B, it outperforms its generative listwise counterpart while running $24.5\times$ faster on TREC DL and $16.7\times$ faster on BRIGHT.

</details>


### [46] [Flide: Accelerating Decision Inference with Exact State Forking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42148)
*Bobo Li,Jiajia Song,Hao Fei,Wynne Hsu,Mong Li Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向大语言模型智能体决策请求的高效、精确推理引擎，通过将分支请求映射到扁平令牌流中执行，显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型适合智能体快速、轻量地进行分类式推理，但现有面向文本生成的推理引擎难以高效处理分支请求：重复计算共享前缀会造成较大开销，而某些优化又会改变输出概率。因此，需要兼顾执行效率与计算精确性的专用推理方案。

Method: 该方法将请求的逻辑树结构与物理执行方式分离，把多个分支组织在单一扁平令牌流中。其核心机制利用驻留在图形处理器上的索引追踪请求依赖，并同时支持标准注意力层和循环式门控增量网络层；在精确算术下，各分支的计算结果与单独执行前向传播一致。系统还将分支拓扑编码为图形处理器元数据，以少量预录制执行图适配不同请求组合，并支持从先前检查点恢复不断扩展的智能体历史。

Result: 在公开的混合模型与决策模型评测中，该方法的查询速度最高可达两种现有推理引擎的9.5倍；同时，其输出概率与浮点32位参考结果的总变差距离最小，体现出较高的数值一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作为智能体决策请求提供了一种兼具高吞吐、低延迟和数学精确性的推理架构。通过统一分支请求的高效物理执行与结果一致性，它为大规模、延迟敏感的智能体决策应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Decision models such as Jev have emerged as a highly promising paradigm for LLM agent systems, providing lightweight and reliable categorical reasoning for rapid System One execution. However, existing generation-oriented inference engines fail to support these workloads effectively, suffering from severe prefix recomputation overheads or distorted output probabilities. To address this challenge, we propose Flide, an efficient and exact inference engine that decouples the logical tree structure of a decision request from its physical execution within a single flattened token stream. Through ForkAttention, Flide navigates request dependencies using GPU-resident indices across both standard attention and recurrent Gated DeltaNet layers, guaranteeing in exact arithmetic that every query branch evaluates identically to an isolated forward pass. By expressing branch topologies directly in GPU-resident metadata, Flide accommodates diverse request mixtures with a compact set of pre-recorded execution graphs while enabling expanding agent histories to resume from checkpoints saved in earlier passes. Extensive evaluations on public hybrid and decision models demonstrate that Flide answers queries up to 9.5 times faster than vLLM and SGLang while staying closest to a float32 reference in total variation distance. By unifying physical execution throughput with mathematical exactness, Flide establishes a principled foundation for scalable, latency-critical decision intelligence in agent systems.

</details>

### [47] [Explain, Then Forget: Recommendation Unlearning under Asymmetric Access Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40658)
*Haichao Zhang,Chong Zhang,Wen Wang,Shi Qiu,Peiyu Hu,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向模型即服务环境的推荐遗忘方法，通过估计待遗忘交互对推荐结果的因果影响，并调整受影响物品的排序分数，在不修改模型参数的情况下消除其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统需要移除噪声点击或过时偏好等历史交互的影响。但在模型即服务架构中，模型由服务提供方远程托管，服务运营方只能通过接口调用，无法访问或更新模型参数，因此依赖重训练或微调的现有遗忘方法难以应用。

Method: 提出因果解释驱动的推荐遗忘框架，分两步实施输出层干预：首先估计待遗忘交互对当前推荐结果的因果贡献；随后依据该因果信号，调整受影响物品的最终排序分数，从而削弱或移除该交互的影响，同时保持模型参数不变。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法的遗忘效果可与完整重训练相当，同时保持推荐效用，并显著降低计算成本。由于无需反复更新模型参数，也有助于避免连续遗忘请求造成的误差累积。

Conclusion: 该方法将推荐遗忘从模型内部参数更新转为输出分数干预，适用于服务运营方无法访问模型参数的场景。实验结果显示，它兼顾遗忘效果、推荐效用与计算效率，并支持持续处理遗忘请求。

Abstract: Recommendation unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific historical interactions that should no longer affect future recommendations, such as noisy clicks or outdated user preferences. However, performing recommendation unlearning becomes much harder in modern Machine-Learning-as-a-Service (MLaaS) architectures, where the recommendation model is remotely hosted by a model provider (MP) and accessed by service operators (SO) only through APIs. In this setting, a service operator serves end users but cannot access or modify internal model parameters. As a result, existing unlearning methods are difficult to apply because they require access to model internals for retraining, fine-tuning, or other parameter updates. To address these challenges, we propose Causal Explanation-based Unlearning (CEU), a novel unlearning framework tailored for MLaaS environments. Instead of modifying internal model weights, CEU operates through a two-step output-level score intervention. First, it estimates how the forgotten interaction causally contributes to the current recommendation. Second, guided by this causal signal, the system removes the influence of the forgotten interaction by adjusting the final ranking scores of affected items. Because CEU leaves model parameters untouched, it naturally supports continuous unlearning requests while avoiding the error accumulation that can result from repeated parameter updates. Extensive experiments on three public datasets show that CEU achieves unlearning effectiveness comparable to full retraining while preserving recommendation utility and significantly reducing computational cost. The source code is available at: https://github.com/zhang-haichao/Explain-and-Forget.

</details>


### [48] [Retrieve-then-Disambiguate: Scalable Reviewer Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40768)
*Vicente Amado Olivo,Joe Petrecca,Wolfgang E. Kerzendorf*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种“先检索、后消歧”的开放算法，从相似论文中发现并排序与投稿主题相关的研究者；在天文学和物理学论文上的测试显示，该方法能找回相当比例的已知相关研究者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研投稿数量不断增长，使同行评审流程承受压力；与此同时，许多具备相关专长的研究者尚未被发现，因而未能纳入潜在审稿人范围。论文将相关研究者发现建模为检索与排序问题。

Method: 针对一篇待评审投稿，先从科学文献中检索主题相似的论文，再对论文作者姓名进行消歧，将可能含糊的姓名对应到唯一研究者身份，并据此识别和排序领域相关的研究者。研究使用开放源代码实现，并在五百篇天文学和物理学论文上进行评估。

Result: 评估以与开放研究者身份标识关联的第一作者、合作者及被引研究者作为代理标签。当检索相似度最高的五百篇论文时，算法分别找回约百分之六十八的第一作者、百分之六十的合作者和百分之四十五的被引研究者。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于相似论文检索并结合作者消歧，可以有效发现与投稿主题相关的潜在研究者，为拓展审稿人候选范围提供可行方案。不过，评估依赖已知作者及被引关系作为代理标签，且当前验证仅覆盖天文学和物理学，效果能否推广至其他学科仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: The growing number of scientific submissions is straining the peer review process, while a large pool of relevant researchers remains unidentified and is therefore not considered as potential reviewers. We formulate the discovery of these researchers as a retrieval problem: given a submission, we retrieve and rank researchers based on domain similarity. We present retrieve-then-disambiguate, an open-source algorithm that identifies domain relevant researchers for a query submission by retrieving similar publications from the scientific literature and resolving ambiguous author names into unique researcher identities. We evaluate the algorithm on 500 astronomy and physics publications. We use ORCID-linked first authors, coauthors, and cited researchers as proxy labels to evaluate the recovery of researchers with known connections to each query publication. When retrieving the top 500 most similar papers, the algorithm recovers roughly 68\% of first authors, 60\% of coauthors, and 45\% of cited researchers. Our code is available at https://github.com/deepthought-initiative/Retrieve-then-Disambiguate.

</details>


### [49] [ProdCrawl: A Replayable Test Collection for Focused Crawling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40855)
*Manuel Rey-Escobar,David Otero,Javier Parapar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出可重复的聚焦式爬虫评测集，帮助研究者在相同网页和标注上公平比较爬虫策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚焦式爬虫需要在下载预算有限时优先选择相关网页，但传统评测依赖实时网站和相关性收获率，网站会变化，且缺少可靠的相关性真值，因此实验难以复现、不同爬虫也难以公平比较。

Method: 作者构建了包含六百二十一个企业与组织网站静态镜像的测试集，共约一百一十万页，并由三个大语言模型投票，将网页标注为产品页或非产品页。研究者可在同一批网页和标签上运行爬虫。论文比较了广度优先、深度优先、文本分类，以及依据已标注页面所对应的相似链接文本为链接评分的方法；后者进一步使用神经嵌入衡量语义相似度。

Result: 实验显示，文本分类和关键词匹配相较于广度优先爬取没有显著优势；利用神经嵌入比较链接文本语义的方法则优于所有简单策略。由于各次评测使用相同网页与标签，结果可以精确复现。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了可重复的聚焦式爬虫评测基础设施，缓解实时网站变化和缺少统一真值带来的评估问题。公开发布的测试集、代码和模型有助于实验复现与公平比较；结果也表明，语义层面的链接文本匹配比简单分类或关键词策略更有效。

Abstract: A focused crawler searches for web pages on a specific topic under a constrained download budget. Since it can only fetch a limited number of pages, its success depends on choosing which links to follow. Focused crawlers are usually evaluated on live websites by harvest rate, the fraction of downloaded pages that are relevant. This has two problems: on the one hand, websites change over time, so a crawl cannot be repeated under the same conditions; on the other hand, there is no ground truth for which pages are relevant. As a result, comparisons between crawlers are unreliable. We present ProdCrawl, a replayable test collection for focused crawling. It comprises static mirrors of 621 company and organization websites, about 1.1 million pages, with each page labeled as a product page or non-product page based on a majority vote among three large language models. Any crawler can be run on exactly the same sites and scored against the same labels. We use ProdCrawl to compare simple baselines (breadth-first, depth-first and text classifiers) with an approach that scores each link by searching for similar link texts whose target pages have already been labeled. Our results show that text classifiers and keyword matching do not significantly outperform breadth-first crawling, whereas neural embeddings, which compare link texts by semantic meaning, outperform all simple strategies. Because every run evaluates over identical pages and labels, these results are exactly reproducible. The collection, codebase, and models are publicly released to support experimental replication and fair crawler comparison.

</details>


### [50] [Context as Infrastructure: Knowledge Compilation for Retrieval in Agentic Systems with Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41172)
*Zhichang Yu,Baining Cao,Sbaaoui Idriss,Qi Wang,Gangqiang Xu,Ling Qin,Xiaoling Jin,An Liu,Shaohao Cai,Xiaoyang Bao,Shengle Chen,Changchang Ding,Pengfei Zhu,Jingxiang Xu,Yifeng Mao,Lu Liu,Hai Jin,Yingfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出将知识编译作为智能体式检索增强生成的供给层：通过把文档整理为结构化、可治理的知识，并用严格流程控制检索，减少无效上下文与成本；其主要量化收益是平均减少百分之三十八的查询词元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体式检索能够通过反复搜索、阅读和改写查询提升回答准确率，但缺乏约束的循环会重复检索、重复阅读，并把未使用的段落塞满上下文窗口。作者认为，问题不再主要是检索准确率，而是如何向模型提供尽可能精简且足够的知识。因此，需要在检索之前增加知识编译层，将文档转化为检索流程更易利用的结构化材料。

Method: 作者介绍一种可配置的知识摄取流程，利用大语言模型为文档补充单个文本片段无法独立承载的上下文，可生成导航层级、主张—证据叶节点、实体图谱、时间线、思维导图、本体、维基和记忆等知识制品。论文重点评估由主张—证据对构成叶节点的导航树，并设置决策门来判断何时停止或调整检索。整体流程将检索循环组织为五个有纪律的环节，使进入模型的上下文尽量精简且充分。

Result: 仅使用基于关键词匹配和文本搜索的工具、配合一个大语言模型的检索流程，在实验中于浏览型基准取得百分之八十五的正确率，在多跳推理基准取得百分之九十一，且无需预构建编译索引或向量索引。结构化制品在多跳基准上未显示可检测的准确率提升，而类似页面索引的提纲在规模扩大时反而降低表现。主张—证据导航树在证据充分时可减少百分之九十一的查询词元，在证据不足时增加百分之一点九，平均减少百分之三十八；盈亏平衡所需的证据充分率约为百分之二，实测约为百分之四十三。决策门将延迟从约一百四十秒降至约三十秒，但以更多检索轮次换取词元节省的替代策略仍会带来延迟成本。

Conclusion: 知识编译并非单纯提升检索准确率的更强检索器，而是为智能体提供经过整理、治理并适配任务的知识供给层。不同结构化制品的价值还体现在治理、系统集成、人员上手、时间信息组织和长期连续性等实际部署需求中，这些收益未必能由准确率基准体现。论文将知识存储与智能体检索流程结合，提出一种面向智能体的数据库思路：交付经过编译和治理的上下文，而非仅返回排序后的文本段落。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation is becoming agentic. A search harness writes queries, reads results, reformulates, and decides when to stop. This largely solves accuracy: a harness with only BM25/grep and MiniMax M3 answers 85% of BrowseComp-Plus and 91% on multi-hop FRAMES in our runs, without compiled or vector indexes. It exposes a context problem: an undisciplined loop repeats queries, rereads documents, and fills the window with unused passages. The missing layer is knowledge compilation: a configurable ingestion pipeline where LLMs enrich documents with context no chunk contains alone--navigation hierarchies with claim-evidence leaves, entity graphs, timelines, mind maps, ontologies, wikis, and memory. Compilation is not a better retriever: under a competent harness, structural artifacts gave no detectable accuracy gain on FRAMES, and a PageIndex-style outline degraded at scale. It is a supply layer. A navigation tree whose leaves are claim-evidence pairs--the mechanism we measure--cuts query tokens by 91% when evidence suffices and adds 1.9% when it does not, a 38% average reduction; this implies a 2% break-even sufficiency rate and a measured rate near 43%. A decision gate cuts latency from 140 s to 30 s, though substitution's token saving is bought with retrieval rounds and carries its own latency cost. Other artifacts--ontology, graph, wiki, timeline, mind map, memory--pay off in deployment (governance, integration, onboarding, temporal orientation, continuity) where accuracy benchmarks cannot see them. We describe RAGFlow's pipeline and harness (open source at https://github.com/infiniflow/ragflow), which turns the loop into five disciplined stations and keeps the context entering the model the smallest sufficient set; together they form an agent-native database: a store that hands the agent compiled, governed context instead of a ranked list of passages.

</details>


### [51] [MILES-Rec: Uncovering Implicit Intents from Mobility Behaviors for Cross-Domain POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41319)
*Yi Zou,Wei Luo,Xuewen Zhang,Zhuang Yan,Pengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种跨领域地点推荐框架，从地图行为中推断用户潜在服务意图，并将其转化为排序后的自然语言查询，以提升地点检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地图应用中的搜索、导航等行为通常服务于出行等实际目标，能够间接反映消费需求，但这些行为提供的证据不完整且含义多样，因此难以准确识别不同用户的个性化服务意图。论文旨在利用异质地图行为弥补这一信息缺口。

Method: 框架先通过渐进式推理示例，综合用户画像与地图历史，依次完成情境理解、意图扩展和查询优先级排序。随后采用与意图对齐的组相对策略优化，根据用户后续交互反馈奖励查询相关性和排序列表的服务覆盖度，同时惩罚冗余及过长回答。其双层信用分配机制结合列表级共享优势与查询级优势，兼顾单条查询质量和查询列表整体的互补覆盖。此外，论文基于大规模地图平台的真实交互日志构建了训练与评测基准，涵盖多类服务及丰富、稀疏两种行为历史。

Result: 实验显示，该方法相较通用大语言模型和专门的推荐基线，取得更高的地点检索效用，并在服务匹配和语义对齐方面表现突出。基于行为反馈的策略优化在四种不同规模的基础模型上均带来稳定提升。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将地图行为转化为经过优先级排序的自然语言服务查询，并利用后续交互进行针对性优化，是从间接行为中推断潜在需求、提升跨领域地点推荐效果的有效路径。

Abstract: Map applications bridge everyday mobility with local services, creating opportunities to recommend places by uncovering users' implicit intents. However, utility-driven map interactions provide only indirect and incomplete evidence of consumption demands, making it challenging to infer personalized service needs from observed behaviors. We propose MILES-Rec, a cross-domain POI recommendation framework that infers latent service intents from heterogeneous map behaviors and expresses them as prioritized natural language queries. It first learns from progressive reasoning demonstrations that synthesize user profiles and map histories for situation understanding, intent expansion, and query prioritization. We then introduce intent-aligned group relative policy optimization, using subsequent user interactions to reward query relevance and ranked service coverage while penalizing redundancy and excessive response length. A two-level credit assignment mechanism integrates shared list-level advantages with query-specific advantages, coordinating individual query quality with the complementary coverage of the complete query list. We further construct MILES-Bench from real user interaction logs on a large-scale mapping platform, supporting training and evaluation across diverse services under rich and sparse behavioral histories. Extensive experiments against general-purpose LLMs and specialized recommendation baselines demonstrate superior POI retrieval utility and strong service and semantic alignment, with consistent gains from behavioral policy optimization across four backbone scales.

</details>


### [52] [Pix2Evidence: A Multimodal RAG System for Text and Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41335)
*Ziniu Liu,Xiaoyang Chen,Dezhi Ye,Jie Liu,Jin Ma,Hao Deng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向视觉丰富文档的检索增强生成系统，将截图中的图像结构与可读取文本联合编码，并通过证据引导训练和候选重排提升问答表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理包含图表、版面和文字的文档时，系统不仅要找出主题相关的内容，还必须定位真正支持答案的证据。仅检索截图可能忽略其中可搜索的文字，而仅依据主题相关性也难以判断哪个截图区域包含答案依据。

Method: 系统将每个截图区域及其可用转录文本融合为单一的图像—文本表示，使版面信息和可读文字共同参与检索。训练时，利用视觉语言模型为问题生成可见证据描述，据此调整检索器；检索后再对候选内容重排序，最后生成答案。

Result: 实验显示，该系统整体优于现有先进方法。在五个常用的维基百科问答基准中，未经重排序的系统在四个基准的主要对比中取得最高答案准确率；在多模态搜索基准上，准确率达到百分之二十六，比像素检索增强生成基线高四点四个百分点。

Conclusion: 将截图结构与文字联合表示，并以答案证据为导向优化检索和重排序，有助于提升视觉丰富文档问答的效果。作者计划公开代码，并在许可与隐私审查允许的范围内分享衍生数据。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over visually rich documents must find both relevant content and the evidence that supports an answer. Screenshot retrieval preserves images and page structure, but written facts within screenshots also need to be searchable. Moreover, topical relevance alone does not identify which retrieved tile contains evidence for the answer. We introduce Pix2Evidence, a multimodal RAG system that encodes each screenshot tile together with its available transcription into a single fused image--text representation, so that layout and readable text are considered jointly during retrieval. To guide evidence selection, a vision-language teacher describes the visible content that supports each training question. We use these descriptions to adapt the retriever, then rerank the retrieved candidates before answer generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Pix2Evidence outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Across five widely-used Wikipedia QA benchmarks, the non-reranked Pix2Evidence leads the main comparison in answer accuracy on four. On MMSearch, it reaches 26.0\%, 4.4 percentage points above PixelRAG. We will release the code and share derived data where licensing and privacy reviews permit.

</details>


### [53] [Defining Page-Centric Visual Document Retrieval and Evaluating Existing Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41360)
*Chongzhi Zhang,Yuqi Zuo,Aixin Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出以页面为基本索引单位的视觉文档检索框架，并系统评估现有基准，指出评测设置与实际应用需求之间仍有明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的文档常以包含丰富视觉信息的分页结构呈现，例如文字处理文档、便携式文档和演示文稿。传统以自由文本为中心的检索方法往往忽略页面结构，或通过任意切分破坏结构，因此需要建立更贴近真实文档形态的检索任务与评测体系。

Method: 论文将页面作为视觉文档检索的基本索引单位，并允许利用文档上下文判断页面相关性。框架区分页面级与文档级两种检索粒度，以及独立项目检索、证据集检索和迭代检索三种范式。作者依据涵盖语料特征、查询构造、相关性标注和排序支持的八项标准，评估了四十二个基准。

Result: 评估发现，现有研究主要集中于页面级独立项目检索，支持文档级、证据集和迭代检索的基准较少。此外，源于真实用户信息需求且独立于目标语料内容的查询，以及分级相关性判断都不常见；各基准在相关性标注的覆盖范围与核验方式上也差异显著。

Conclusion: 当前评测基准尚不足以验证视觉文档检索在真实应用中的多样能力。未来基准设计应使任务要求、相关性定义和评测协议相互一致，并补足不同检索粒度与范式的评测支持。论文还提供了基准选择建议，以帮助研究者依据具体任务选用合适资源。

Abstract: In modern information retrieval (IR), documents are increasingly encountered as structured sequences of visually rich, paginated units, rather than as flat text. Example documents encountered in our daily life include Word files, PDFs, and slide decks. Free text-centric retrieval systems either ignore this structure entirely or destroy it through arbitrary passage chunking. In this paper, we formulate page-centric Visual Document Retrieval (VDR) with pages as the fundamental indexing units and document context available for assessing page relevance. The formulation distinguishes two retrieval granularities, page and document, and three retrieval paradigms: independent-item, evidence-set, and iterative retrieval. We then assess 42 benchmarks using eight criteria covering corpus characteristics, query construction, relevance annotation, and ranking support. Our analysis shows two main findings on current VDR research. First, evaluation remains concentrated on page-level independent-item retrieval, with fewer resources supporting document-level, evidence-set, and iterative settings. Second, queries originating from real user information needs independently of target corpus content and graded relevance judgments are uncommon, while the coverage and verification of relevance judgments vary substantially. Both findings reveal gaps between the capabilities applications require and those existing evaluation protocols can establish. We provide benchmark selection guidance and identify priorities for future benchmark design, emphasizing alignment between task requirements, relevance semantics, and evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [54] [BrowseFlow: Sparse Event Flow Matching for Generative User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41374)
*Yitian Pang,Jiayi Zhang,Zhenjia Ma,Xiaowei Yue,Dagui Chen,Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种条件离散流匹配方法，用于生成更符合真实分布的用户浏览轨迹，并构建配套基准数据集；在三个数据集上的实验中，该方法取得最佳分布表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟面临两项不足：相同推荐内容可能引发不同浏览行为，而单条日志只能记录其中一种结果；此外，点击、点赞等关键反馈事件在以滚动为主的轨迹中较为稀疏，逐步预测方法容易使这些事件在训练中占比过低。

Method: 该方法将一次浏览响应表示为浏览范围及按顺序排列的关键事件，并在曝光约束下直接建模关键事件的数量与位置。模型先以概率方式预测浏览范围，再通过离散流匹配学习关键事件序列的条件分布：随机保留事件构造部分序列的概率路径，并通过匹配转移率学习随机插入事件的过程。评估方面，采用能量评分衡量响应保真度与生成多样性，并以瓦瑟斯坦距离比较关键事件数量分布；同时提出淘宝浏览基准，将真实推荐情境与细粒度浏览轨迹配对。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在分布层面的表现始终最佳。摘要未提供具体数值或逐项基线比较结果。

Conclusion: 论文通过显式建模浏览范围和稀疏关键事件序列，兼顾了浏览行为的随机性与关键反馈的稀疏性，为生成式用户模拟提供了新的建模与评估方案。

Abstract: User simulation aims to reproduce how users interact with content and products across online platforms. Recent generative approaches have enriched user simulation with behavioral history and contextual information. However, realistic browsing simulation still faces two challenges. (i) Stochastic Responses: Even with the same recommendation feed, the same user may produce different browsing responses, yielding distinct behavioral trajectories. A logged trajectory records only one such realization. (ii) Sparse Key Events: Clicks and likes provide important feedback but occur sparsely within browsing trajectories dominated by progression actions such as scrolling. Many stepwise simulators predict these actions in a dense sequence, where key events contribute relatively few training targets. To address these challenges, we propose BrowseFlow, a conditional discrete flow matching method for generative user simulation. For Sparse Key Events, BrowseFlow represents each response by its browsing extent and an ordered sequence of key events, directly modeling their counts and locations under exposure constraints. BrowseFlow learns the conditional distribution of complete browsing responses through probabilistic extent prediction and discrete flow matching over key events. Random event retention defines probability paths over partial event sequences, while transition rate matching learns stochastic insertion dynamics. For Stochastic Responses, we use the energy score, a proper scoring rule, to evaluate response fidelity and generated variability, complemented by the Wasserstein distance between key event count distributions. We further introduce TaobaoBrowse, a benchmark pairing real recommendation contexts with fine-grained browsing trajectories. Experiments across three datasets demonstrate that BrowseFlow consistently achieves the best distributional performance.

</details>


### [55] [ProSearch: Benchmarking Multi-Constraint Protocol Retrieval in Experimental Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41396)
*Wenliang Liang,Zhiyuan Ning,Haolin Chen,Yuanchun Zhou,Wenjuan Cui,Yi Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出科学实验方案多约束检索基准“方案检索”，包含六个领域的1515条查询；实验显示现有检索系统表现有限，联合约束绑定是主要难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能科学研究中的实验设计与方法复现，需要检索同时满足多个相互依赖条件的实验方案。然而，现有基准缺乏对这类能力的系统评估，难以准确衡量科学检索系统处理复杂约束的水平。

Method: 构建“方案检索”基准，强调真实性、复杂性与诊断性：查询依据真实实验记录和专家模板生成，覆盖60个存在依赖关系的实验字段，并为每条查询设置3至5项明确约束，包括数值约束和否定约束。基准构建流程包括基于大语言模型的信息抽取、数据驱动的约束选择、受控查询生成，以及依据证据进行相关性验证。研究还评测了19种代表性检索器，并开展补充分析。

Result: 基准共包含六个实验科学领域的1515条查询。表现最佳的检索器在前十位结果上的平均归一化折损累计增益仅为0.290；加入重排序后提升至0.474。诊断分析表明，显式约束匹配能为排序提供较强信号；人工错误分析则发现，将多个约束联合绑定到同一方案上是关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: “方案检索”为系统评估科学实验方案的多约束检索能力提供了基准测试平台。实验结果显示，当前系统仍难以可靠处理约束间的联合关系，后续研究应重点提升多条件的协同匹配与方案排序能力。

Abstract: In AI for Science scenarios, experimental design and method reproduction require retrieving protocols that satisfy multiple interdependent constraints, yet existing benchmarks lack systematic evaluation of this capability. To address this gap, we introduce ProSearch, a benchmark for multi-constraint protocol retrieval in experimental science. ProSearch has three key features: (1) realism, with queries grounded in real experimental records and expert-defined templates; (2) complexity, with 60 experimental fields and their dependencies; and (3) diagnosticity, with 3-5 explicit constraints per query, including numerical and negation constraints. We construct ProSearch through LLM-based extraction, data-driven constraint selection, controlled query generation, and evidence-based relevance validation. The resulting benchmark contains 1,515 queries across six experimental science domains. We conduct extensive experiments on ProSearch with 19 representative retrievers and complementary analyses. The best-performing retriever achieves an average nDCG@10 of only 0.290, which improves to 0.474 with reranking. Structured diagnostic analysis shows that explicit constraint matching provides a strong ranking signal, while manual error analysis identifies joint constraint binding as a key bottleneck. ProSearch provides a testbed for systematically evaluating scientific retrieval systems.

</details>


### [56] [How BERT-based Cross-Encoders Model Relevance in Information Retrieval?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41425)
*Mathias Vast,Basile Van Cooten,Laure Soulier,Benjamin Piwowarski*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文通过可解释性实验，研究交叉编码器如何整合查询与文档信息、形成相关性判断，并考察其在域外数据上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉编码器在信息检索中效果突出，但计算效率较低，其相关性预测机制，尤其是查询与文档的匹配过程，仍缺乏充分理解。这限制了模型优化，可能导致域外检索效果不佳，也引发了对过度参数化模型效率的担忧。

Method: 研究追踪相关性预测过程中不同阶段及不同输入片段之间的信息流，分析构成信息流并支持相关性判断的信号，重点考察查询与文档之间的匹配。研究还通过一系列可解释性实验，评估三个在规模和预训练策略上有所差异的基于双向编码器的交叉编码器模型，并在域内、域外数据集上进行分析。

Result: 摘要指出，研究系统考察了交叉编码器的相关性建模，并进一步探讨匹配信号所发挥的作用。摘要未提供具体实验数值或各模型、数据集之间的详细比较结果。

Conclusion: 理解交叉编码器的信息处理与匹配机制，有助于解释其相关性预测、支持模型优化，并分析其在域外场景中的行为。本文通过研究信息流和匹配信号，补充并拓展了对交叉编码器相关性建模机制的认识。

Abstract: Cross-encoders are the most effective but also the least efficient models in Information Retrieval. Despite their success, the mechanisms underlying their relevance predictions remain poorly understood, particularly how they process to match queries and documents. This lack of interpretability limits their optimization, can lead to suboptimal retrieval, especially out-of-domain, and raises efficiency concerns for over-parameterized models. To address these challenges, this paper investigates two under-explored aspects of cross-encoder behavior. First, to understand the relevance prediction process, we trace the flow of information at the different stages of the process and between different input segments. We study the nature of the signals that compose this flow and that allow cross-encoders to predict the relevance of a passage to a query. Our work particularly focuses on the matching performed by the cross-encoders between queries and documents. Second, we explore how cross-encoders behave with out-of-domain documents and/or queries. Through a series of interpretability experiments, we evaluate three BERT-based cross-encoder models, varying in size and pre-training strategies, across both in-domain and out-of-domain datasets. Overall, this study both consolidates what is known about how cross-encoders model relevance and extends it by exploring the role of matching signals.

</details>


### [57] [KITE: Retrieval as a Decision Procedure for Long-Term Conversational Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41521)
*Feng Li,Kailun Tan,Zhibo Zhou,Youxiang Wang,Lejun Liu,Huaigu Li,Fei Du,Huilong An*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种不依赖向量相似度的对话记忆检索框架：以受控主题索引组织记忆，并将问题转化为可检查的检索计划，通过固定操作判定哪些证据真正满足问题条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有许多大语言模型智能体的长期记忆系统依赖嵌入向量检索。语义相似度有助于找到相关内容，却不能保证检索到的记忆满足问题所隐含的具体条件，因此需要一种更透明、可验证的证据检索方式。

Method: 框架将多轮、多会话对话整理为结构化知识，并以受控扩展和整合的主题词汇表建立索引。回答问题时，先把问题编译成基于同一索引的检索计划，再通过固定的查找与排序操作执行；同时使用稀疏词汇检索，补回结构化表示可能遗漏的表面细节。

Result: 在两个长程对话记忆基准上，框架分别达到百分之九十三点五和百分之八十六的准确率；每个问题仅使用约一千六百个供阅读器处理的上下文词元。摘要未提供对照系统或统计显著性等细节。

Conclusion: 对话记忆检索可以超越单纯的相似度排序。通过让记忆构建与问题解析共享受治理的索引，检索能够转化为可检查的决策过程，从而更明确地判断哪些证据符合问题要求。

Abstract: Many long-term memory systems for LLM agents are built around embedding-based retrieval. Al- though effective for semantic matching, similarity ranking alone does not establish whether re- trieved memories satisfy the conditions implied by a question. In this paper, we introduce KITE (Knowledge-Indexed Temporal Evidence), a vector-free framework that formulates memory re- trieval as an explicit decision procedure. KITE organizes multi-session conversations as structured knowledge indexed by a governed topic vocabulary, which evolves through controlled expansion and consolidation. At query time, questions are compiled into inspectable retrieval plans over the same knowledge index that guides memory construction. The plans are then executed through fixed lookup and ranking operations, while sparse lexical retrieval recovers surface details omitted from the structured representation. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show that KITE achieves 93.5% and 86.0% accuracy while using only about 1.6k reader-context tokens per ques- tion. These results demonstrate that conversational memory can move beyond similarity ranking by unifying memory construction and query compilation around a governed index, turning retrieval into an inspectable decision procedure for determining which evidence satisfies the question.

</details>


### [58] [Towards Trustworthy Multi-stakeholder Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41674)
*Katarina Marcincinova,Adrian Gavornik,Matus Mesarcik,Michal Kompan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文聚焦多目标、多利益相关方推荐系统的可信性，梳理尚待解决的挑战与关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统需要协调众多利益相关方彼此冲突的需求，而每一方又可能拥有多个目标，令系统所处环境复杂。尽管多目标、多利益相关方推荐已受到研究关注，其可信性问题仍未得到充分重视；随着可信性逐渐成为新的重要标准，有必要系统探讨相关问题。

Method: 本文以议题梳理和研究展望为主，指出构建可信的多利益相关方推荐系统所面临的开放挑战，并提出需要进一步回答的重要问题。摘要未说明具体算法或实验方法。

Result: 本文的主要产出是对可信多利益相关方推荐系统的研究空缺与待解决问题进行强调和归纳。摘要未报告定量实验结果或具体性能提升。

Conclusion: 要使多目标、多利益相关方推荐系统满足可信性要求，未来研究需要进一步关注并解决其相关挑战。本文旨在推动这一研究方向的发展，但摘要未提供具体解决方案。

Abstract: Recommender system (RS) nowadays has to reflect, combine, and solve often contrasting requirements and expectations from dozens of stakeholders. In fact, the problem is even more complicated as each of the stakeholders can simultaneously have various objectives creating a diverse and complicated environment with several stakeholders and their objectives in which a RS operates. To address this problems the Multi-objective and Multi-stakeholder RS have been studied in the literature. However, a limited attention has been paid to trustworthiness aspects of such RS, which slowly becomes a new standard. In this paper, we highlight open challenges and important questions which need to be addressed on the way to trustworthy Multi-stakeholder RS.

</details>


### [59] [Search When Needed, Proceed When Reliable: Metacognitive Reliability Supervision for Efficient Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41738)
*Minhao Sun,Zhijing Wu,Xian-Ling Mao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于中间推理状态可靠性的元认知训练框架，使模型更准确地判断何时搜索，以及搜索是否真正提升了答案可靠性；在七个问答基准上取得了更好的准确率与效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依据最终答案正确性或完整搜索轨迹反馈进行训练，但实际表现同时受到生成随机性、检索质量和后续推理能力影响，因此难以直接判断模型在决策时是否掌握了足够信息，也难以衡量检索证据带来的实际帮助。理想的奖励机制应在确有需要时鼓励搜索，并优先奖励对答案有贡献的搜索。

Method: 框架评估模型局部推理结论的可靠性，并据此区分两类信号：当前状态可靠性分数，用于判断模型能否不经搜索直接作答；搜索行动分数，用于衡量检索证据使可靠性发生的变化。两者共同构成可靠性感知的过程奖励，并用于组相对策略优化训练，从而为搜索与不搜索决策提供更细粒度的监督。

Result: 在七个问答基准上的实验表明，该方法相较于强基线取得了更优的准确率与效率权衡。摘要未提供具体指标或各基准上的分项结果。

Conclusion: 以中间推理状态的可靠性作为搜索决策依据，有望弥补仅依赖最终答案或整段轨迹反馈的不足，使模型既能在信息不足时主动搜索，也能识别并利用真正改善答案可靠性的检索证据。

Abstract: Efficient agentic search requires large language models (LLMs) to determine whether they can reliably proceed with their current knowledge or need additional external evidence. Supervision based on final-answer correctness or trajectory-level feedback evaluates realized trajectory performance. However, observed trajectory performance is jointly shaped by stochastic generation, retrieval quality, and downstream reasoning. Such signals therefore provide only indirect evidence of how reliable the current decision state was and how retrieved evidence changed its reliability. An effective reward should encourage search when external information is needed and favor searches that contribute to the answer. To this end, we propose a metacognitive framework for training LLMs to make search decisions based on the reliability of their intermediate reasoning states. Our framework estimates the reliability of local conclusions to distinguish whether the current information supports proceeding without search from how much retrieved evidence improves that reliability. The current-state reliability score assesses whether the model can proceed without search. The search-action score measures how retrieved evidence changes estimated reliability. Together, these scores define a reliability-aware process reward for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), providing finer-grained supervision for search and no-search behavior. Experiments across seven question-answering benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off than strong agentic-search baselines. Our code and model checkpoints will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [60] [Completeness and Cost of Retrieval Structures for Multi-Tool Requests in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41905)
*Shuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文比较了六类工具检索结构在多工具任务中的表现与成本。核心发现是：完整找齐所有必需工具远比找到单个工具困难；经过少量领域数据训练的集合检索器效果较好且无需调用大语言模型，而层级检索虽有竞争力，却耗时且调用成本高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索方法大多针对只需调用单个工具的查询设计和评估。然而，多工具任务只要漏掉任一必需工具，智能体就可能无法完成请求。整体平均指标还可能掩盖单工具与多工具查询之间的差异，因此需要直接衡量必需工具集合是否完整，并同时考察检索成本。

Method: 研究比较六类检索结构：词法与稠密向量检索、由大语言模型进行查询分解、由大语言模型导航的层级结构、先匹配服务器再匹配工具、依赖关系图，以及经过训练的集合级检索器。实验使用两个多工具测试集：含三百四十五条查询并带专家依赖标签的应用世界数据集，以及涵盖二百五十七种工具的一百零三项模型上下文协议基准任务。主要指标是前若干名结果中包含全部必需工具的查询比例，并记录检索阶段的大语言模型调用次数、令牌用量和延迟；另以七千九百六十一条工具检索查询分析单工具与多工具场景，并用官方应用世界智能体检验下游任务成败。

Result: 在七千九百六十一条查询中，前十名结果的完整率在单目标查询上为百分之五十八至百分之六十七，在目标数不少于四个的查询上则仅为百分之八至百分之十七；不同检索器在单目标与多目标查询上的排名甚至会反转。在两个测试集上，未针对测试集训练且每条查询最多调用一次大语言模型的结构，在前十六名范围内完成的应用世界查询均不超过百分之十。效果较好的两种结构各有代价：使用七十五至九十条领域内查询训练的集合级检索器，在应用世界和基准测试上的完整率分别为百分之三十三和百分之五十八，且无需调用大语言模型；层级检索器分别达到百分之三十和百分之四十，但每条查询中位数需调用大语言模型三十一至三十八次，耗时约五分钟。前者常把漏掉的工具排在截断线附近，后者则可能找到目标接口后，未继续收集其依赖工具。下游验证中，检索结果完整的任务有二十一次全部成功；结果不完整的任务仅三十九次中成功两次。

Conclusion: 多工具检索应以必需工具集合的完整率为核心评估标准，并区分不同工具数量的查询，不能仅依赖单工具基准或总体平均分。少量领域数据训练的集合级检索器在效果与调用成本之间表现出较好的折中；层级检索虽能提高完整率，但需要显著更多时间和模型调用。检索完整性与下游任务成功密切相关，漏检任何关键工具都可能严重损害智能体表现。

Abstract: An agent that must call several tools to fulfill a request fails when the tool retriever omits any one of them, yet tool retrievers are mostly built and evaluated on queries requiring a single tool. We compare representatives of six retrieval structures (flat lexical and dense retrieval, LLM query decomposition, LLM-navigated hierarchies, server-then-tool matching, dependency graphs, and trained set-level retrievers) across two multi-tool testbeds: 345 AppWorld queries with expert dependency labels and 103 MCP-Bench tasks over 257 MCP tools. We measure completeness@k, the share of queries whose entire required tool set appears in the top k, alongside the LLM calls, tokens, and latency that each structure incurs at retrieval time. A motivating analysis on all 7,961 ToolRet queries shows that aggregate completeness mixes two regimes: completeness at 10 is .58 to .67 on single-target queries and .08 to .17 on queries with four or more targets, and the ordering of retrievers on single-target queries reverses on multi-target ones. Across the two testbeds, none of the compared structures that is untrained on the testbed and makes at most one LLM call per query completes more than 10% of AppWorld queries at k = 16. The two structures that do better incur different costs: a set-level retriever trained on 75 to 90 in-domain queries reaches .33 on AppWorld and .58 on MCP-Bench with no LLM calls, while an LLM-navigated hierarchy reaches .30 and .40 with a median of 31 to 38 LLM calls and about five minutes per query. Their failure modes also differ: the trained retriever mostly ranks the missing tools just below the cutoff, whereas the hierarchy finds the target API and stops before collecting its prerequisites. In a downstream check with the official AppWorld agent, tasks with a complete retrieved set succeeded 21 of 21 times, whereas incomplete ones succeeded 2 of 39 times.

</details>


### [61] [Recommendation Feedback Loops Shape Urban Income Segregation and Venue Visitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41911)
*Guangyuan Weng,Lin Chen,Ciro Beneduce,Giovanni Mauro,Massimiliano Luca,Takahiro Yabe,Bruno Lepri,Luca Pappalardo,Esteban Moro*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究通过长期仿真发现，地点推荐系统的反馈循环会加剧收入群体间的接触隔离，并使不少餐饮场所失去访客；偏向较不热门场所的重排序可缓解这两种影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地点推荐系统会影响人们实际前往的地点，而这些访问又会成为系统后续训练的数据，形成持续反馈。仅用历史访问评估离线准确率，无法反映这种反馈对城市长期社会交往和场所存续的影响。城市中的出行距离、场所容量以及访客之间的相互影响，也使相关评估更具挑战。

Method: 研究构建长期仿真框架，使用美国四个都市区的细粒度人员移动数据，并纳入出行距离和场所容量限制。研究聚焦餐饮场所，衡量两项城市影响：不同收入群体的实际接触隔离程度，以及未获模拟居民访问的场所比例。研究还测试了在现有推荐算法之上优先呈现较不热门场所的重排序干预。

Result: 所有算法在所有都市区中，至少导致五分之一的场所不再获得访问；其中超过一半的场所流失源于系统重新训练。准确率越高的算法，带来的收入群体隔离通常越严重；准确率最高的算法使隔离程度比现实高出百分之十五至二十一。偏向较不热门场所的重排序能降低场所流失和群体隔离，而且通常不会显著增加人们被引导至不舒适场所的情况。

Conclusion: 推荐系统的影响评估不应止于离线准确率，还应考察反馈循环造成的长期城市后果，并报告群体隔离与场所流失等指标。对较不热门场所进行适度重排序，是一种可在现有算法基础上缓解上述影响的干预方式。

Abstract: Location-based recommender systems shape where people go in cities and learn from the visits they steer, forming a human-AI feedback loop. This feedback loop shapes whom people share places with across income groups and which venues keep attracting customers. Yet platforms judge these systems by their offline accuracy on past visits, a test in which the suggestions never change where people go and which therefore cannot capture long-term effects. Studying these effects in cities is challenging because, unlike online resources, travel constraints and limited venue capacity shape where people can go, while each recommended visit also changes whom other visitors encounter. To capture such features, we build a long-term simulation framework based on fine-grained human mobility data from four U.S. metropolitan areas, with distance and capacity constraints. Focusing on food venues, we study two urban consequences: how unevenly people's exposure is distributed across income groups (experienced income segregation) and the share of venues receiving no visits from simulated residents (venue disappearance). We find that, under every algorithm and in every metropolitan area, at least a fifth of venues stop receiving visits, and that more than half of this loss arises from retraining. More importantly, more accurate algorithms leave people more segregated, and the most accurate algorithm leaves people 15-21% more segregated than they actually are. We then test a re-ranking intervention that favours less popular venues and runs on top of a platform's existing algorithm. It lowers both venue disappearance and segregation across algorithms, largely without directing people to venues where they may feel less comfortable. Our results suggest that impact assessments of recommender systems should account for the feedback loop and report segregation and venue disappearance beyond accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [62] [Automatic Construction of Strong Simple Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40700)
*Caleb N. Ellington,Jiaqi Wang,Sohan Addagudi,Elijah Cole*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，大语言模型能够为科学领域的深度学习任务自动构建有竞争力的简单基线，有助于规模化检验新方法是否真正带来进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估新深度学习方法的价值，必须与恰当的基线比较。但科学研究中常因遗漏或缺少基线而夸大方法进步。随着论文数量增长，人工同行评审难以全面发现此类问题，因此需要可扩展的基线构建方式。

Method: 研究团队从《自然》《科学》《细胞》系列期刊的同行评审论文中整理出36项监督分类与回归任务，并使用大语言模型为每项任务构建简单基线。研究还比较了不同科学领域、发表期刊、测试阶段计算量以及可获取的论文背景信息对基线表现的影响。

Result: 自动构建的基线表现较强：相对于朴素预测器与深度学习模型之间的性能差距，基线的差距恢复比例中位数为95.5%，均值为85.7%。在12项研究中，简单基线的中位表现达到或超过了深度学习模型。

Conclusion: 大语言模型自动构建基线具有实用性和可扩展性，可作为物理科学与生命科学研究中检验深度学习贡献的辅助工具，帮助识别因基线不足而造成的进步高估。但该研究展示的是一种有效的检查手段，并不意味着自动基线能够取代专家评审。

Abstract: To rigorously establish the value of a novel deep learning method, it must be compared against appropriate baselines. As deep learning has become increasingly common in the physical and life sciences, there have been numerous cases in which apparent progress has turned out to be an artifact of omitted or missing baselines. In this work, we investigate whether this problem can be mitigated by using large language models to automate the construction of baselines. We assemble 36 supervised classification and regression tasks from peer-reviewed studies in Nature, Science, and Cell-family journals that investigate novel deep learning methods for scientific applications. We then use a language model to construct simple baselines for each task. These automatically constructed baselines are strong, recovering a median of 95.5% (mean 85.7%) of the gap between a trivial predictor and the score of the deep learning model. The median simple baseline matches or exceeds the performance of the deep learning model in 12 studies. We further examine the variation across scientific domains and publication venues and we characterize the effects of test-time compute and access to different levels of study context. As human peer review has struggled to keep up with the rapidly increasing volume of research articles in recent years, our results suggest that automated baseline construction is a practical and scalable check on deep learning contributions in the physical and life sciences.

</details>


### [63] [Bayesian Distribution System State Estimation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40728)
*Nan Lin,Peter Palensky,Pedro Vergara Barrios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向配电网状态估计的后验采样方法：仅学习变电站注入的概率分布，在采样时结合潮流方程、网络拓扑和实时测量进行修正，从而生成满足物理约束并能反映不确定性的状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中压配电馈线的实时测量稀少，配电网状态估计因而依赖二次变电站注入的伪测量及其先验分布。加权最小二乘法虽能保持潮流方程精确，却通常将先验简化为均值和方差；监督式深度学习方法则容易同时近似潮流关系，并依赖特定测量配置。论文旨在保留贝叶斯估计结构，更准确地描述注入先验，同时避免模型被特定测量集或网络配置限制。

Method: 提出后验采样状态估计方法。利用历史数据和流匹配训练神经网络，以学习变电站注入的概率分布；神经网络不负责近似潮流方程。生成估计样本时，再将潮流方程、网络拓扑和测量信息通过具备噪声感知能力的卡尔曼式修正纳入后验采样。由此，模型可复用于不同测量集、噪声水平和拓扑，并使每个后验样本对应物理一致的运行状态。

Result: 在三十三节点馈线上，该方法在全部十二种测试设置中的概率注入误差指标均优于全协方差高斯先验，改善幅度为百分之六点七至七点五。其百分之九十置信区间对真实注入的覆盖率几乎达到精确后验样本的水平，而监督式估计器仅为百分之三十七至七十八。在二百四十节点、三相不平衡的艾奥瓦馈线上，该方法在注入、电压和支路电流误差方面均优于其他估计器。快速版本每个快照耗时零点二至零点六秒，并且无需重新训练，在七种重构拓扑上优于高斯先验及重新训练的监督式估计器。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将学习任务限定为未知注入先验，并在采样阶段显式施加物理方程和测量约束，有助于兼顾估计精度、不确定性可靠性、物理一致性及跨拓扑泛化。该方法尤其适用于实时测量有限、网络结构可能变化的配电网状态估计场景。

Abstract: Medium-voltage feeders have few real-time measurements, so distribution system state estimation (DSSE) rests on pseudo-measurements of the secondary substations, a prior in Bayesian terms. Weighted least squares keeps the power flow exact but reduces this prior to a mean and variance, while supervised deep learning estimators also learn the power flow and are tied to one measurement set. We propose PoSE (posterior sampling for state estimation), which keeps the Bayesian structure of DSSE and learns only what the distribution system operator does not know, namely the distribution of substation injections, modelled by a neural network trained with flow matching on historical data. The power flow equations, grid topology and measurements enter only at sampling, through a noise-aware Kalman-type correction. The neural network therefore never approximates the power flow, but every posterior sample is a physically consistent operating point, and one trained prior serves any measurement set, noise level and topology. On the IEEE 33-bus feeder, PoSE lowers the probabilistic injection error (CRPS) by 6.7-7.5% against a full-covariance Gaussian prior in all 12 tested settings. It provides reliable uncertainty: the 90% intervals contain the true injection almost exactly as often as exact posterior samples would, whereas those of supervised estimators contain it in only 37-78% of cases. On the 240-bus unbalanced three-phase Iowa feeder, it gives the lowest injection, voltage and branch-current errors of all estimators. A fast variant matches this accuracy at 0.2-0.6s per snapshot and, without retraining, beats Gaussian priors and retrained supervised estimators on seven reconfigured topologies.

</details>


### [64] [Don't Just Impute, Detect: Task-Aware Diffusion Pipelines for Edge Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40732)
*Jeongsu Park,Taehyeon Yun,Geonwoo Kim,Yuji Lim,Moohong Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向下游异常检测任务的扩散修复方法，减少多传感器数据缺失插补造成的异常信号弱化与误报；在部分数据集上显著提升检测效果，并可在单核中央处理器上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业物联网边缘数据常因网络中断或硬件故障出现连续片段缺失。传统“先插补、后检测”方案通常只以均方误差训练插补器，倾向于用最可能的正常模式填补缺失区域，可能抹平异常证据；同时，正常数据中的边界伪影也会引发误报。这种插补偏差表明，插补目标应考虑下游异常检测任务，而不应只追求重建误差最小。

Method: 作者提出任务感知扩散修复器，采用条件扩散模型修复缺失数据，并依据一个冻结的下游检测器所形成的损失目标进行训练。由于无法实际对完整扩散采样轨迹反向传播，方法将任务相关损失施加于噪声预测过程直接得到的单步干净窗口估计上，因此训练成本与普通扩散训练相当。论文还通过消融实验单独评估异常增强目标的作用。

Result: 在严格的逐点对点评估中，经过五个随机种子实验及配对显著性检验，该方法在标准机器数据集上相较未修复的检测器使调和平均指标提高0.338，显著性检验结果为0.0015；消融结果显示，仅异常增强目标就带来0.463的提升。作者发现，收益与可利用的连续且相互冗余的通道数量，以及缺失区间所移除的异常证据有关；在以独热编码命令通道为主的遥测数据集和一个电力监测数据集上则没有收益。采用十步采样时，单个窗口在单核中央处理器上的处理时间为41.4毫秒，模型大小为4.03兆字节，可支持约24路并发的每秒一次数据流。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将下游检测目标纳入缺失数据修复过程，能够缓解仅以重建误差为导向的插补偏差，并在具有连续冗余传感器信号、且缺失会遮蔽异常证据的场景中显著改善异常检测。该方法计算开销较低，但效果依赖数据特性，并非对所有遥测数据集都有效。

Abstract: Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) edge pipelines routinely lose contiguous blocks of multi-sensor data to network dropouts and hardware faults. The standard remedy is a disjointed "impute-then-detect" pipeline whose imputer is trained only for Mean Squared Error (MSE). Such an imputer fills a missing block with the most probable normal pattern: a partially observed anomaly is smoothed back toward normality, and boundary artifacts on normal data raise false alarms. We call this imputation bias and propose the Task-Aware Diffusion Refiner (TADR), a conditional diffusion refiner trained against the loss landscape of a frozen downstream detector. Because back-propagating through the sampling trajectory is intractable, the task-aware terms act on the one-step clean-window estimate that the noise prediction already yields, so training costs no more than plain diffusion training. Under strict Point-to-Point evaluation over five seeds with paired significance tests, TADR raises F1 by $0.338$ over the unrefined detector on SMD ($p = 0.0015$), and the anomaly enhancement objective alone contributes $0.463$ in ablation. We also characterise when the method helps: the gain tracks the continuous, mutually redundant channels available and the anomaly evidence the outage removes, and it is absent on telemetry datasets dominated by one-hot command channels and on PSM. At ten sampling steps TADR runs in $41.4$ ms per window on a single CPU core, sustaining about $24$ concurrent 1 Hz streams from a $4.03$ MB model. Code and per-seed results are available at https://github.com/int-park422/TADR.

</details>


### [65] [Unsupervised Feature Selection Using Local Intrinsic Dimensionality -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40736)
*Oussama Chelly,Michael E. Houle,Ken-ichi Kawarabayashi,Muhammad Rajabinasab,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需类别标签的无监督特征选择方法：依据特征的局部内在维数衡量其区分能力，并通过去除局部冗余特征生成完整排序。在二十三个高维数据集上的评估显示，该方法整体表现突出，且小邻域通常效果更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据会降低学习算法的效率与效果，而许多应用缺少类别标签，无法依赖监督信息进行降维。因此，论文希望设计一种不需要标签或类别数、同时能识别有用特征并减少冗余的特征选择方法。

Method: 方法将数据矩阵转置，使每个特征成为一个点；再根据各特征与其最近邻特征之间的距离，以最大似然法估计局部内在维数。依据局部内在维数与距离不可区分性之间的关系，局部内在维数较低的特征被视为更具区分能力，并获得较高重要性分数。随后，方法在每个特征邻域中保留一个代表特征，降低局部冗余特征的排名，从而得到可供任意数量特征选择使用的完整排序。时间复杂度为随特征数平方和样本数线性增长。

Result: 论文在二十三个高维基准数据集上，将该方法与经典方法、近期方法及随机基线进行比较，评估覆盖五种下游分类器的分类准确率与曲线下面积，以及聚类准确率和归一化互信息，并考察常规与激进的特征筛选范围。该方法在监督评估中排名第一，在聚类评估中排名第一或并列第二；其统计表现位于顶尖组，且运行速度快于最接近的竞争方法。部分近期方法未能胜过随机基线。敏感性分析表明，较小的邻域规模通常效果最佳。

Conclusion: 基于局部内在维数的特征区分能力评估与冗余抑制，可在不使用标签信息或类别数的情况下实现有效的无监督特征排序。实验结果支持该方法在多种分类和聚类任务上的竞争力；实际应用中可优先尝试较小的邻域规模。

Abstract: High dimensionality degrades both the efficiency and the effectiveness of learning algorithms, and in many applications no class labels are available to guide dimensionality reduction. This paper proposes LIDFS, an unsupervised feature selection method based on Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID). The method builds on the formal equivalence between LID and the indiscriminability of distance measures. The data matrix is transposed so that each feature is treated as a point, and the LID of each feature is estimated by maximum likelihood from the distances to its $k$ nearest neighboring features. Features with lower LID exhibit greater local discriminability and receive higher importance scores. A refinement step then retains one representative per feature neighborhood and demotes locally redundant features. The result is a complete ranking from which any number of features can be selected without rerunning the procedure. LIDFS does not use any information about labels or the number of classes, and runs in $\mathcal{O}(d^2 n)$ time for $n$ instances and $d$ features. LIDFS is compared with classic and state-of-the-art methods and a random baseline on 23 high-dimensional datasets, the classic benchmark collection. The evaluation uses classification accuracy and AUC with five downstream classifiers, as well as clustering accuracy and normalized mutual information, over both full and aggressive selection ranges. LIDFS ranks first in the supervised evaluations and first or tied second in the clustering-based evaluations. It belongs to the top statistical group together with methods that require the number of classes, and it runs faster than its closest competitor. Several recent methods, by contrast, fail to outperform the random baseline. A sensitivity analysis shows that small neighborhood sizes yield the best results.

</details>


### [66] [Certified Behavioral Retention for Continual Adaptation with Held-Out Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40740)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq,Abrar Shahid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种更新后行为保留门控方法：用预留历史样本检验候选策略，只有在最坏锚点上的前向散度不超过预算时，才接受允许范围内最大的插值更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续适应不仅要提升新任务表现，还要控制更新对受保护旧行为造成的改变。现有更新流程可能缺少可审计的保留检查，且有限样本上的实测结果不能自动保证未观测区域的行为稳定。论文旨在提供一种不依赖候选生成方式、能明确设定行为变化预算的更新后认证框架。

Method: 该框架将一部分历史观测保留为锚点，不参与候选模型优化；随后在冻结表示上比较旧策略与候选策略的输出分布，并沿预先声明的更新路径测试不同插值系数，选择满足最坏锚点前向散度预算的最大系数。有限检查无需动作标签或完整轨迹。理论部分区分锚点上的实测保留与锚点之外的条件性保证，并在表示覆盖和利普希茨连续性等假设下，推导受保护支持集上的统一行为差异界，同时分析仿射软最大头的正则性、覆盖与压缩、插值单调性、网格分辨率，以及轨迹级和顺序组合保证。

Result: 在作为非平稳传感器策略代理的受控人体活动识别实验中，无约束适应的平均遗忘率为百分之一；设置行为差异预算为零点零五时，该方法将平均遗忘降至零，但最终平均准确率也从百分之九十三点零四降至百分之八十八点四八。这体现了保留旧行为与适应新任务之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法为能够提供可比较策略分布、且具有明确更新路径的候选生成器提供了可审计的行为保留检查。其保证依赖明确的表示覆盖与正则性假设，不能仅凭锚点检查推及任意未观测区域；实验也不证明其适用于基础模型或机器人。实际部署仍需另行验证当前任务效用。

Abstract: Continual adaptation requires deciding not only how to update a model, but whether to commit an update without exceeding a declared budget for changes to protected behavior. We introduce Certified Anchor-Based Adaptation (CABA), a candidate-agnostic post-update gate that reserves historical observations from candidate optimization, compares old and candidate policies on frozen representations, and selects the largest tested interpolation coefficient whose worst-anchor forward KL divergence remains within budget. The finite check requires neither action labels nor complete trajectories. We develop a theory-led certification framework that separates measured anchor retention from conditional off-anchor guarantees. Under explicit representation-coverage and Lipschitz assumptions, an accepted update satisfies a uniform behavioral-divergence bound on protected support. The analysis gives regularity bounds for affine softmax heads, probabilistic coverage and compression guarantees, interpolation monotonicity conditions, grid-resolution bounds, and trajectory-level and sequential-composition guarantees. In a controlled UCI Human Activity Recognition study used as a non-stationary sensor-policy proxy, CABA reduces mean forgetting from 1.01 percent for unconstrained adaptation to 0.00 percent at budget epsilon = 0.05, while reducing final average accuracy from 93.04 percent to 88.48 percent. The experiment illustrates a retention-plasticity trade-off and does not establish foundation-model or robotic performance. CABA provides an auditable retention check for candidate generators exposing comparable policy distributions and a declared update path, while deployment also requires separate evidence of current-task utility.

</details>


### [67] [AutoHebb: Self-Modifying Neural Networks that Zero-Shot Infer and Continually Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40757)
*Daniel Levine,David van Dijk,Kenneth Kay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出自主赫布网络：通过元学习、赫布可塑性和自主学习控制，使简单且可分析的网络具备零样本推断与持续学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类和动物能够在新情境中进行零样本推断并持续学习，但现有模型往往缺少实现这些能力所需的强归纳偏置和自主学习控制。此外，模型若过于复杂便难以分析，若过于专用则缺乏表达能力。论文旨在构建兼具生物学依据、简洁性与表达力的学习模型。

Method: 提出一种在线学习的前馈网络。网络将外部反馈与内部活动结合，生成全局学习信号，即神经调质；该信号调节基于生物学机制的三因子赫布学习规则，从而更新网络中的可塑性权重。研究者通过元学习搜索固定权重，并考察网络在零样本推断和持续学习任务中的表现及其内部算法。

Result: 网络能够完成零样本推断任务，并在未经过专门训练时表现出零样本推断和持续学习能力；其行为还可被逆向分析为明确算法。在相关任务上，网络优于多种现代学习模型。与相关模型的比较显示，元学习、赫布可塑性和自主学习控制均对表现至关重要。

Conclusion: 自主赫布网络为研究生物学启发的学习与泛化提供了一种兼具简洁性、表达力和可分析性的框架，可用于生成假设、分析学习机制并探索新的学习形式。

Abstract: Humans and animals have a unique capacity to generalize and learn in novel circumstances, including "0-shot" inference (ZI) and continual learning (CL) -- two powerful cognitive abilities. Despite this importance, efforts to model ZI and CL are seriously limited in several ways. First, existing models typically lack components critical to ZI and CL in the brain, namely, strong inductive biases (for ZI) and autonomous control of learning (for CL). Second, models can be less tractable to analysis if model components are overly complex (lacking simplicity), or too task-restricted if model components are overly bespoke (lacking expressivity). Here we introduce autonomous Hebbian networks ("AutoHebb"), a class of online learning feedforward neural networks that realize strong inductive biases and autonomously controlled learning, and are both simple and expressive. Unlike standard models that learn via externally controlled optimization (e.g. gradient descent), AutoHebb networks combine external feedback with internally generated activity to produce a global learning signal ('neuromodulator') fed back to the network. The neuromodulator in turn gates a biologically grounded local learning rule (3-factor Hebbian rule) that updates networks' plastic weights. Using meta-learning to search for fixed (non-plastic) weights, we find that AutoHebb networks (1) succeed on ZI tasks, (2) show emergent (untrained) ZI/CL abilities, (3) admit reverse-engineering into explicit algorithms, and (4) outperform modern learning models on ZI/CL tasks. Lastly, performance comparisons to related models implied an essential role for three model components: meta-learning, Hebbian plasticity, and autonomous control of learning. These findings indicate that AutoHebb may be useful in hypothesis generation, analysis, and discovery of biologically grounded forms of learning and generalization.

</details>


### [68] [Frozen in Time: How a Physics Foundation Model Forgets Under Sim-to-Real Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40857)
*Haaris Mian,Miles Cranmer,Alessandro Favero,Payel Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，科学基础模型在由仿真数据适配至实验数据时会发生灾难性遗忘；在真实数据微调中加入少量仿真数据回放，可显著保留原有能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学基础模型虽能通过微调适应新的偏微分方程数据分布，但微调可能损害模型已有知识。论文聚焦于仿真到现实的适配过程，考察灾难性遗忘是否会妨碍这类模型的实际应用。

Method: 以用于连续介质动力学的沃尔鲁斯十三亿参数模型为对象，进行两阶段适配：先用圆柱绕流仿真数据微调，再用同一系统的实验数据适配。研究比较微调前后的滚动预测表现，并分析数据集依赖性、任务表征相似度等潜在遗忘因素；同时测试在真实数据微调时混入少量仿真数据的回放策略。

Result: 仅经过二十轮仿真数据微调，模型在原预训练数据集上的单步误差最高增至原来的三十五倍；随后适配实验数据又进一步削弱其仿真任务表现。在真实数据微调中回放仅百分之一的仿真数据，便能恢复大部分仿真性能，同时维持对实验动力学的有效适配。

Conclusion: 灾难性遗忘是科学基础模型实现仿真到现实迁移的一项实际障碍。轻量级的数据回放策略能够显著改善旧任务能力保留，并兼顾对新实验数据的适配。

Abstract: Scientific foundation models (SciFMs) have achieved remarkable performance on a wide range of PDEs, including the ability to efficiently adapt to unseen data distributions with additional finetuning. As with all neural architectures, finetuning such models comes with the risk of catastrophic forgetting, whereby adapting to new tasks degrades prior knowledge of the model. We study catastrophic forgetting in Walrus-1.3B, a SciFM for continuum dynamics, over the course of a two-stage sim-to-real adaptation: first finetuning on simulated flow around a cylinder and then adapting to experimental data of the same system. We find that finetuning on the simulations rapidly degrades rollout performance on the original pretraining datasets, with one-step errors increasing up to 35x after just 20 epochs. Subsequent adaptation to real data further erodes performance on the simulated task. We characterize the potential drivers of forgetting, such as dataset dependence and task representation similarity. We then show that replaying as little as 1% simulated data during real-data finetuning recovers most of the lost simulation performance while retaining effective adaptation to the experimental dynamics. Our results identify catastrophic forgetting as a practical obstacle to sim-to-real adaptation of SciFMs and show that lightweight replay strategies significantly improve retention.

</details>


### [69] [FARO: Function-Space Adaptive Return-to-Risk Optimization via Cross-Block Equal-Risk-Contribution Budget Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40858)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该方法依据参数块的实际效果选择更新几何，并用等风险贡献原则重新分配各块的更新预算；实验显示，它能匹配最佳手工方案，且进一步带来小幅但稳定的损失下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型训练受计算资源限制，现有优化方案通常由人工决定不同参数层采用何种更新方式，并让所有参数块共享固定的全局更新预算。这种做法依赖人为先验，难以适应各参数块实际表现出的差异。

Method: 该方法以参数块作为决策单位，依据不同更新几何带来的实际反事实收益，为每个块选择几何方式；随后冻结这些选择，并采用源自投资组合理论的跨块等风险贡献原则，重新分配各块既定的预算。
