Recent approaches frame this task as property-conditioned generation, where diffusion models trained on natural sequences are guided toward high predicted activity through reward-based fine-tuning or sampling-time guidance. We show that single-reward evaluation can obscure reward-hacking behavior. By decomposing evaluation into property, novelty, and plausibility, we identify an empirically underexplored region of the evaluation space in which all three objectives are satisfied, but no existing baseline succeeds. We postulate that this gap arises because prior methods treat guidance primarily as a matter of magnitude. In regulatory DNA, however, sequence grammar is spatially nonuniform: motif positions are sensitive to perturbation, while background regions allow more exploration. We therefore propose LOCOGEN by reformulating guidance as a problem of locality. LOCOGEN learns an invariant motif mask through sequence-level invariance learning to protect motif positions, while applying OOD damping only to background positions. It operates at sampling time on a frozen diffusion backbone, without retraining the backbone. Across HepG2, K562, and SK-N-SH, LOCOGEN reaches this previously underexplored region of the property-novelty-plausibility space. Code is available at https://github.com/Lotfollahi-lab/LocoGen.

</details>


### [140] [Do LLMs Know When They Are Wrong About Molecules? In-Context Property Prediction with Self-Reported Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41745)
*Matthias Busch,Marius Tacke,Tamadur Albaraghtheh,Sviatlana V. Lamaka,Mikhail L. Zheludkevich,Roland C. Aydin,Christian Feiler,Christian J. Cyron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较了七种前沿大型语言模型与传统基线在分子性质预测中的表现。干净数据集上，提供更多示例通常能提升模型准确率；模型自报的不确定性只能有限地反映预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型已应用于许多领域，但其分子性质预测能力仍缺乏充分研究。研究旨在评估模型能否借助上下文示例预测分子性质、报告可信度，并与使用相同分子训练的传统方法公平比较，同时考察训练数据污染的影响。

Method: 评估七种前沿大型语言模型，使用四个分子性质数据集，并筛查数据是否可能被模型预先记忆；其中三个数据集较为干净，水溶解度数据集则被多数模型记忆，作为对照。研究改变上下文示例数量，要求模型报告预测及不确定性，并与传统基线比较；另评估不同推理投入对准确率的影响。

Result: 在三个干净数据集上，所有大型语言模型都能从上下文示例中获益；使用三十个示例时，表现最佳的模型超过所有基线。增加推理投入对准确率影响不大。自报不确定性与误差的排序关系较弱，斯皮尔曼秩相关系数最高为零点四六，约与基线的不确定性相当。结合预测与不确定性后，脂溶性任务中，少量示例时最佳模型最有把握的一半预测最准确；在β-分泌酶、抗病毒活性任务，以及大量示例下的脂溶性任务中，最佳模型与最佳基线表现相当。对于被记忆的水溶解度数据集，增加示例并未提升模型表现，且模型对能够回忆出目标值的分子报告了更低的不确定性。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型能够通过上下文学习有效预测部分分子性质，在特定设置下可超过传统基线；但优势依赖任务与示例数量，自报不确定性目前只能有限地识别错误。训练数据污染可能造成表面上的记忆能力，因此评估模型时应筛查污染，并谨慎解释其预测与可信度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are used in many domains, but molecular property prediction with them is still little explored. In this work we evaluate the capability of 7 frontier LLMs to predict molecular properties together with a self-reported uncertainty from in-context examples, and compare them with classical baselines trained on the same molecules. We use 4 datasets and screen each for contamination of the training data of the LLMs: three are clean, and the fourth, ESOL, is memorised by most of the models and serves as a comparison. On the clean datasets every LLM improves with the context, at 30 examples the most accurate one beats every baseline. The requested reasoning effort changes accuracy little. The self-reported uncertainty ranks the prediction errors weakly, with Spearman rank correlations of up to 0.46, about the level reached by the uncertainties of the baselines. Combining uncertainty and prediction, on lipophilicity the most certain half of the best LLM is the most accurate at small context sizes; on BACE and antiviral, and on lipophilicity at large context sizes, the best LLMs are on par with the best baseline. On the contaminated ESOL dataset the LLMs show no improvement with the context size, as expected for a memorised dataset, and their stated uncertainty is smaller on the molecules whose value they recall.

</details>


### [141] [Latent-Lens: Visual Perception in Small Language Models Through Latent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41751)
*Mo Shahdloo,Faegheh Sardari,Mathew Salvaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 潜在透镜是一种轻量级编解码器，使冻结的视觉语言模型与冻结的小型语言模型能通过隐藏状态直接传递视觉信息，减少文本沟通造成的细节损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化多智能体系统通常只能通过离散文本交换信息，容易丢失空间和细粒度视觉细节；小型语言模型容量有限，因此尤其依赖输入信号的质量。已有潜在通信方法主要研究语言模型之间的状态传递，视觉语言模型如何向独立预训练的纯文本语言模型传递视觉信息仍未解决。

Method: 提出潜在透镜，由两部分组成：领域编码器将视觉语言模型的隐藏状态映射至小型语言模型的嵌入空间；注意力解码器以低秩适配形式训练小型语言模型关注这些视觉前缀。视觉语言模型和小型语言模型均保持冻结，仅训练约一百六十万个参数，约占两者总参数量的百分之零点二。

Result: 在视觉问答任务中，该方法在合成场景问答和通用问答数据集上分别达到百分之八十一点零和百分之六十五点四；同等低秩适配参数预算下，文本沟通基线分别为百分之五十八点三和百分之六十一点八。摘要还报告，相较冻结文本沟通基线，相对提升为百分之三十点二至百分之六十九点八。在图像描述任务中，该方法较同等基线提升十六点五个百分点，较冻结文本基线提升十八点八个百分点。研究也在架构、词表和隐藏维度不同的模型组合上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 结果表明，少量可训练参数即可弥合冻结视觉语言模型与小型语言模型之间的表示差距，并通过潜在空间通信保留比文本传递更丰富的视觉信息，从而提升视觉问答与图像描述表现。

Abstract: Bridging independent vision-language models (VLMs) and small language models (SLMs) in a modular multi-agent system typically forces them to exchange information as discrete text, which creates a lossy bottleneck for spatial and fine-grained details. This constraint is especially acute for SLMs, whose limited capacity makes them more reliant on the quality of the input signal. Recent latent-communication methods exchange states between language models. How a VLM can pass visual information to a separately pretrained text-only SLM though remains unexplored. We propose \textbf{Latent-Lens}, a lightweight codec that bridges the representation gap between a frozen VLM and a frozen SLM directly in latent space, enabling them to communicate purely through hidden states. The codec consists of a domain encoder that projects hidden states from the VLM into the SLM's embedding space, and an attention decoder formalised as a LoRA that teaches the SLM to attend to these visual prefixes, while training only ${\sim}1.6$M parameters (0.2\% of the combined model size). On visual question answering (CLEVR, GQA), Latent-Lens achieves $81.0\pm7.9$\% and $65.4\pm5.4$\%, compared with $58.3\pm5.7$\% and $61.8\pm1.0$\% for text communication with the same LoRA budget (+30.2--69.8pp relative to frozen text communication), while on image captioning (Flickr8k) it improves over the same baseline by +16.5 (+18.8 frozen). We also evaluate Latent-Lens with TinyLlama-1.1B and InternVL2-1B, which use different architectures, vocabularies, and hidden dimensions.

</details>


### [142] [Implicit Assumptions About Tasks in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41792)
*Kathrin Korte,Christian Metzner,Akshay Bedhotiya,Viet Anh Khoa Tran,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，持续学习中的测量结果取决于数据本身、任务划分方式及学习者所经历的训练过程，研究者应明确每项性质和指标所属的层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习通常将数据集切分为多个任务并依序呈现，但关于数据相似性、变化速度和呈现顺序的讨论，往往没有说明这些性质是数据固有的、由任务划分产生的，还是由学习者的训练经历决定的。层次混淆会使实验结果难以解释和比较。

Method: 作者将持续学习相关性质区分为数据、划分和学习者三个层次，并明确各层次可定义的属性。随后通过受控的教师—学生实验，分别改变同一数据轨迹的划分粒度、呈现顺序及数据相似结构，观察测量结果的变化；同时审查代表性基准，检查其是否报告这些属性，并讨论预训练数据不可见时的描述方式。

Result: 实验显示，即使底层数据轨迹相同，仅改变任务划分粒度或呈现顺序，也会产生不同的测量结果；改变数据本身的相似结构同样会影响结果。对基准的审查则发现，相关性质并非总会被明确说明。对于预训练语料未公开的基础模型，其数据性质无法直接检查，可视为已体现在模型初始状态中，而后续训练过程仍可单独描述。

Conclusion: 持续学习实验的设计与报告应明确区分数据、任务划分和学习者训练过程三个层次，避免将不同层次的性质混为一谈。这样才能更准确地解释指标、比较基准，并在数据不可见时清楚界定可分析的训练部分。

Abstract: Continual learning is the study of how a model accumulates knowledge from data received over time, rather than all at once. In practice, this is typically achieved by dividing a dataset into tasks and presenting them sequentially. Explanations of the resulting measurements take into account factors such as the similarity between segments, the rate at which the data changes and the order of presentation. Each of these factors can be defined in terms of the underlying data, the partition imposed on it, or the learner that encounters it. These levels do not necessarily coincide, and it is usually left implicit which level a reported quantity refers to. We separate these and identify which properties acquire a value at each level. In controlled teacher--student experiments, we demonstrate that partitioning the same underlying trajectory at different levels of granularity, changing the order of presentation, and varying the similarity structure of the underlying data all result in different measured outcomes. We review representative benchmarks to determine which of these properties are stated. Where the underlying data cannot be inspected, as in foundation models built on undisclosed pre-training corpora, its properties are inherited by the model's initial state, and the training applied on top of it can be described.

</details>


### [143] [Accurate and Auditable ECG Classification via Visibility Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41795)
*Artur Xavier,Guilherme Henrique Gomes Evangelista,Aitazaz Khan,Antonio Luiz Pinho Ribeiro,Mohammed J. Zaki,Srinivasan Parthasarathy,Wagner Meira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于稀疏可见图的心电图分类模型，在保持较高分类性能的同时，能够通过对应到具体心电时间点的节点进行临床可检验的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习心电图分类器虽能准确预测，却往往难以说明哪些波形区域支撑了判断。研究旨在提升模型可解释性，使其依据的波形证据能够与临床知识对应并接受检验。

Method: 研究提出一种图神经网络模型，将每个心搏表示为由带时间戳的波形转折点构成的稀疏可见图。由于图节点对应具体心电时间点，研究者可删除临床定义的波形区域，或改变心搏时序，以观察预测如何变化。

Result: 在代码测试集上，该模型的宏平均Ｆ１值为０．８８９，优于包括心电图基础模型在内的五种基线方法。其稀疏图所需存储空间约为由全部采样点构图的１４％。对于传导障碍，超过９０％的正确预测中，临床预期对应区域是最强证据；心动过缓和心动过速判断会随心率变化而变化，即使传导间期保持不变；去除逐搏变化后，房颤判断会消失。

Conclusion: 稀疏可见图将较强的分类性能与可定位、可干预的解释结合起来。实验表明，模型对多类心律与传导异常的判断能够通过波形区域和心搏时序操作进行临床相关性检验。

Abstract: Physicians read electrocardiograms (ECGs) from waveform shape and timing, yet accurate deep-learning classifiers rarely show which waveform regions drive their predictions. We introduce LYNX-ECG, a graph neural network that represents each heartbeat as a sparse visibility graph of timestamped turning points. On CODE-Test, LYNX-ECG reaches a macro F1 score of 0.889, outperforming five baselines including the ECGFounder foundation model, while its graphs need about 14% of the storage of graphs built from every sample. Because each node maps to a specific ECG time, we can delete clinically defined waveform regions and alter beat timing to audit predictions. For conduction disorders, the clinically expected region was the strongest evidence in more than 90% of correct predictions. Bradycardia and tachycardia calls follow heart rate with conduction intervals held fixed, and atrial fibrillation calls vanish when beat-to-beat variation is removed. Sparse visibility graphs thus pair accurate ECG classification with clinically grounded, testable explanations.

</details>


### [144] [On-device Detection of Wearer-induced NLOS on Chest-worn UWB Tags: Machine Learning, Link Indicators, or Both?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41799)
*Markus Gallacher,Vu Anh Minh Le,Matteo Trobinger,Davide Vecchia,Carlo Alberto Boano,Gian Pietro Picco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究胸前佩戴标签时人体遮挡造成的非视距识别，比较多种识别方法，并提出结合机器学习与无线链路指标的混合方法及分类器推荐工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超宽带测距在视距条件下精度较高，但佩戴者身体会遮挡无线信号，导致测距误差。此类人体引发的非视距问题研究不足；而标签端识别还必须兼顾准确率、运行时间与能耗，现有机器学习方法可能难以满足设备限制。无线链路指标计算轻量、速度快，但相关探索较少。

Method: 研究人员面向胸前佩戴标签，评估并比较了二十一种机器学习和无线链路指标分类器，同时提出利用两类方法互补性的混合分类器。分析基于一百五十万条样本，覆盖不同信道、物理层配置、环境、受试者和身体朝向；此外，开发了可按用户约束推荐合适分类器的工具。

Result: 所有分类器均取得较好的识别准确率，但不同方法的相对表现以及误分类造成的测距误差，会随信道和物理层配置显著变化。运行时间和能耗差异也很大：部分方法仅带来有限的准确率提升，却最多消耗其他方法三十九点六倍的能量。

Conclusion: 人体遮挡识别不能只看分类准确率，还应结合信道与设备配置、测距影响、运行时间和能耗综合选择。论文提供的混合方法及推荐工具可辅助在具体约束下挑选分类器，并公开了数据集与工具。

Abstract: Ultra-wideband (UWB) enables high-accuracy distance estimation (ranging), but performance degrades in non-line-of-sight (NLOS) conditions. When UWB tags are worn, the human body inevitably causes a wearer-induced NLOS (WNLOS), yielding significant errors; still, despite its relevance, WNLOS remains understudied. Moreover, WNLOS detection typically must occur on-device at the tag, requiring classification accuracy to be balanced against tight runtime and energy constraints often hard to meet with machine learning (ML) -- the dominating NLOS classification approach. Meanwhile, UWB radio link indicators (LI) offer a faster and lightweight alternative that has rarely been explored. We study existing on-device ML and LI classifiers when applied to WNLOS, and propose novel hybrid ones exploiting their complementarity. We focus on chest-worn tags and analyze 21 classifiers on a dataset of 1.5 million samples across channels, PHY settings, environments, subjects, and orientations. Results show that while all of them achieve good classification accuracy, relative performance, including ranging errors due to misclassification, depends heavily on channel and PHY configuration. Runtime and energy efficiency also vary dramatically; some techniques offer marginal accuracy gains while consuming up to 39.6x more energy. Therefore, we also introduce a recommender tool to identify optimal classifiers under user-defined constraints, publicly released with our dataset.

</details>


### [145] [Towards Out-of-Distribution Uncertainty Quantification for Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials via Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41801)
*Stefan P. Schmid,Tobias Setzer,Maximilian Scheurer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究通过在大规模、多样化材料数据上预训练不确定性估计器，为不同机器学习原子间势提供可迁移的可靠性评估；实验显示，预训练模型可直接用于多类数据，而微调能更稳定地反映预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势要在实际场景中可靠应用，需要判断其预测何时可信、何时可能失效。现有许多事后不确定性估计方法主要在小数据集上训练，适用范围较窄。随着基础原子间势的发展，研究者希望通过大规模、多样化数据预训练不确定性估计器，提升其跨数据集和跨化学空间的适用性。

Method: 作者在大规模材料科学数据集上预训练多个不确定性估计模型，用于评估不同机器学习原子间势的预测不确定性；随后在分布内和分布外的吸附能预测任务上测试其表现，并比较直接迁移使用与进一步微调的效果。

Result: 预训练的不确定性估计器能够在多个数据集上直接提供可用的零样本不确定性估计。进一步微调后，预测不确定性与实际预测误差之间始终呈正相关，即使在难度最高的数据集上也能反映模型可靠性变化。

Conclusion: 该研究为构建适用于机器学习原子间势的基础型不确定性模型提供了初步依据。此类模型有望向用户提供预测前的可靠性与适用范围信息，推动相关势函数在实际材料应用中的部署；不过，摘要所述证据主要来自吸附能预测任务，未来仍需检验其在更广泛任务和化学空间中的泛化能力。

Abstract: Informative uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the practical deployment of machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs), as it provides insight into their reliability across chemical space. Although numerous post-hoc uncertainty estimation methods for MLIPs have recently been proposed, most have been trained in low-data regimes, restricting their applicability to narrow domains of interest. Inspired by the emergence of foundational MLIPs, we propose pre-training uncertainty estimators on large, diverse datasets to enable broader applicability. We pre-train several UQ models on large materials science datasets to quantify the uncertainty of different MLIPs, and evaluate their performance on both in-distribution and out-of-domain (OOD) adsorption-energy predictions. We find that pre-trained uncertainty estimators provide viable zero-shot uncertainty estimates across multiple datasets, while fine-tuning consistently yields positive correlations between predicted uncertainty and prediction error, even on the most challenging datasets. These results lay the groundwork for foundational UQ models for MLIPs, ultimately facilitating their deployment in practical settings by providing users with a priori information about model reliability and applicability.

</details>


### [146] [Backpropagation-free Deep Learning for Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41805)
*Miha Kapš,Blaž Bertalanič,Carolina Fortuna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将三种无需误差反向传播的学习方法应用于单变量时间序列分类，并在一百一十二个数据集上系统评估；它们整体表现低于传统基准，且不同方法在特征学习能力与计算成本上各有取舍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分类模型通常依赖误差反向传播，但该训练方式存在更新锁定和权重传递问题。直接反馈对齐、直接随机目标投影和扩散式无反传方法有望缓解这些限制，不过此前几乎只在二维图像任务上评估，尚不清楚它们对时间序列分类是否有效。

Method: 研究将上述三种学习方法适配到一维卷积网络，并在一百一十二个时间序列数据集上进行系统比较，为每种方法分别优化超参数。研究还分析各数据集与初始模块时间网络之间的性能差距，并考察类内时间变化与这一差距的关联。为检验卷积特征学习能力，研究将展平读出替换为全局平均池化，并将其影响与相同架构下的反向传播结果进行差分中的差分比较。

Result: 三种优化后的方法平均调和平均分为零点七六至零点七九，低于火箭特征变换法的零点八三和初始模块时间网络的零点八四。类内时间变化是与性能差距关联最稳定的数据集属性，偏斯皮尔曼相关系数为零点二八至零点四零。全局平均池化对直接反馈对齐和直接随机目标投影的影响比对反向传播更不利，差分中的差分分别为负零点零二五和负零点零五五；无反传方法则与反向传播相近，结果为正零点零一三。相较同架构的反向传播，直接随机目标投影减少百分之三十三的训练浮点运算量，但只有在序列长度约超过七百步时才训练更快；直接反馈对齐没有降低计算量；无反传方法减少百分之三十八的峰值内存，但训练时间增加百分之四十八。

Conclusion: 无需误差反向传播的学习方法能够用于时间序列分类，但目前整体准确性仍逊于成熟基准，且效果受到类内时间变化影响。池化实验显示，直接反馈对齐和直接随机目标投影可能难以学到有效卷积特征，而无反传方法在这一方面更接近反向传播。它们的实际价值主要体现在特定资源权衡上：直接随机目标投影适用于较长序列的计算量优化，无反传方法则以更长训练时间换取更低峰值内存。

Abstract: Deep neural networks for time series classification (TSC) are trained almost exclusively by error backpropagation, which imposes update locking and the weight-transport problem. Backpropagation-free learning rules such as Direct Feedback Alignment (DFA), Direct Random Target Projection (DRTP), and the diffusion-inspired NoProp relax these constraints but have been evaluated almost exclusively on 2D image benchmarks. We adapt these three learners to univariate TSC with 1D convolutional architectures and present the first systematic study of them across 112 datasets from the UCR time series archive, with one hyperparameter-optimized configuration per method. The optimized learners reach mean F1 scores of 0.76 to 0.79, below ROCKET (0.83) and InceptionTime (0.84). We propose an analysis framework that relates the per-dataset gap to InceptionTime to dataset properties and tests convolutional feature learning by replacing the flatten readout with global average pooling, measured as a difference-in-differences (DD) against a backpropagation control on the identical architecture. Intra-class temporal variability is the dataset property most consistently associated with the gap for all three learners (partial Spearman $ρ$ from $0.28$ to $0.40$). Pooling harms DFA and DRTP more than backpropagation (DD $-0.025$ and $-0.055$), consistent with near-random convolutional features, whereas NoProp tolerates pooling as backpropagation does (DD $+0.013$). Relative to backpropagation on the same architectures, DRTP reduces training floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 33% and trains faster only on series longer than about 700 time steps, DFA yields no computational reduction, and NoProp reduces peak memory by 38% at the cost of 48% longer training.

</details>


### [147] [Exploring Bad Minima in Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41807)
*Mina Remeli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究源任务上的良好泛化是否必然意味着能够迁移到相关目标任务；通过三类针对迁移学习过程的对抗攻击进行检验，发现源任务泛化与迁移能力关系密切，但无法据此否定前者可能是成功迁移的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习虽已广泛成功，但知识迁移成立所需的条件尚不清楚。论文聚焦于一个关键问题：模型在源任务上泛化良好，是否必然能迁移到相关目标任务？

Method: 论文假设源任务上的良好泛化并不必然保证迁移能力，并尝试通过针对迁移学习过程的三类对抗攻击寻找反例，再以实证结果分析两者之间的关系。

Result: 实验显示，源任务上的泛化能力与目标任务上的迁移能力存在较强关联。不过，研究未能排除源任务良好泛化仍可能是成功迁移的充分条件。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了源任务泛化与迁移能力之间的紧密联系，但尚未明确判定良好泛化是否足以保证迁移成功；迁移成立的必要条件仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Transfer learning is a highly successful learning paradigm in deep learning which leverages knowledge from one task and applies it to another. Despite its success, the necessary conditions for knowledge transfer are unclear and require further research. In this dissertation, our aim is to understand transferability a little better by answering the following question: Does good generalization on a source task necessarily imply transferability to a related target task? We hypothesize the answer is negative, and attempt to find counterexamples via adversarial attacks on the transfer learning procedure. Over three types of adversarial attacks, our empirical results show a strong relationship between generalization on the source task and transferability. These results do not rule out generalization on the source task as a sufficient condition for successful transfer.

</details>


### [148] [Continual Reinforcement Learning Is an Integration Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41809)
*Nis Meinert,Jakob Gawlikowski,Edward Grant,Max Cyprys,Nathan Schurr,Neil Boston,Alfredo Ibias,Milind Tambe,Janusz Marecki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向终身持续学习强化学习智能体的十项设计与评估要求，评述现有方法和基准，并提出一个持久世界场景及综合评测基准设想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常只考察持续学习目标中的部分能力，且不同能力往往在彼此独立的实验中评估。因此，单项进展或分散的基准结果，尚不能证明这些能力能在同一个智能体的持续生命周期内并存。论文旨在明确长期设计目标，并推动对智能体整体能力的综合评估。

Method: 作者提出一组具有明确立场的十项设计与评估要求，并据此审视有代表性的现有研究方法与基准。作为补充，论文设计了一个可交互的紧凑型持久世界场景，并概述一种基准方案，用于在同一持续生命周期中综合考察相关行为能力。

Result: 论文指出，现有工作虽在部分要求上取得进展，但据作者所知，尚无已发表的评估证明十项要求已整合于同一个智能体。所评述的基准分别检验目标中的不同方面，无法证明这些属性能够在一个智能体的持续生命周期中同时成立。论文提供了场景，并提出了综合基准的评测设想；摘要未报告该基准的实证结果。

Conclusion: 持续学习智能体的长期目标应是将十项要求整合到单一系统中，而非仅分别优化或验证个别能力。未来评估需要在统一、持续的任务环境中检验多种能力能否共同维持；论文提出的持久世界场景与基准方案为此提供了方向。

Abstract: This paper presents an opinionated set of ten desiderata intended to inform the design and evaluation of reinforcement-learning agents that learn continually throughout a continuing lifetime. We use these desiderata to examine a representative selection of existing approaches. Progress on individual desiderata is valuable, but the longer-term aim should be to integrate all ten within a single agent. To our knowledge, no published evaluation has yet provided evidence for such an integration. Likewise, the representative benchmarks we review probe different parts of this target, but separate evaluations do not establish that the corresponding properties coexist within one agent throughout a continuing lifetime. As a secondary contribution, we therefore present a compact persistent-world scenario as a playable companion to the desiderata and outline a proposed benchmark that examines a broad range of associated behavioral capabilities together within one such lifetime.

</details>


### [149] [DIFFNAS: Predictor-Free Neural Architecture Search via Diffusion Model and Zero-Cost Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41818)
*Yize Sun,Hanzhang Ma,Zixin Wu,Xuechun Li,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需性能预测器的扩散式神经网络架构搜索方法，利用零成本代理指标引导架构生成，在部分基准上实现高精度并显著降低搜索开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络架构搜索通常依赖反复训练候选模型，或使用训练得到的性能预测器，因此计算成本较高。已有扩散式搜索方法也往往需要性能预测器。本文旨在借助无需训练的代理指标，降低扩散式架构搜索的成本。

Method: 提出一种基于离散图扩散模型的架构搜索框架，以无需训练的性能指标——包括衡量特征多样性的指标和基于权重符号的指标——作为条件，引导模型生成候选架构。该方法不使用预训练性能预测器，从而减少搜索过程中的计算开销。

Result: 在神经网络架构搜索基准测试集二百零一上，该方法为图像分类数据集十和一百找到了最优架构，准确率分别为百分之九十四点三七和百分之七十三点五一；与扩散式神经网络架构生成方法相比，搜索成本最高降低十七点五倍。但在移动网络第三版搜索空间中，其准确率低于该对比方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将离散图扩散模型与零成本代理指标结合，可以在无需性能预测器的情况下实现高效架构搜索，并在部分搜索空间中取得有竞争力的结果。不过，其在不同搜索空间中的表现并不一致，仍需进一步提升泛化能力与搜索效果。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has advanced the automated design of deep networks, but remains computationally expensive due to reliance on model training or performance predictors. We introduce DIFFNAS, a predictor-free NAS framework that leverages a discrete graph diffusion model guided by zero-cost performance proxies. Unlike prior diffusion-based NAS methods that require learned performance predictors, DIFFNAS conditions the generative process on training-free metrics such as MeCo and SWAP, enabling efficient architecture search with minimal computational overhead. Experiments on NAS-Bench-201 show that DIFFNAS finds the optimal architectures for CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 (94.37% and 73.51%) without a pretrained predictor, while reducing search cost by up to 17.5x compared to DiffusionNAG. On the MobileNetV3 search space, its accuracy is lower than that of DiffusionNAG. These results highlight the promise of diffusion models combined with zero-cost proxies for scalable and effective architecture search.

</details>


### [150] [ElTab: Elastically Looped Tabular Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41819)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出弹性循环表格变换器，通过在随机选择的早期出口进行监督，使单一模型适应不同推理预算；实验显示其在降低训练与推理成本的同时，保持了有竞争力的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格变换器虽有较强预测能力，但推理开销较大，不易部署于资源受限环境。循环变换器通过共享权重减少参数量，却可能因提前停止而显著损失预测性能。研究旨在兼顾模型性能、计算成本与部署灵活性。

Method: 提出弹性循环表格变换器，在训练时随机选取不同的早期出口进行监督，使同一个模型能够在不同计算深度下输出预测。部署时可依据可用预算选择推理深度，无须为每种预算单独训练模型。

Result: 在三十个开放机器学习分类数据集上，单个弹性循环模型的表现与六个针对不同深度训练的循环模型相当，训练成本降低一半以上。与十二层的表格变换器基线相比，其推理浮点运算量减少百分之二十五，同时保持相当的预测性能。

Conclusion: 弹性深度训练使单一表格模型能够根据部署预算调整计算量，在预测性能、模型存储和推理成本之间实现较好的折中，为资源受限场景提供了更灵活的部署方案。

Abstract: Tabular transformer models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur substantial inference costs, making them difficult to deploy in resource-constrained settings. While looped transformers reduce parameter count through weight sharing, they can lose significant predictive power when stopped early. To address this challenge, we introduce the Elastically Looped Tabular Transformer (ElTab), a single model trained to operate across different computational budgets through supervision at randomly sampled early exits. Across 30 OpenML classification datasets, a single ElTab model matches six depth-specific looped models while cutting training cost by more than half, and matches a 12-layer TabPFN-style baseline with 25% fewer inference FLOPs. These results show that elastic-depth training yields a single model that adapts to the deployment budget at hand, offering a favorable trade-off among predictive performance, storage, and computational cost.

</details>


### [151] [Trainable Vector Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41822)
*Anastasiia Prutianova,Alina Bogdanova,Mikhail Shutov,Dmitrii Poimanov,Anatoly Gorkov,Ilia Zharikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于直通估计的向量量化优化方法，通过分块知识蒸馏提升大语言模型低比特量化后的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量量化能够压缩大语言模型权重、降低内存占用并加快推理，但极低比特率会损害模型性能。由于量化参数离散，性能恢复困难；现有免训练方法通常仅最小化逐层重构误差，难以直接优化更广泛的目标。

Method: 该方法引入直通估计，并将量化模型的优化改写为对可训练潜在权重进行梯度下降，从而支持任意目标函数优化。具体采用分块知识蒸馏，在模型质量与训练效率之间取得平衡，并设计了多项加速训练的实用策略。

Result: 实验表明，该方法兼容多种现代向量量化方案，并在低比特训练后量化中取得领先效果，所需训练预算较小，且实现简洁。例如，对七百亿参数的拉玛第二代模型进行二比特量化后，在常见问答基准上的准确率仅下降约百分之一。

Conclusion: 通过梯度优化和分块知识蒸馏，该方法突破了传统免训练量化主要依赖逐层重构误差的限制，使极低比特量化模型能够更有效地恢复性能，并展现出接近无损压缩的潜力。

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) is a rapidly evolving technique for compressing Large Language Model (LLM) weights, reducing memory usage, and speeding up inference. However, extreme compression deteriorates model performance, which is challenging to recover due to the discrete nature of VQ parameters. Existing methods employ training-free approaches that are limited to layer-wise reconstruction loss minimization. To address this limitation, we propose TVQ, a novel method that leverages straight-through estimation to enable gradient descent-based optimization of vector-quantized LLMs. By reformulating VQ LLM optimization with trainable latent weights, TVQ enables arbitrary objective minimization. We constitute our approach by applying TVQ in a block-wise knowledge distillation paradigm, which balances quality and training efficiency, and propose several practical solutions to accelerate training. In experiments on modern LLMs, we demonstrate that TVQ is compatible with modern VQ schemes. As a result, TVQ achieves state-of-the-art quality in low-bit post-training quantization settings with a modest training budget and straightforward PyTorch implementation. For instance, TVQ achieves near-lossless 2-bit quantization for LLaMA2-70B with only 1% accuracy drop on popular Question-Answering benchmarks.

</details>


### [152] [On k-nearest-neighbor regression in metric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41896)
*László Györfi,Vladimir G. Pestov,Harro Walk,Roi Weiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究度量空间特征上的近邻回归均方一致性，提出基于邻居选择密度的充分条件，并展示仅有可数个有限纳盖塔维数层并不足以保证无界响应下的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统近邻回归的一致性理论常依赖斯通支配条件，但该条件可能偏强，也难以揭示特征空间的几何结构、邻居选择机制与响应变量矩条件之间的关系。本文旨在建立适用于一般度量空间的均方一致性条件，并厘清分类一致性结果能否推广到无界响应的回归问题。

Method: 固定一个距离分布无原子的特征分布，考察独立查询点对应的均匀随机邻居所诱导的概率重新分配，并用该邻居选择密度分别控制近似误差与条件响应方差。进一步提出基于固定总体质量邻域的对应条件，分析响应具有更高阶矩时对斯通支配条件的削弱，并将几何维数条件与邻居选择密度联系起来。

Result: 当近邻数趋于无穷且其与样本量之比趋于零时，若邻居选择密度在二次可积意义下对特征分布具有一致有界性，且响应具有有限四阶矩，即可得到均方一致性。文中还给出固定总体质量邻域形式的条件，并恢复欧氏空间中的密度有界条件。有限全局纳盖塔维数可推出斯通支配条件。另一方面，作者构造了一个具有可数有限纳盖塔维数分层的空间，在该空间中，某个固定的无噪声响应虽具有所有有限阶矩，其近邻回归均方误差仍发散。

Conclusion: 邻居选择密度提供了分析一般度量空间近邻回归均方一致性的统一工具；响应矩条件越强，所需的支配条件可以越弱。但分类问题中可数分层的有限纳盖塔维数所保证的普遍一致性，不能直接推广到无界响应的均方回归。

Abstract: We study mean squared consistency of k-nearest-neighbor regression for metric space valued features. For a fixed feature distribution mu with atomless distance distributions, we describe how neighbor selection redistributes probability through the density of a uniformly chosen neighbor of an independent query. This gives separate conditions for the approximation error and the conditional response variance, and weakens Stone's domination condition when stronger response moments are available. In particular, when k_n tends to infinity and k_n/n tends to zero, a uniform L2(mu) bound on the selection densities suffices under a finite fourth response moment. We give a corresponding condition in terms of neighborhoods of fixed population mass and recover the Euclidean density bound. Finite global Nagata dimension gives Stone's domination. While sigma-finite Nagata dimensionality is known to ensure universal consistency of k-NN classification in complete separable spaces, we show that this is not the case for mean squared consistency with unbounded responses: we construct a space in this class on which one fixed noiseless response with all finite moments has diverging mean squared error.

</details>


### [153] [Binary Sparse Codes: Where are we on Energy Efficient Representations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41933)
*Jose Miguel Ramos,Luis SaCouto,Andreas Wichert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出以相似性保持来衡量二值稀疏编码的信息保真度，并比较四类代表性方法。结果显示，稀疏度与信息保持通常存在权衡；竞争式稀疏编码较好地兼顾了两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏编码有望在降低神经活动的同时保留输入信息，但现有研究对“信息保留”缺乏统一、可量化的定义，因此难以公平比较不同方法。论文旨在建立原则性的评估框架，检验编码是否既足够稀疏，又能保留输入数据之间的关系结构。

Method: 作者提出一组基于相似性保持的指标，通过衡量二值稀疏码对输入空间关系几何的复现程度来评估信息保真度，并在匹配的实验条件下比较四种具有代表性的方法，涵盖理论、反向传播深度学习和局部赫布学习等范式。

Result: 实验呈现出稳定的权衡：信息保留较好的模型往往稀疏度较低；接近最优随机稀疏码均匀性的模型，则往往牺牲信息保留。竞争式稀疏编码是唯一在接近最大稀疏度的同时仍保有有意义信息的架构。

Conclusion: 同时实现高稀疏度与高信息保留仍是开放挑战。论文提供了可复用的量化评估框架，有助于系统比较稀疏编码方法，并衡量未来研究在兼顾效率与表征能力方面的进展。

Abstract: Biological neural systems achieve impressive computational capabilities under tight metabolic constraints, relying on sparse cortical representations that minimise total neural activity. This combination of efficiency and representational power has motivated growing interest in sparse coding as a computational strategy for artificial systems. Artificial models designed to produce binary sparse codes span three broad paradigms, theoretical methods grounded in efficient coding, deep learning methods trained via backpropagation, and biologically plausible methods that learn through local Hebbian rules, each yielding codes whose quality depends not only on achieving low activation density but also on preserving the informational structure of the input. The latter property, however, lacks an agreed-upon quantitative definition, leaving the field without a principled basis for comparing methods. We propose a set of metrics that formalise information preservation as similarity preservation, measuring how faithfully a binary sparse code reproduces the relational geometry of the input space, and apply this framework to compare four representative methods under matched conditions. The results reveal a consistent trade-off in which models that achieve high information preservation do so at the cost of sparsity, while models approaching the uniformity of an optimal random sparse code tend to sacrifice it. Competitive Sparse Codes, a biologically plausible approach, is the only architecture to occupy a region of near-maximal sparsity while retaining meaningful information preservation. These findings frame the simultaneous satisfaction of both factors as an open challenge for the field and provide a reusable evaluation framework for measuring progress toward it.

</details>


### [154] [Observable-Specific Validity Tests for Scientific AI: Controlled Approximations and Intervention Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41939)
*Xiaoshn Nee,Haobo Zhong,Xiaomin Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出针对具体可观测量的有效性认证框架，并通过振子、哈伯德二聚体、神经网络拟合、因果干预和机制区分等案例，检验近似模型的误差、物理迁移与响应预测是否可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学人工智能中的近似模型可能在能量等某一量上表现准确，却无法正确预测响应；即使误差低于某个容许阈值，也未必满足特定误差界所提供的认证。因此，评价模型不能只看单一预测误差，而需明确其适用对象、参照模型、误差标准、容许范围、参数域及所经受的变换或干预。

Method: 作者建立面向具体可观测量的有效性记录，将降阶模型与参照模型、误差度量、容差、参数范围及变换或干预联系起来。随后以多个理论和数值案例检验该框架：为奇异摄动振子推导位移与速度的显式误差证书；利用哈伯德二聚体的精确关系比较能量与响应标准所接受的耦合范围；对重复神经网络拟合施加量纲齐次性约束；通过成对变换检验受保护的尺度关系，并以耦合交叉分析响应迁移所需的额外信息；此外，还构造因果模型与高斯噪声下的机制区分问题，推导固定控制查询量的理论要求，并提供二百五十六项任务的评测接口。

Result: 振子的位移和速度证书给出不同的收敛阶，并提供充分的有效性保证。哈伯德二聚体的精确关系显示，能量标准与响应标准对应不同的可接受耦合范围。对十六次重复拟合施加量纲齐次性后，能量尺度迁移误差由百分之五十二点三降至百分之零点零七六八；同时，联合满足能隙与响应容差的拟合比例中位数由零升至百分之百。成对变换验证了受保护的尺度关系，而耦合交叉揭示了响应迁移所需的额外信息。因果案例表明，模型能够在强烈负向观测关联之下恢复正向干预响应。对于响应首次在第k阶出现差异的两种机制，固定控制查询量的精确需求按x的负二k次方增长，数值实验验证了完整成功率曲线。

Conclusion: 论文强调，科学人工智能的可信度应按可观测量分别认证，而不能由某项预测准确或低于容差直接推断。将误差界、参数域、物理约束、变换与干预纳入可核查的评测，有助于区分近似模型何时有效、何时可迁移，以及能否识别不同机制。

Abstract: An approximation can reproduce an energy while missing its response, or satisfy an error tolerance without being certified by a given bound. We formulate observable-specific validity records for scientific artificial intelligence (AI) that link a reduction to its reference model, error measure, tolerance, parameter domain, and transformations or interventions. Explicit displacement and velocity certificates for a singularly perturbed oscillator recover distinct convergence orders and provide sufficient validity guarantees. Exact Hubbard dimer relations resolve the different coupling domains accepted by energy and response criteria. Across 16 repeated neural fits, imposing dimensional homogeneity reduces energy scale transfer error from 52.3% to 0.0768% and raises the median fraction jointly satisfying gap and response tolerances from zero to 100%. Paired transformations verify the protected scaling relation, while a coupling crossover identifies the additional information needed for response transfer. A causal model recovers a positive intervention response beneath a strong negative observational association. For two mechanisms observed with Gaussian noise whose responses first differ at order $x^k$, we derive an exact fixed control query requirement scaling as $x^{-2k}$ and verify its full success curve numerically. Together with a 256-task evaluation interface, these results provide independently checkable tests of controlled approximation, physical transfer, and mechanism discrimination in scientific AI.

</details>


### [155] [Chronos-2: A foundation model for multivariate time series forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41956)
*Abdul Fatir Ansari,Oleksandr Shchur,Jaris Küken,Andreas Auer,Boran Han,Pedro Mercado,Syama Sundar Rangapuram,Huibin Shen,Lorenzo Stella,Xiyuan Zhang,Mononito Goswami,Shubham Kapoor,Danielle C. Maddix,Pablo Guerron,Florian Saupe,Markus Reichstein,Tony Hu,Junming Yin,Nick Erickson,Prateek Mutalik Desai,Hao Wang,Huzefa Rangwala,George Karypis,Yuyang Wang,Michael Bohlke-Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chronos-2 是面向零样本预测的时间序列基础模型，能够联合建模多变量时间序列及异质协变量，并在多项基准和科学应用中取得较强效果，同时保持较高推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数现有时间序列基础模型将各条序列独立处理，难以刻画序列之间以及序列与外部驱动因素之间的关系；传统预测模型又通常需要针对不同领域的数据集分别开发和训练。论文旨在通过大规模预训练，构建可跨领域使用、具备不确定性量化能力的统一预测模型。

Method: 提出 Chronos-2，用于零样本预测。模型从历史上下文中直接推断多变量时间序列之间的关系，并联合处理异质协变量，在无需针对具体任务进行专门处理的情况下生成概率预测。

Result: 在多种基准测试中，Chronos-2 的平均预测准确性达到领先水平：相较最强基线，技能评分提高 5.2 个百分点，胜出率提高 10 个百分点。其推理效率也较高：在单张中端图形处理器上，预测 100 条时间序列用时不足 1 秒；端到端速度分别比最佳任务专用点预测器和概率预测器快 50 倍与 1000 倍。在传染病预测、宏观经济建模、碳通量预测和径流模拟等应用中，其表现达到或超过相应领域的专用方法。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，单一预训练模型能够学习不同时间系统之间共享的结构，并以零样本方式支持跨领域预测。Chronos-2 将多序列关系建模、异质协变量处理、概率预测与计算效率结合起来，为科学预测提供了一种通用方案。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting with uncertainty quantification is critical in a variety of scientific applications ranging from epidemiology and economics to energy systems and earth sciences. Yet most forecasting models still need to be developed and trained separately for each domain-specific dataset. Time series foundation models (TSFMs) aim to remove this limitation and enable the application of the same model across domains through large-scale pretraining. However, most existing TSFMs treat each time series independently and therefore cannot model interactions among time series and external drivers. Here, we introduce Chronos-2, a foundation model for zero-shot forecasting that models the interactions between multivariate time series and heterogeneous covariates, which commonly arise in scientific applications. The model infers relationships among time series directly from historical context and generates probabilistic forecasts without task-specific treatment. Across various benchmarks, Chronos-2 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on average by improving skill scores by 5.2 percentage points relative to the strongest baselines and increasing win rates by 10 percentage points. Chronos-2 is also computationally efficient, generating forecasts for 100 time series in under one second on a single mid-range GPU, 50x and 1000x faster end-to-end than the best task-specific point and probabilistic forecasters, respectively. In addition, we demonstrate its effectiveness in diverse scientific settings -- including infectious-disease forecasting, macroeconomic modeling, carbon-flux prediction, and runoff simulation -- where the model matches or surpasses specialized methods developed for each domain. These results show that a single pretrained model can capture structure shared across temporal systems.

</details>


### [156] [Robust Learning of Multi-Index Models under Logconcave Distributions via Weak Partial Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41961)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Daniel M. Kane,Mingchen Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向对数凹分布的布尔概念学习新框架，借助弱部分分类器、递归矩匹配分区及矩差异检测，提升多指标模型等任务中的鲁棒学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有提升方法往往依赖难以实现的分布无关弱学习器，或只能提供较弱的近似保证。此外，提升过程会遇到经条件化后不再保证对数凹的分布，使针对对数凹分布设计的弱学习器难以直接应用。论文旨在克服这些障碍，为多指标模型及相关概念类提供更快、更强的鲁棒学习方法。

Method: 论文提出基于弱部分分类器的提升框架，证明即使标签只在输入空间的一部分有定义，弱学习器仍足以支持强无悔学习。为处理条件分布带来的困难，作者设计递归的矩匹配分区方法：将空间划分为多项式数量的凸区域，使大多数良好区域中原分布与其条件分布的低阶矩近似一致；在这些区域内，表现良好的多指标模型可与低次多项式相关，从而得到弱假设，再将其组合为所需的弱学习器。核心子程序通过对矩张量进行谱分析，并结合对数凹测度等周性结果，寻找能够捕获非平凡矩差异的低维子空间。

Result: 该框架扩展到部分分类和可测试学习，并为对数凹边缘分布下的多类线性分类器、半空间函数等基本概念类带来改进的学习器。论文还给出了比相关既有方法显著更快的鲁棒学习算法；摘要未提供具体复杂度或实验数值。

Conclusion: 研究表明，弱部分分类器可以作为强无悔学习的有效基础；通过递归分区协调条件分布与原分布的低阶矩，并高效定位矩差异，可将这一思路用于对数凹分布下的多指标学习。矩差异检测方法本身也可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: We develop a new algorithmic framework for learning Boolean concept classes under logconcave distributions. Our framework applies to broad families of multi-index models and yields significantly faster robust learners than were previously known in these settings. It extends to partial classification and testable learning. Consequently, we obtain improved learners for several fundamental concept classes, including functions of halfspaces and multiclass linear classifiers, under logconcave marginals.
  Our starting point is a new boosting framework based on weak partial learners. Classical boosting approaches require often-intractable distribution-free weak learners or yield weak approximation guarantees. We show instead that weak learners for partial classification, where labels may be undefined on part of the domain, suffice for strong agnostic learning. The key challenge is that boosting requires a weak learner that succeeds under conditioned distributions that are not guaranteed to be logconcave. To address this, we develop a recursive moment-based partitioning procedure: given a logconcave distribution $X$ and a conditional version $X'$, we partition the space into polynomially many convex regions so that, on most regions, the low-degree moments of $X$ and $X'$ approximately agree. On each good region, a well-behaved multi-index model correlates with a low-degree polynomial, giving a weak hypothesis. Combining these hypotheses yields the required weak learner.
  A key ingredient of our approach is an efficient method for detecting moment discrepancy. Specifically, we show how to find a low-dimensional subspace that captures any non-trivial discrepancy between $X$ and $X'$, if one exists. Our approach combines spectral analysis of moment tensors with recent progress on isoperimetry for logconcave measures. This discrepancy-finding procedure may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [157] [New Lower Bounds for Private PAC Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41980)
*Prathamesh Dharangutte,Amartya Sanyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出独立类别打包参数，用以刻画差分隐私可实现概率近似正确学习的样本复杂度；该参数给出通用下界，并在若干类别上得到紧界或改进界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的弗兰克尔—切尔诺夫维度和利特尔斯通维度无法单独决定差分隐私学习的样本复杂度。论文通过构造这两种维度相同、但私有学习样本需求不同的类别，说明需要新的复杂度刻画。

Method: 提出独立类别打包参数：考察类别族中各类别能够独立打包进目标类别的程度，并以其最大总样本复杂度定义该参数。证明任何满足给定隐私与准确率要求的学习器（包括不受限于目标类别的学习器），至少需要与该参数除以准确率和隐私参数乘积成正比的样本数，即下界为 Ω（参数／（αε））。

Result: 利用该框架，论文为轴对齐矩形、区间并集和线性子空间证明了紧的样本复杂度下界，并改进了半空间与凸多边形的下界。此外，对于乘积类别，以及满足特定打包和覆盖条件、可直接使用目标标签的更一般类别，独立类别打包参数能够在对数因子范围内刻画私有概率近似正确学习的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 独立类别打包参数为差分隐私学习提供了超越既有维度的下界工具，并在若干重要类别上实现紧致或更强的下界。对于满足相应结构条件的类别，它还近似完整刻画样本复杂度。

Abstract: We study realizable PAC learning under approximate differential privacy, and give a framework for proving lower bounds on its sample complexity. We construct classes with identical VC and Littlestone dimensions but different private sample complexities at common privacy and accuracy parameters, showing that these dimensions do not determine the sample complexity, even for improper learners. To address this we introduce the Independent Class Packing (ICP) parameter, the largest total sample complexity of classes in $\mathcal{G}$ that can be packed independently into a class $\mathcal{H}$, and prove that every $(\varepsilon, δ)$-DP, $(α, β)$-PAC learner for $\mathcal{H}$, possibly improper, uses $Ω\left(\mathrm{ICP}(\mathcal{H}, \mathcal{G})/(α\varepsilon)\right)$ examples. Using this framework we prove tight lower bounds for axis-aligned rectangles, union of intervals and linear subspaces and improved lower bounds for halfspaces and convex polygons. Finally we also show that for product classes, and more generally when the same classes pack into $\mathcal{H}$ and cover its domain so that target labels can be used directly, ICP characterizes private PAC sample complexity up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [158] [When Does Jacobian Fine-Tuning Stabilize Neural PDE Emulators?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42013)
*Shreeyam Bangera,Manya Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究区分混沌偏微分方程神经模拟器误差的物理增长与模型自身动力学造成的额外放大，并发现雅可比微调在模型放大误差较强时尤其有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经模拟器通常以单步预测为目标训练，却在部署时反复自回归运行，导致误差逐步累积。对混沌系统而言，误差增长既可能来自系统本身的物理敏感性，也可能来自模型动力学对误差的额外放大；仅靠常规雅可比正则化，难以区分哪些增长可以通过训练改善。

Method: 作者提出一阶误差分解：将模拟器实际误差与一个反事实误差进行比较。反事实误差把相同的单步预测错误输入求解器的雅可比矩阵传播，据此定义放大比率，用来衡量模型自身动力学对误差的额外放大。研究覆盖四种偏微分方程、五种网络架构、两种模型规模，共二十一个配置，并在一个李雅普诺夫时间尺度上评估误差。此外，作者比较了沿模型自身误差方向匹配求解器雅可比的微调方法与随机方向匹配，并评估了输入噪声注入。

Result: 雅可比微调在放大比率不超过一点二五的三十四个随机种子中，仅有一个使误差降低超过百分之十；在放大比率至少为四的二十个种子中，则全部有效。后一组的误差几何平均降低幅度，小模型约为八倍，三万三千参数模型约为五十五倍。收益大致等于微调造成的单步精度代价除以放大比率。沿误差方向匹配的方法在三十五个种子中的二十八个优于同等代价的随机方向匹配；输入噪声注入与前者表现相近。

Conclusion: 雅可比微调并非普遍有效：其价值取决于模型动力学是否显著放大误差。放大比率可用于识别适合此类干预的情形；当额外放大较强时，针对模型误差方向进行微调能够大幅改善长时间滚动预测，而放大较弱时，微调带来的单步精度损失可能抵消收益。

Abstract: Neural emulators of chaotic PDEs are trained on one-step prediction but deployed autoregressively, where their errors compound. Jacobian-based regularisers aim to curb this growth, but in a chaotic system part of the growth is physical and cannot be trained away. We separate the two sources of this error with a first-order decomposition. We compare the emulator's error with a counterfactual that propagates the same one-step mistakes through the solver's Jacobian, giving an amplification ratio: the factor by which the model's own dynamics amplify its errors. We study 21 configurations across four PDEs, five architectures and two model sizes (8-10k and 33k parameters), with errors measured at one Lyapunov time. Jacobian fine-tuning reduced the error by more than 10% for 1 of 34 seeds whose amplification ratio was at most 1.25, and for all 20 seeds whose ratio was at least four; for the latter, the error fell by a factor of 8 (small models) and 55 (33k models) in geometric mean over seeds. The gain approximately equals the one-step accuracy cost of fine-tuning divided by the amplification ratio. Matching the solver's Jacobian along the model's own error direction (EDJM) outperformed random directions at the same cost on 28 of 35 seeds; input-noise injection performed comparably to EDJM.

</details>


### [159] [Know When the Equation Is Wrong: Structural Uncertainty for LLM-Guided Symbolic Regression under Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42024)
*Mostafa Cham,Bayu Adhi Tama,Jianwu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了 LLM-SR-UQ，通过保留多个候选方程并利用它们之间的分歧刻画结构不确定性，从而改善符号回归模型在外推场景中的预测区间覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有由大语言模型引导的符号回归方法通常只关注单个最佳方程，难以反映不同函数形式带来的不确定性。多个方程可能同样符合观测数据，却在外推时给出差异很大的预测；实验还发现，标准保形区间虽能达到名义上的分布内覆盖率，却常在外推时失效。

Method: 提出 LLM-SR-UQ 框架，保留相互竞争的候选方程，并以它们预测结果之间的分歧衡量对真实函数形式的不确定性。在标准固定宽度保形方法的基础上，框架进一步构造结构区间，用以量化候选方程的分歧，并识别需要扩大预测区间的区域。

Result: 在 LLM-SRBench 数据集上的实验表明，纳入结构分歧后，框架显著改善了覆盖率表现，同时维持分布内覆盖率。将该方法应用于其他符号回归方法时，也表现出较好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 对候选方程结构的不确定性进行建模，有助于弥补仅依据单一方程构造预测区间的不足，尤其能提升外推时的不确定性刻画与覆盖表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can help discover mathematical equations from data, but most LLM-guided symbolic regression methods focus on a single best equation without quantifying uncertainty over alternative functional forms. This is challenging because distinct equations can fit the observed data similarly while producing substantially different extrapolations. Our experiments on the LLM-SRBench dataset show that standard conformal intervals achieve nominal in-distribution coverage but frequently fail under extrapolation. To tackle this challenge, we introduce LLM-SR-UQ, a framework that retains competing equation structures and uses their disagreement to characterize uncertainty about the underlying functional form. We extend the standard fixed-width conformal approach to quantify structural intervals, which measure disagreement among candidate equations and identify where prediction intervals should widen. Extensive experiments show our framework substantially improves coverage attainment by incorporating structural disagreement while preserving in-distribution coverage. Our approach also shows good generalizability when it is applied to other symbolic-regression methods.

</details>


### [160] [GhostPrefix: Synchronization-Free Universal Prefix Attacks for ASR Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42026)
*Shengding Liu,Ashwin Innuganti,Qiben Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GhostPrefix通过预先学习的短音频前缀，在无需与语音同步或了解后续语句的情况下操纵多种自动语音识别系统；实验显示其在数字环境和真实播放环境中均具有较高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有音频攻击往往针对单条语音定制、依赖与受害语音的时间同步，或只能实现有限目标，难以适用于实时、未知内容的语音场景。论文旨在揭示一种更通用、可复用且无需同步的攻击方式，并评估其对语音识别及下游人工智能应用的风险。

Method: GhostPrefix学习一个可添加到任意实时语音前的短音频片段，以实现语义引导、指定文本劫持或转录破坏。为应对语音上下文不断变化的问题，方法结合词元级损失与新的表示级目标，增强攻击效果的稳定性和输入无关性；同时将前缀约束为接近良性环境声音的分布，并提升其抵御真实播放失真的能力。

Result: 研究在四个系列的13种开源自动语音识别模型上进行评估。数字环境中，语义引导和指定文本劫持的平均攻击成功率分别为97.6%和93.4%；非定向攻击可将词错误率提高至1.18。真实播放环境中，两类定向攻击的成功率分别为91.5%和89.5%。攻击还会影响语音助手和人工智能笔记工具，导致系统性语义误解。

Conclusion: 研究表明，无需同步、可复用于任意语音的对抗前缀是自动语音识别及其下游应用面临的一类现实且此前关注不足的威胁。其高成功率和跨场景影响凸显了提升语音识别系统鲁棒性及加强下游应用防护的必要性。

Abstract: We present GhostPrefix, the first synchronization-free universal adversarial prefix attack against automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems. Unlike prior audio attacks, which are typically crafted per utterance, require synchronization with the victim waveform, or target narrow objectives, GhostPrefix learns a short, reusable audio segment prepended to arbitrary live speech to exert real-time control over ASR outputs without knowledge of the future utterance or temporal alignment. A key challenge is maintaining stable adversarial effects across unknown, evolving speech contexts. GhostPrefix combines token-level losses with a novel representation-level objective for stable, input-agnostic effects. GhostPrefix supports three attack modes: (i) prompted prefix attacks for semantic steering, (ii) targeted prefix attacks for transcript hijacking, and (iii) untargeted prefix attacks for transcription degradation. To remain perceptually inconspicuous, we regularize prefixes toward benign environmental-sound distributions and improve robustness against over-the-air distortions. We evaluate GhostPrefix across 13 open-source ASR models from four families. In digital settings, it achieves average attack success rates of 97.6% for prompted steering and 93.4% for targeted hijacking, while driving word error rate to 1.18 under untargeted attacks. Over the air, it achieves success rates of 91.5% and 89.5% for prompted and targeted modes, respectively. These manipulations propagate into downstream AI-assisted pipelines, including voice assistants and AI note-taking tools, causing systematic semantic misinterpretations. GhostPrefix exposes synchronization-free universal prefixes as a practical, under-explored threat to real-world ASR and downstream applications. Artifacts and demos are available at https://ghostprefix.github.io/.

</details>


### [161] [CausalXRL: Explainable Reinforcement Learning through Causal Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42028)
*Yanming Zhang,Eric Papenhausen,Klaus Mueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出因果可解释强化学习框架因果智析，通过因果图推理生成透明、结构化且多层次的决策解释，并在基准环境和即时战略游戏中进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体虽能在复杂环境中取得出色表现，但其决策过程往往难以解释，降低了用户信任，也增加了调试和改进行为的难度。因此，论文旨在提升强化学习决策的可理解性。

Method: 提出因果智析框架，利用因果图推理解释智能体的决策过程，提供不同层次、结构清晰的说明。评估分为两部分：在基准强化学习环境中量化解释忠实度和因果结构学习效率；在即时战略基准游戏微型即时战略中开展专家定性研究。

Result: 定量结果表明，该框架能够提供忠实的解释，并高效学习因果结构。专家研究显示，参与者认为它有助于检查即时战略游戏中的高层策略。

Conclusion: 因果智析为强化学习提供了一种基于因果推理的可解释方案。初步评估表明，该方法兼具解释忠实度与因果结构学习效率，并能支持专家理解高层策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a powerful paradigm for training autonomous agents and has achieved impressive performance in complex environments. However, this success often comes at the cost of interpretability, diminishing trust and complicating efforts to debug and improve agent behavior. To address these challenges, we introduce CausalXRL, a novel framework for explainable reinforcement learning (XRL). A key feature of CausalXRL is its use of causal graph reasoning, which provides transparent, structured, multi-level explanations of agent decision-making. We validate CausalXRL through comprehensive case studies and a two-part evaluation: (1) a quantitative analysis of explanation fidelity and causal-structure learning efficiency in benchmark RL environments, and (2) a qualitative expert study assessing explainability in the real-time strategy (RTS) benchmark MicroRTS. The quantitative results show that CausalXRL can provide faithful explanations while efficiently learning causal structures, and the qualitative expert study suggests that participants found CausalXRL useful for inspecting high-level RTS strategies.

</details>


### [162] [There Is No Free Loop: When Does Looping a Trained Model Pay for Its Inference Compute?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42030)
*Rhea Sanjay Sukthanker,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在预训练模型中追加循环层，仅在参数规模较小、参数预算固定的模型上显示出收益；对较大模型，额外推理计算并未带来相应提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环结构让每个词元重复经过一组网络层，可在不增加参数量、模型存储空间和训练成本的情况下提升计算深度。但它会增加每个生成词元的推理开销，因此关键问题是：在预训练完成后追加循环，是否值得付出额外计算成本？

Method: 研究者在通义千问第三代及第三代点五系列、参数规模从八亿到九十亿的模型上评估追加循环的效果，并与两类基线比较：一是参数量相同、但训练时不含循环的模型；二是推理浮点运算量相同、且采用最佳非循环配置的模型。

Result: 只有八亿参数的循环模型同时优于两类基线。四十亿和九十亿参数的循环模型均未超过参数量相同的非循环模型。结果并非因为基准测试过于简单：在相同测试中，九十亿参数的非循环模型最高比四十亿参数模型高出十三分，说明仍有显著提升空间。

Conclusion: 对已预训练模型追加循环，并非普遍有效的提升方式。其收益主要出现在小模型且参数预算固定的情形；对较大模型，循环带来的额外推理成本没有转化为足够的性能优势。

Abstract: Looping re-applies a block of layers so that each token passes through the block more than once. Adding a loop to a trained model is attractive because it leaves the parameter count, and hence the disk size, the memory footprint and the training cost unchanged. Unfortunately, every generated token pays for the loop. Re-applying m of N layers makes each token about (N+m)/N times as expensive. Loops that a model learns during large-scale pretraining justify this price, but whether loops added after pretraining justify this cost remains unclear. We compare a looped model with two simple baselines. We first compare it with the same model trained identically without the loop (equal parameters). Secondly, we compare it with the best unlooped use of the same inference floating-point operations (equal FLOPs). Across Qwen3 and Qwen3.5 models from 0.8B to 9B parameters, only the looped 0.8B model beats both baselines. At 4B and 9B, the looped model does not beat the first baseline, i.e. the same model trained without the loop. This is not because the benchmarks are too easy, since on the same benchmarks the unlooped 9B model still scores up to 13 points higher than the 4B model, showing that there is ample room for improvement. Introducing loops in an already pretrained model therefore only pays for small models with a fixed parameter budget.

</details>


### [163] [A richness-sensitive Acoustic Biodiversity Index from species-level detections: classifier benchmarking, false-positive control and validation against standard soundscape indices in an African savanna and an urban zoo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42042)
*Muhammad Saeed,Dmitry Mikhaylov,Mohamed H. Alhosani,Yasser F. Al Wahedi,Rory Pilossof,Ahmed Arabi,Walid Shaaba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于物种级检测的声学生物多样性指数，并发现经误报控制和观测努力标准化后，该指数比传统声能指数更能反映物种丰富度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声学指数概括的是声音能量分布，而非发声物种，因此往往难以准确追踪物种丰富度。研究旨在构建更具生态解释性的生物多样性指标，并检验其可靠性及局限。

Method: 研究将物种检测的香农多样性按当地、当地季节预期的区域物种库进行标准化，形成主指标；另将时间稳定性作为独立组成部分报告。研究先在包含一千二百一十六段叫声、二十四个类别的严格标注热带数据集上，以录音组为单位进行交叉验证，对比四种分类器；随后将表现最佳的嵌入特征与逻辑回归分类方法用于津巴布韦赞比西河谷稀树草原和阿联酋动物园三种栖息地的录音，并采用地点与季节筛选、误报剔除及昼夜匹配的稀释抽样，将观测努力统一为六小时。

Result: 在分类器比较中，鸟鸣识别模型嵌入特征结合逻辑回归表现最佳，宏平均调和均值为零点七五零；随机按音频帧划分数据会使准确率从零点四一虚高至零点七零。进一步的地点筛选从动物园录音中剔除五百一十七条、涉及九个不合理物种的高置信度误报。统一观测努力后，稀树草原记录到二十一个物种，第一阶希尔数为九点八三，标准化指数为零点三八；动物园各点记录到一点三至十三个物种，指数为零点零三至零点二一。该指数与物种丰富度高度相关，斯皮尔曼相关系数为零点九三；六种传统指数均未达到同等水平，表现最好者的相关系数为零点四八。时间稳定性指标在稀树草原较低，显示其会惩罚鸟类活动自然的昼夜变化。

Conclusion: 以物种级检测为基础的指数，在控制误报、明确区域参考物种库并统一观测努力后，比基于声能的传统指数更能准确、直观地表征物种丰富度。不过，时间稳定性应与多样性主指标分开解释，因为自然的昼夜活动周期可能降低该指标，并不必然意味着生态状况较差。

Abstract: Acoustic indices are widely used as rapid proxies for biodiversity, but they summarise the distribution of sound energy rather than the species producing it and often track richness poorly. We revise and test an Acoustic Biodiversity Index (ABI) built from species-level detections. Its primary form, ABI_E, is Shannon diversity of detections normalised by the regional species pool expected at the site and season (H / ln S_ref); temporal stability T is reported as a separate component. We first benchmarked four classifiers on a strongly labelled tropical dataset (1,216 calls, 24 classes) with recording-grouped cross-validation. BirdNET embeddings + logistic regression performed best (macro-F1 0.750 +/- 0.029), and random frame-level splits inflated accuracy from 0.41 to 0.70. We then applied BirdNET v2.4 with a location and season filter to recordings from a riverine savanna in the Zambezi Valley, Zimbabwe, and from three habitats at Emirates Park Zoo and Resort, Abu Dhabi, United Arab Emirates. A stricter post-hoc location filter removed 517 high-confidence detections of 9 implausible species, all at the zoo. After diel-matched rarefaction to equal effort (6 h), the savanna site held 21 species (Hill number 1D = 9.83; ABI_E = 0.38) against 1.3-13 species (ABI_E = 0.03-0.21) at the zoo sites. Across 149 recorded hours, ABI_E correlated strongly with richness (Spearman rho = 0.93), whereas six standard indices did not (best: ACI, rho = 0.48). The stability term was lower at the savanna site (0.39) than at the zoo (0.65-0.67), showing that it penalises the natural diel cycle of bird activity. Species-level indices with explicit false-positive control, a declared regional reference pool and effort standardisation provide a more interpretable biodiversity indicator than energy-based acoustic indices.

</details>


### [164] [Differentially Private Synthetic Data from Sliced Chebyshev Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42081)
*Lucas Rosenblatt,R. Teal Witter,Christopher Musco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出差分隐私合成数据算法“斯普莱斯”，通过匹配随机方向上的切片切比雪夫矩，在高维数据中实现与维度无关的全局分布误差保证，并在基准测试中表现领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实用方法多致力于近似保留低维边缘分布，难以可靠保留高维整体结构；而已有针对全局精度的方法，其代价会随数据维度呈指数增长。论文旨在克服这一维度灾难，提供兼具理论保证与实用性能的差分隐私合成数据方法。

Method: 算法对目标分布在多个随机方向上的切片切比雪夫多项式矩进行加噪测量，再拟合出与这些测量矩相匹配的分布。作者还证明，对单位球内的分布，若在约为误差参数倒数阶数的低阶切比雪夫矩上进行匹配，并使用约与维度成正比、与误差参数平方成反比数量级的随机方向，即可保证相应的切片 Wasserstein-1 误差。实际实现借鉴了张量分解领域的工具。

Result: 在隐私参数为常数时，给定包含 n 个样本的数据集，该算法输出的私有分布与目标分布之间的切片 Wasserstein-1 距离至多为约 n 的平方根倒数，忽略对数因子；这一误差界不依赖数据维度。实验表明，该方法在广泛基准测试中的表现优于或达到此前差分隐私合成数据方法的领先水平。

Conclusion: 论文表明，通过在随机方向上匹配多项式矩，可以在避免维度指数级代价的同时获得有力的全局分布保证。该方法兼具维度无关的理论误差界和有竞争力的实践表现，为高维差分隐私合成数据提供了新的方案。

Abstract: Research on practical differentially private (DP) synthetic data has largely focused on methods that approximately preserve low-dimensional marginals of a target distribution, and can thus only hope to preserve higher-dimensional, global structure as a lucky by-product. At the same time, existing methods that target global accuracy guarantees pay a price exponential in the data dimension, $d$.
  In this work, we address this ``curse of dimensionality'' by presenting a new DP synthetic data algorithm, SPLICE, that comes with a compelling global distributional guarantee, even in high-dimensions, and moreover, is practically state-of-the-art. Our algorithm is based on measuring noisy sliced Chebyshev polynomial moments of the target distribution and fitting a distribution to match, a twist on recent polynomial methods for 1-D synthetic data.
  We prove that, given a dataset of $n$ examples, SPLICE returns a private distribution with Sliced Wasserstein-1 distance $\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$ from the target, for constant privacy parameters. Notably, this bound is independent of the data dimension, $d$. Along the way, we prove a moment matching result of independent interest: for a distribution on the unit ball, matching the first $O(1/α)$ Chebyshev moments along $\tilde{O}(d/α^2)$ random directions guarantees $α$ Sliced Wasserstein error.
  Finally, we provide a practical implementation of the algorithm that borrows tools from the tensor decomposition literature and achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to prior DP synthetic data methods on an extensive benchmark.

</details>


### [165] [Cost-Aware Stopping for Freeze-Thaw Bayesian Optimization: When Does One-Step Lookahead Suffice?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42085)
*Long Vu,Jongseong Brad Choi,Binoy Ravindran,Zhoulai Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以成本为依据的停止规则：当任何候选的预期改进都不足以抵偿继续训练的成本时便停止，并用两步前瞻证书纠正可能过早的停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结—解冻贝叶斯优化可以分阶段、可恢复地评估候选方案，但通常依赖预算上限或收敛检验来决定何时停止，难以直接权衡训练收益与成本。论文因此研究如何最大化不设固定预算的效用，即最佳观测分数减去每次训练增量的成本。

Method: 在固定代理模型下，采用一步前瞻规则：选择预期改进最大的候选继续训练；若所有候选的预期改进均不超过单次增量成本，则停止。作者证明该规则的期望效用不低于立即停止，但它仍可能过早停止。为此，作者构造两步前瞻价值的下界证书，用以识别并纠正过早停止；反复应用该纠正不会降低代理模型下的期望效用。

Result: 代理模型分析显示，成本较低时，一步规则平均而言容易过早停止；成本较高时，其效用损失较小。对三个超参数优化基准的预注册检验发现，在多数高成本设置下，一步规则与纠正方法相比不劣；在三个基准中的两个上，纠正方法在低成本设置下更有收益。代理模型中信息共享越充分，一步规则的效用越高。应用于两种已发表方法的代理模型时，一步规则以较少训练增量换取较低分数。

Conclusion: 预期改进停止规则简单、具备成本意识，可作为冻结—解冻贝叶斯优化的基准方法；其表现取决于代理模型。低成本场景中，两步前瞻证书有助于避免过早停止，而高成本场景下，一步规则通常已具有竞争力。

Abstract: Freeze-thaw Bayesian optimization (BO) evaluates candidates in resumable increments but usually stops at a budget or convergence test. We maximize a budget-free utility, the best observed score minus a cost per increment, with a one-step lookahead: advance the candidate with the largest expected improvement (EI) under a fixed surrogate, and stop once no EI exceeds the cost. This rule, EI stopping, never falls below stopping immediately in expected utility, yet it can stop too early. We construct a certificate, a lower bound on the value of two-step lookahead, that proves such a stop premature, and repeating this correction never lowers expected utility under the surrogate. Under the surrogate, EI stops are premature on average at low costs and lose little at high costs. On three hyperparameter optimization benchmarks, a pre-registered test finds EI stopping noninferior to the correction in most high-cost settings, while the correction gains at low costs on two of the three; more information sharing in the surrogate raises EI stopping's utility. On the surrogates of two published methods, EI stopping trades lower scores for fewer training increments. EI stopping is a simple cost-aware reference whose utility depends on the surrogate.

</details>


### [166] [Towards Understanding the Impact of Plasticity Loss on Reinforcement Learning in Stochastic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42100)
*Philipp Bordne,André Biedenkapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，随机性对深度强化学习中的网络可塑性影响取决于环境难度：在困难任务中可能加速可塑性丧失，而在简单任务中可发挥正则化作用；保留可塑性的方法能提升部分困难任务的学习稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习虽已取得显著进展，却常表现脆弱。已有研究将这种脆弱性与神经网络可塑性丧失联系起来，但这一现象在随机环境中的表现尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在系统分析环境随机性、可塑性变化与学习稳健性之间的关系。

Method: 研究者在三个连续控制任务上训练软演员—评论家智能体，并对观测和动作加入不同程度的噪声，以控制环境随机性。随后比较保留网络可塑性的方法及其组合方案，并考察训练阶段、任务难度和噪声水平对学习表现及失效模式的影响。

Result: 在两个较困难的任务中，保留可塑性的方法提高了学习稳健性，且观测噪声越大，其优势越明显。将网络重置与层归一化结合，在所有测试的观测噪声水平下都不如单独重置。随机性会在困难环境中加速可塑性丧失，但在简单环境中可能作为正则化因素帮助维持可塑性。可塑性丧失主要集中于训练早期；在最具挑战性的随机环境中，时序差分不稳定性比渐进式可塑性丧失更可能成为主要失效原因。

Conclusion: 随机性对深度强化学习的影响并非单一：其作用取决于任务难度和训练阶段。提升稳健性不能只关注可塑性丧失，还需考虑时序差分学习的不稳定性；同时，干预措施的组合不一定优于单一方法。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) is a powerful framework for sequential decision-making. Despite many high-profile successes in recent years, deep RL is known to be brittle. An emerging line of research attributes this brittleness to a loss of plasticity in neural networks. However, plasticity loss has so far been understudied in stochastic environments. We study this interplay systematically, training SAC agents on three DeepMind Control tasks augmented with controlled levels of observation and action noise. We find that plasticity-preserving methods increase learning robustness on the two harder tasks, with their advantage growing as observation noise increases, while combining resets with layer normalization underperforms resets alone at every observation noise level tested. Further, stochasticity can accelerate the loss of plasticity in harder environments, whereas in easier settings noise can act as a regularizer that sustains plasticity. Lastly, plasticity loss is most pronounced in the early stages of training. In the most challenging stochastic settings, TD instability rather than gradual plasticity loss becomes the dominant failure mode.

</details>


### [167] [Hear No Evil: Detecting Gradient Leakage by Malicious Servers in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20651)
*Fei Wang,Baochun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从防御者视角分析联邦学习中的恶意梯度泄露攻击，指出攻击效果与隐蔽性难以兼得，并提出轻量级客户端检测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户端上传的梯度可能泄露本地数据；恶意服务器还可操纵全局模型，诱导客户端产生更易泄露信息的更新。论文旨在系统评估此类攻击的实际风险及可行防御。

Method: 全面分析恶意梯度泄露攻击及其依赖的模型操纵技术，并考察常见归一化方法和联邦平均机制对攻击的影响。基于攻击有效性与隐蔽性之间的权衡，提出一种在本地训练开始前检查模型更新的轻量级客户端检测机制。

Result: 研究发现，攻击难以同时实现高效重建私有数据和逃避检测；在采用常见归一化及联邦平均的现实场景中，这一限制尤为明显。许多攻击可通过基础监控发现，所提检测机制也能以较低开销识别可疑更新。

Conclusion: 恶意梯度泄露虽在理论上构成隐私威胁，但其现实攻击能力受到有效性与隐蔽性冲突的制约。配合基础监控和轻量级客户端检测，可为注重隐私的联邦学习系统提供实用防护。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that gradient updates in federated learning (FL) can unintentionally reveal sensitive information about a client's local data. This risk becomes significantly greater when a malicious server manipulates the global model to provoke information-rich updates from clients. In this paper, we adopt a defender's perspective to provide the first comprehensive analysis of malicious gradient leakage attacks and the model manipulation techniques that enable them. Our investigation reveals a core trade-off: these attacks cannot be both highly effective in reconstructing private data and sufficiently stealthy to evade detection -- especially in realistic FL settings that incorporate common normalization techniques and federated averaging.
  Building on this insight, we argue that malicious gradient leakage attacks, while theoretically concerning, are inherently limited in practice and often detectable through basic monitoring. As a complementary contribution, we propose a simple, lightweight, and broadly applicable client-side detection mechanism that flags suspicious model updates before local training begins, despite the fact that such detection may not be strictly necessary in realistic FL settings. This mechanism further underscores the feasibility of defending against these attacks with minimal overhead, offering a deployable safeguard for privacy-conscious federated learning systems.

</details>


### [168] [Hyperbolic Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42104)
*Iulia Rusu,Anoop Praturu,Sergei Gepshtein,Tatyana Sharpee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出双曲逆向强化学习，用特征与学习参数之间的双曲距离构造奖励函数；实验表明，该方法在多种任务中预测表现有竞争力，并能揭示特征之间的组合式奖励结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有逆向强化学习方法常用线性权重、神经网络等方式表示奖励，却通常不考虑特征空间的几何结构。这可能限制模型在数据较少时的预测能力，也不利于解释多个特征如何共同影响行为。论文受神经回路中几何组织现象启发，探索双曲几何能否改善奖励建模。

Method: 提出双曲逆向强化学习，将奖励表示为特征向量与可学习参数向量之间双曲距离的函数，并以欧氏距离版本作为对照。作者在确定性和随机策略条件下，将该方法与多种线性及非线性基线比较；此外，将其用于分析人们在博物馆展厅中多个展品之间的导航行为。

Result: 在既有基准上，双曲方法的表现与先进模型相当；在训练数据较少和环境较密集时表现突出。博物馆导航分析显示，奖励不仅取决于各展品距离特征本身，也包含这些特征之间不可分离的组合关系。

Conclusion: 以双曲距离构造奖励，为逆向强化学习提供了一种兼顾预测能力与可解释性的表示方式。该方法尤其有助于在有限数据条件下建模行为，并揭示特征间的联合影响，为研究组合式奖励结构提供了框架。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) seeks to infer the reward function underlying observed behavior, but its predictive accuracy and interpretability depend on how that reward is computed from the underlying features. While prior IRL reward functions have been implemented through a variety of techniques including linear weights, neural networks, or other function approximators, none make assumptions about the geometry of the underlying feature space. Inspired by geometric organization observed in neural circuits, we introduce Hyperbolic inverse reinforcement learning (Hyperbolic IRL), which parameterizes reward as a function of Hyperbolic distance between the feature vector and a learned parameter vector, as well as its Euclidean distance variant. We tested our methods against a range of linear and nonlinear IRL baselines under both deterministic and stochastic policies. Our Hyperbolic approach achieved comparable performance to state-of-the-art models on established benchmarks and excelled in the low data limit and across dense environments. Applied to human navigation among multiple exhibits in a museum room, Hyperbolic IRL reveals reward structure that depends not only on individual exhibit-distance features, but also on non-separable conjunctions between them. Together, these results demonstrate that a Hyperbolic reward parameterization can improve both the predictive accuracy and interpretability of IRL, providing a framework for studying how non-separable, conjunctive structure can begin to explain behavior.

</details>


### [169] [Leaner Training, Lower Leakage: Revisiting Memorization in LLM Fine-Tuning with LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20856)
*Fei Wang,Baochun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，与全量微调相比，低秩适配微调能在保持较强任务表现的同时，显著降低模型记忆训练数据的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型记忆训练数据可能使大语言模型遭受数据提取攻击。尽管预训练阶段的记忆现象已得到广泛研究，微调阶段的影响，尤其是参数高效的低秩适配微调，仍缺乏充分探讨。已有关于模型规模和数据重复的结论是否适用于不同微调策略，也有待重新检验。

Method: 研究重新考察微调中的数据记忆，并比较不同微调策略下模型规模、数据重复等因素与记忆现象的关系。作者采用较宽松的、基于相似度的记忆度量方法，评估低秩适配微调与全量微调的记忆风险及任务表现。

Result: 研究发现，模型规模和数据重复对低秩适配微调中记忆现象的影响趋势，并不遵循它们在预训练和全量微调中的既有规律。按基于相似度的度量，低秩适配微调的记忆风险显著低于全量微调，同时仍能保持较强的任务表现。

Conclusion: 微调策略会改变数据记忆的规律，不能直接将预训练或全量微调中的发现套用于低秩适配微调。低秩适配微调有望在维持任务效果的同时降低数据提取风险，但这一结论依赖于所采用的记忆度量及研究设定。

Abstract: Memorization in large language models (LLMs) makes them vulnerable to data extraction attacks. While pre-training memorization has been extensively studied, fewer works have explored its impact in fine-tuning, particularly for LoRA fine-tuning, a widely adopted parameter-efficient method.
  In this work, we re-examine memorization in fine-tuning and uncover a surprising divergence from prior findings across different fine-tuning strategies. Factors such as model scale and data duplication, which strongly influence memorization in pre-training and full fine-tuning, do not follow the same trend in LoRA fine-tuning. Using a more relaxed similarity-based memorization metric, we demonstrate that LoRA significantly reduces memorization risks compared to full fine-tuning, while still maintaining strong task performance.

</details>


### [170] [Fast Privacy Loss Accounting for Random Allocation via Multiscale FFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42117)
*Vitaly Feldman,Moshe Shenfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出多尺度快速傅里叶变换算法，加速随机分配采样方案的隐私损失分布计算，并降低舍入误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机分配采样有助于隐私优化和高效聚合，但现有隐私损失分布计算器的运行时间随精度参数α的倒数平方增长，难以高效满足高精度需求。此外，舍入操作会给隐私损失均值和卡方散度带来误差。

Method: 提出多尺度快速傅里叶变换算法：在每个二进制区间内采用均匀网格表示似然比，并利用快速傅里叶变换精确计算区间内每一对数值相加后的舍入结果。算法保留原计算器的理论保证。论文还采用保持均值的连点投影进行舍入，以控制舍入误差。

Result: 算法将运行时间对α的依赖从平方级改善为约α倒数乘以其对数，即从O(α⁻²)降至O(α⁻¹ log(1/α))。保持均值的投影将均值和卡方散度上的舍入误差从α量级降至O(α²/log(t))。实验显示，在高精度设置下，算法取得数个数量级的加速，并为采用前述采样方案的差分隐私随机梯度下降提供了更紧的隐私界。

Conclusion: 多尺度网格与快速傅里叶变换能够显著提升随机分配采样隐私会计的计算效率；配合保持均值的舍入方法，还能进一步减少关键统计量的误差，从而改善实际差分隐私分析中的隐私界。

Abstract: In the random allocation sampling scheme each user's data is used in $k$ steps chosen randomly and uniformly from a sequence of $t$ steps. This scheme is known to improve on Poisson sampling in private optimization and in communication-efficient aggregation. The existing privacy loss distribution (PLD) accountant for this scheme runs in time quadratic in the inverse of the accuracy parameter $α$ (Feldman and Shenfeld, 2026). We give an algorithm, \emph{multiscale FFT}, that represents likelihood ratios on a grid that is uniform within each dyadic interval and computes the exact rounding of every pairwise sum using FFTs within each interval. It keeps the guarantees of the accountant and improves the dependence of its running time on $α$ from $O(α^{-2})$ to $O(α^{-1}\log(1/α))$. In addition, we demonstrate that rounding can be performed via the mean-preserving connect-the-dots projection of (Doroshenko et al., 2026). This reduces the errors that rounding introduces in the mean of the privacy loss and in the $χ^2$ divergence of the computed pair from the order of $α$ to $O(α^2/\log(t))$. In our experiments, our algorithm achieves speedups of several orders of magnitude at high accuracy and gives improved privacy bounds for DP-SGD with PREAMBLE.

</details>


### [171] [Gradient Descent on Two ReLU Neurons: Global Landscape and Bifurcation Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42118)
*Binghua Li,Mengzhe Li,Denny Wu,Tianhao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究一个最简双神经元模型中的梯度下降学习过程，揭示学生神经元先聚合到易学的平分方向，再沿较难的分裂方向分化并匹配教师神经元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解梯度下降如何从数据中学得特征，是神经网络理论中的核心问题。即使在结构简单的多指标模型中，学习过程也可能包含多个阶段与对称性破缺，因此需要同时刻画损失地形和训练轨迹。

Method: 研究者分析一个设定良好的双神经元整流线性单元教师—学生模型：输入服从各向同性高斯分布，采用总体平方损失，并从小幅随机初始化出发进行总体梯度流分析。分析重点是两种方向：承载主要信号、相对容易学习的平分方向，以及推动神经元分工、相对难以学习的分裂方向。

Result: 损失地形分析表明，凡是两个学生权重均非零的临界点，不是全局极小点就是鞍点。动力学分析显示，学生神经元起初会向一个平分方向的鞍点聚合；随后离开该鞍点，沿分裂方向发生专门化，最终分别靠近教师神经元。

Conclusion: 该研究从损失地形与训练动力学两方面解释了一个简单多指标模型中的多阶段对称性破缺：学习先由易学方向捕获主要信号，再由较难的分裂方向促成特征专门化。结果说明，这种过程可在常见的模型结构与训练算法下自然出现。

Abstract: Understanding how gradient descent learns features remains a central challenge in neural network theory. We study this question in a minimal multi-index setting that already exhibits rich multi-phase gradient dynamics: a well-specified two-neuron ReLU teacher-student model under isotropic Gaussian inputs, trained by population gradient flow from small initialization. We show that the dynamics are organized by two directions: the "easy" bisector direction, which carries the leading signal, and the "hard" splitting direction, which governs specialization. To characterize the loss structure and learning behavior, we analyze the population squared-loss landscape and show that every critical point with both student weights nonzero is either a global minimum or a saddle. We then track the gradient-flow trajectory, showing that student neurons first collapse toward a bisector saddle before escaping and specializing to teacher neurons. Our results provide both a landscape and dynamics account of the multi-phase symmetry-breaking behavior that arises in a simple multi-index model and under standard algorithmic and architectural choices.

</details>


### [172] [Physiological Trajectories and Observation Patterns in ICU Mortality Prediction: Transportability Under Changing Measurement Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42126)
*Benjamin T. Pike*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，生理指标随时间变化的轨迹比测量频率和时点等观察模式更容易跨医院、跨数据库迁移；预测模型还应在不同测量条件下接受评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重症监护病房的死亡预测既依赖患者的生理状态，也可能利用医护人员何时、多久测量一次指标所体现的信息。然而，观察模式可能受医院流程和数据可用性影响，未必能迁移至新环境。研究旨在区分生理轨迹与观察模式的预测价值，并检验测量时点是否在观测数量之外独立影响模型表现。

Method: 研究使用三套重症监护数据库预测患者入院后四十八小时内的院内死亡。研究构建逐步扩展的特征表示：先使用最近一次测量值，再加入生理指标的纵向变化摘要，最后加入测量频率与时点特征。模型在一个数据库的七千七百六十二名患者中训练，并在另外两套数据库及多家医院的数据中评估。研究还在固定保留观测数量的条件下，比较不同测量时点保留策略对预测表现的影响。

Result: 加入生理轨迹后，模型在外部数据上的对数损失有所下降，显示其具有较稳定的迁移价值。观察模式特征改善了本地开发模型的表现，但在从一个数据库迁移至另一数据库时，增益不明确。固定观测数量后，保留分布于整个预测窗口的测量，以及移除窗口末段测量，均显示测量时点会影响预测表现；这种影响在加入观察模式特征的模型中更明显。测量可用性降低时，本地模型中观察模式特征的增益还可能由正转负。

Conclusion: 生理轨迹是较具可迁移性的预测信息，而测量频率与时点相关特征对具体数据环境和测量可用性更敏感。评估临床预测模型时，除了检验其能否适用于新患者群体，也应考察其在不同测量流程与数据缺失条件下的表现。

Abstract: Objective: To distinguish the transferable predictive value of physiological trajectories from that of observation patterns in ICU mortality prediction, and to assess whether measurement timing affects performance independently of observation quantity.
  Materials and Methods: In-hospital mortality was predicted at 48 hours using MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV, and eICU. Nested representations progressively integrated latest-value snapshots (R1), longitudinal physiological summaries (R2), and measurement frequency and timing features (R3). Models fitted on 7,762 MetaVision patients were evaluated on 21,682 MIMIC-IV patients and 43,878 eICU patients across 204 hospitals. The effect of measurement timing was assessed while holding the number of retained observations constant.
  Results: Physiological trajectories improved external prediction in eICU (R2-R1 log-loss difference -0.00922, 95% CI -0.01148 to -0.00704). Observation-pattern features improved locally developed eICU models (R3-R2 -0.00257, 95% CI -0.00370 to -0.00146), but not clearly after transfer from MetaVision (-0.00012, 95% CI -0.00206 to +0.00208). R3 was favoured in 10 of 30 source bootstrap refits. At matched observation counts, retaining measurements spread throughout the window reduced log loss relative to random retention by 0.03280 for R3 versus 0.00703 for R2. Removing the final 12 hours increased log loss relative to count-matched random removal by 0.01947 for R3 versus 0.00822 for R2. Under reduced availability, the local R3 increment changed from beneficial to harmful.
  Discussion: Physiological trajectories transferred more consistently, while observation-pattern features were particularly sensitive to the timing and availability of measurements.
  Conclusion: Clinical prediction models should be evaluated not only in new patient populations but also under the measurement conditions that supply their inputs.

</details>


### [173] [Instance-dependent sequential limits as an exact information ledger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42133)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将多类序贯决策问题的经典下界统一写成一条可核算的推导链，并指出各步骤造成的差距及其来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机赌博机遗憾、序贯检验、最佳臂识别和马尔可夫决策过程等问题，常用相似的信息论论证得到实例相关下界，但从一般恒等式到经典极限之间包含若干松弛，因而难以判断算法与下界相差多少、差距出自何处。

Method: 作者把推导过程整理为精确的“账本”：从散度分解出发，经数据处理、统计量构造、检验论证，再对备选实例与资源分配进行优化。除第一步为恒等式外，其余步骤都明确记录引入的损失。利用链式法则，数据处理造成的损失恰为条件散度；对于自然指数族，该项在给定各臂采样次数和奖励和时为零。作者还提出一参数鞅恒等式，将分解推广到两个实例之间的任意倾斜程度，并通过逐项松弛恢复经典下界。

Result: 在七个已发表来源的案例中，涵盖电影评分和发动机更换等任务，账本能够定位常见算法与经典极限之间的差距。除渐近步骤产生的缺陷外，其余缺陷通常可由算法自身运行数据计算；均匀量化步骤的缺陷则只有在类别保证能够表示为一个数值时才可计算。对于训练模型识别和瓦尔德检验，最大的差距通常来自均匀量化。

Conclusion: 该工作把不同问题中的经典下界统一为一条带有明确损失项的推导链，不仅说明下界如何得到，也提供了诊断其松弛程度的方法。其分析表明，经典极限与实际算法之间的差距往往不只来自信息损失，保证条件的均匀量化也可能是主要来源。

Abstract: Instance-dependent limits for bandit regret, sequential tests, best-arm identification and Markov decision processes are proved by one chain. It runs from a divergence decomposition through data processing to a statistic, a testing step that turns a class guarantee into a requirement on the history, and optimization over alternatives and allocations. Only the first step is an identity. This work writes the chain as an exact ledger. By the chain rule, data processing loses exactly a conditional divergence, which vanishes for arms in natural exponential families at the per-arm counts and reward sums. A one-parameter family of martingale identities extends the decomposition to every tilt between two instances. The classical limits follow by relaxations with named defects. All defects but the asymptotic step's can be computed from an algorithm's own runs, the uniform quantification's only when the class guarantee is a number. On seven published sources, from movie ratings to engine replacement, the ledger locates the difference between standard algorithms and the classical limits. For identification among trained models and for Wald's test the largest term is usually the uniform quantification's.

</details>


### [174] [Counting on Dummies: Privacy Amplification with Observable Counts and Truncated Batches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42139)
*Vitaly Feldman,Audra McMillan,Kunal Talwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出通过加入随机数量的虚拟数据点来隐藏批次大小，并在批次截断时维持差分隐私保证；该机制适用于泊松抽样和随机分配，在截断场景下展现出更优的隐私与效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机批次抽样可借助隐私放大改善隐私保证，但常见抽样方式会产生大小不固定的批次，其隐私保证依赖于批次大小不被泄露。在分布式场景中，聚合服务器或通信观察者可能获知批次大小；此外，内存和计算限制可能要求将超出上限的批次截断，而截断会显著增加隐私损失。

Method: 提出一种利用随机数量虚拟数据点对批次大小进行隐私化的机制，使批次大小不易被观察，并在批次截断后仍能维持隐私保证。论文分析了该机制在泊松抽样和随机分配下的表现，设计了便于高效且相对紧确地计算隐私损失的分析方法。通过抽样带来的隐私放大，该机制所需虚拟数据点的期望数量渐近地少于直接对敏感度为一的批次计数添加非负噪声所需的数量。

Result: 论文表明，该机制易于在分布式环境中实现，并可用于两类常见抽样方案。针对截断批次的泊松抽样实验显示，与近期提出的直接截断隐私损失计算方法相比，该机制取得了更好的隐私与效率权衡。

Conclusion: 随机虚拟数据点为隐藏批次大小和缓解截断造成的隐私损失提供了一种简单、可部署的方案。其分析兼顾高效性与较紧确性，且实验结果支持其在截断场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: In many practical deployments of Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD), the server processes data in batches. Randomly sampling these batches allows for better privacy bounds via privacy amplification results. Most sampling schemes with provable privacy amplification, including Poisson sampling and random allocation, result in variable batch sizes. The privacy guarantee of these schemes requires that the batch sizes are not revealed. In distributed settings, the server performing the aggregation or any party observing the communication traffic may learn these batch sizes. Furthermore, in both centralized and distributed settings, memory and computation constraints may require each batch to be of some maximum size $s$, necessitating truncation of the batch when it exceeds this limit. It is known that this truncation can result in a significant increase in privacy loss. In this work, we address both of these challenges via a simple mechanism that uses a random number of \emph{dummy points} to privatize the batch size and maintain the privacy guarantee even under truncation. We demonstrate that our mechanism is easy to implement in a distributed setting, and provide a general analysis of this mechanism for both Poisson sampling and random allocation. Our analysis is designed to support efficient and relatively tight privacy accounting.
