### [208] [Lifelong Learning of Scheduling Rules with Surrogate-assisted Genetic Programming for Dynamic Flexible Job Shop Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41158)
*Luyao Zhu,Fangfang Zhang,Yi Mei,Ke Chen,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向动态柔性作业车间调度终身学习的代理辅助遗传编程方法，可在适应新任务的同时减少灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗传编程训练出的调度规则通常只适用于规模和分布相近的任务；任务特征改变后性能容易下降，而直接微调旧规则又可能造成灾难性遗忘。针对动态柔性作业车间调度中的连续异质任务，尤其是作业或批次动态到达的情形，亟需兼顾新任务适应与既有知识保留的终身学习方法。

Method: 提出代理辅助遗传编程框架，包含三项关键设计：一是采用自适应两阶段训练策略，协调模型对新任务的适应能力与对既有知识的稳定保持；二是依据任务相似度调整知识复用权重，控制不同历史任务知识的迁移程度；三是改进代理模型并加入距离约束，以提高估计准确性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够有效缓解灾难性遗忘，同时保持较强的新任务适应能力；与多种对比方法相比表现更优。摘要未提供具体数据或任务基准，因此无法进一步量化性能提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该研究将终身学习引入动态柔性作业车间调度的遗传编程，表明通过自适应训练、相似度引导的知识复用和距离约束代理模型，可以更好地应对连续异质任务，为动态调度规则的持续学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Genetic programming (GP) has been widely used to learn scheduling rules for dynamic flexible job shop scheduling (DFJSS). However, trained rules tend to only generalise to tasks of similar scales and distributions, and when task characteristics change, learned rules often suffer significant performance degradation. Moreover, fine-tuning pre-trained rules on new tasks often leads to catastrophic forgetting, where prior knowledge is lost. This motivates the need for GP methods that can adapt to new tasks while preserving past knowledge, leading to a lifelong learning paradigm. However, lifelong learning in GP for DFJSS remains largely under-explored. To address this gap, this paper proposes a surrogate-assisted GP method for lifelong learning to handle sequential heterogeneous DFJSS tasks with job or batch arrivals. The framework includes three key components: an adaptive two-stage training strategy to balance plasticity and stability, a task-similarity-based weighting mechanism to regulate knowledge reuse, and an improved surrogate model with distance constraints to enhance estimation accuracy. Experimental results show that the proposed method effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting while maintaining strong adaptability, outperforming several compared methods and demonstrating the potential of lifelong GP in dynamic scheduling environments.

</details>


### [209] [FrontierMat: Benchmarking Language Models at the Frontier of Materials R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41162)
*Feng Chen,Jianguo Huang,Yushan Xiao,Yue Li,Bijun Tang,Xuyu Dong,Xingguo Wang,Feiyang Xie,Jianhui Gu,Dongfeng Cao,Hongda Chen,Yongji Gong,Ruiqi Li,Shuxin Li,Xingji Li,Ruitao Lv,Li Song,Lei Wang,Ye Zhang,Xiaoxu Zhao,Xitao Zheng,Bo An,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FrontierMat 是一个由 330 道前沿材料研究问题构成的基准，用于评估语言模型能否依据真实实验信息推断研究结论；结果显示当前模型仍有明显不足，而针对性训练可显著提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多源自教材和考试，主要检验已确立的知识，难以反映语言模型处理前沿材料研发问题的能力。为衡量模型能否支持真实科研与产业研发，作者构建了经过领域专家验证的前沿问题测试集。

Method: 基准包含来自航空航天、半导体、具身智能和核聚变等领域的 330 道问题。每题基于一项真实研究，要求模型推断被隐去的实验结果，或依据选定实验证据得出结论。问题按功能领域、材料体系和主导机制尺度标注，其中 130 题需要跨尺度推理。研究还通过元权重持续预训练与尺度模块化自蒸馏，对开放权重模型进行领域适配。

Result: 最强通用模型的得分为 53.7%；在匹配题目上，跨尺度问题比单尺度问题低 8.7 个百分点。半数问题至多只有一个模型答对。模型的主要失误是把“听起来合理”的判断当作实际观测结果。领域适配使开放权重模型得分从 36.7% 提升至 55.4%，达到最强专有模型的水平；其在公开材料基准上的表现也有所改善，且通用基准总体表现未下降。

Conclusion: FrontierMat 为评估语言模型在材料研究前沿问题上的能力提供了经专家验证的测试平台。实验结果表明，模型在证据忠实性和跨尺度推理方面仍面临挑战，但有针对性的领域适配能够显著提升其科研问题解决能力。

Abstract: To accelerate materials discovery, language models must be able to address problems at the research frontier. Yet textbook- and examination-derived benchmarks primarily assess established knowledge, leaving performance on frontier research problems largely untested. We introduce FrontierMat, a benchmark of 330 problems drawn from frontier academic and industrial research in fields including aerospace, semiconductors, embodied intelligence and nuclear fusion. Every problem is validated by leading experimentalists and industry experts in its field. Each problem presents a real study and asks the model to infer a withheld outcome or draw a conclusion from selected experimental evidence. Problems are annotated by functional domain, material system and the scale of the governing mechanism; 130 require cross-scale reasoning. The strongest general-purpose model scores only 53.7%, and on matched problems, cross-scale problems score 8.7 points below single-scale ones. Half of the problems are solved by at most one model, and models fail chiefly by endorsing what is plausible rather than what was observed. A domain-adaptation study with meta-weighted continued pretraining and scale-modular self-distillation raises an open-weight model from 36.7% to 55.4%, matching the strongest proprietary model, with improvements on public materials benchmarks and no decline in aggregate performance on general benchmarks. FrontierMat provides a testbed for measuring progress toward language models that support frontier materials R&D.

</details>


### [210] [LLM Detoxification via Transition-Aware Reward-Guided Representation Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41165)
*Aishan Liu,Yisong Xiao,Xianglong Liu,Yongxin Huang,Siyuan Liang,Zonghao Ying,Yang Qu,Jian Yang,Yaodong Yang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向毒性状态转变的测试时表示引导方法，通过逐步探索学习细粒度奖励，并以由粗到细的采样降低生成内容的毒性，同时尽量保留模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可能生成有害或冒犯性内容，因此需要有效的测试时去毒方法。现有表示引导技术常采用静态或粗粒度干预，未充分刻画生成内容从非毒性到毒性状态的变化，因而难以提供精确指导。

Method: 该方法先沿毒性方向逐步引导模型表示，构造从非毒性到毒性状态的轨迹，并利用轨迹上的毒性评分，将稀疏标注转化为密集的列表式监督，以训练能够感知毒性转变的逐词奖励模型。推理时，模型沿相反的非毒性方向进行由粗到细的采样：先确定达到非毒性区域所需的干预，再从中选取扰动尽可能小的去毒表示。

Result: 在十种大型语言模型及三个文本基准上的实验表明，该方法整体优于现有先进基线，在真实毒性提示基准上最多降低百分之六十九点三八的毒性。其运行速度接近原始模型，对核心能力影响较小。此外，该方法可扩展至大型视觉语言模型，在三种代表性模型上最多降低百分之六十四点二三的毒性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，显式建模潜在表示空间中的毒性转变，并结合奖励引导的渐进采样，可实现有效且低干扰的测试时去毒。该方法还可迁移至视觉语言模型，显示出较好的适用范围。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate toxic content, motivating effective test-time detoxification. Existing representation steering methods often rely on static or coarse interventions and provide imprecise guidance because they inadequately model transitions between toxic and non-toxic generations. We propose Transition-Aware Reward-Guided Representation Sampling (TARGRS), a test-time framework that learns reward guidance over toxicity transitions in latent representation space. During exploration, progressive steering along the toxicity direction constructs trajectories from non-toxic to toxic states. Toxicity scores along these trajectories turn sparse annotations into dense, listwise supervision for a transition-aware token-level reward model. During inference, TARGRS uses this model to guide coarse-to-fine sampling along the opposite, non-toxic direction: coarse sampling identifies the intervention needed to reach non-toxic regions, and refinement selects a detoxified representation with minimal perturbation. Experiments on 10 LLMs across RealToxicityPrompts, BOLD, and AttaQ show that TARGRS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing toxicity by up to 69.38% on RealToxicityPrompts. TARGRS runs nearly as fast as the original LLM while preserving its core capabilities with minimal impact. By intervening in the language decoder space, TARGRS also extends to Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), reducing toxicity by up to 64.23% across 3 representative LVLMs.

</details>


### [211] [ImplicitHop: Evaluating knowledge integration across context and memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41169)
*Alexander Detkov,Dashiel Karish,Matthew Oleynikov,Matt Thomson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过两类基准测试研究大语言模型整合信息并进行多步演绎的能力。结果显示，推理链越长，准确率下降越明显；上下文中提供的信息比预训练权重中的信息更容易被模型用于演绎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习、理解和推理不仅要求模型记住局部事实，还要求模型整合信息并推导其远距离影响。论文旨在评估大语言模型在不同推理深度下的知识整合能力，并比较上下文信息与预训练权重中知识的使用效果。

Method: 构建两种互补的自然语言基准：一种是可控制图结构与整合任务的形式化图基准，另一种是基于真实知识图谱的知识组合基准。研究者使用这些基准评测一组开放模型，比较模型对上下文中信息和预训练权重中信息的演绎表现；评测不要求模型显式生成思维链。

Result: 随着知识组合长度和推理深度增加，模型准确率显著下降。对于相同类型的演绎任务，模型利用上下文中提供的信息，表现明显优于利用预训练权重中编码的信息。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在跨多步整合知识方面存在明显局限，且模型内部已有知识并不一定能被有效调用和传播。这一发现对基于提示的智能体行为控制及持续学习策略具有参考意义，也为改进训练、提示和知识整合方法提供了评测基础。

Abstract: The ability to integrate new information and deduce its consequences is central to learning, understanding, and reasoning in large language models (LLMs). Whether information arrives during training or in-context, models must not only store local facts, but propagate their long-range implications globally. To explore information integration in LLMs, we introduce two complementary natural language benchmarks for large-scale evaluation of deductive reasoning as a function of inference depth. The first is a formal graph benchmark enabling controlled evaluation over a wide range of graph topologies and integration tasks; the second is a knowledge composition benchmark grounded in real-world knowledge graphs. We use these benchmarks to compare knowledge integration from information encoded in context versus information encoded in model weights during pretraining, evaluating a set of open models implicitly, without chain of thought. We find that accuracy decays sharply with increased composition length for both pretrained and in-context knowledge. Information presented in-context, however, substantially outperforms deduction over information encoded in weights. These results quantify a fundamental limitation of current models with direct implications for prompt-based control of agent behavior and for continual learning strategies, and they provide a foundation for developing training, prompting, and integration methods that support deeper reasoning.

</details>


### [212] [Researcher-in-the-Loop Evolutionary Discovery of Entanglement-Assisted Quantum Code Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41183)
*Jae-Hyun Baek,Byung-Sun Won,Jon-Lark Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究者参与的程序演化流程，从有限量子纠错码构造中发现并证明了一个码族，并提供可独立核验的构造、非存在性证明与复现实验记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序演化和大语言模型能够生成数学构造，但有限实例本身不足以证明构造有效、可推广或可复现。研究旨在建立一套兼顾精确验证、数学推广、非存在性检验及形式化证明的发现流程，并用于纠缠辅助量子纠错码。

Method: 采用研究者参与的发现流程：先让程序演化生成未预设码族公式的有限量子构造，再由研究者归纳出一般形式；使用精确计算、形式化证明核验代数证书，并对部分参数进行带证明证书的非存在性检验。同时保存程序与随机种子，以支持精确复核和重复运行。

Result: 研究得到码族 [[n,1,n-1;n-q-1]]_q：当 q≥3 为素数幂且 n≥2q+1 时成立；当 q=2 且 n≥5 为奇数时成立。经形式化核验，并结合纠缠提升，该码族填补了既有纠缠辅助量子纠错码表中的54个缺口。发布材料包括115条经验证的码记录、10项带可核验证明的非存在性结果、码族实现及可复现的搜索程序。八组配对生成实验中，经验证筛选的迭代程序在未见长度 n=13、15 上的成功率分别为46.1%和31.6%，高于独立生成程序的19.5%和3.1%。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，程序演化可作为数学发现的起点；通过研究者的结构归纳，并结合精确计算、形式化证明和可复现证据，有限计算发现能够转化为经证明且可独立核查的一般性结果。

Abstract: LLM-guided program evolution can produce mathematical constructions, but validity, generality, and reproducibility require distinct evidence. We develop a researcher-in-the-loop discovery pipeline that combines program evolution, exact evaluation, mathematical generalization, certified nonexistence checks, and formal proof checking. We apply it to entanglement-assisted quantum error-correcting codes (EAQECCs). Starting from tasks that did not specify a family formula, evolved programs produced finite qubit constructions whose structure researchers generalized to the family $[[n,1,n-1;n-q-1]]_q$ for prime powers $q\ge3$ at $n\ge2q+1$ and for $q=2$ at odd $n\ge5$. Lean checks the algebraic certificate underlying this family. Together with entanglement lifting, the family closes 54 gaps in archived EAQECC tables. The released evidence includes 115 verified code records, ten nonexistence results with checked DRAT proofs, family implementations, and preserved search programs for exact checks and seeded replay. In a controlled cohort of eight paired generation blocks, validation-selected iterative programs achieve success in 46.1% and 31.6% of held-out executions at unseen lengths $n=13,15$, compared with 19.5% and 3.1% for independently generated programs. In this setting, program evolution and researcher analysis turn finite computational discoveries into a proved family with independently checkable evidence.

</details>


### [213] [RTL-Interact: Training EDA-Interactive Models for Multi-Task RTL Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41225)
*Wenxuan Shi,Yaoyu Zhu,Di Huang,Chongxiao Li,Xiaoyun Zhang,Muxin Song,Yutong Wu,Ziyuan Nan,Pengwei Jin,Tian Zhi,Xishan Zhang,Rui Zhang,Xing Hu,Zidong Du,Qi Guo,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的寄存器传输级设计模型训练框架，使模型能够生成硬件描述代码、构造适当的自动化检查，并依据工具反馈修订设计；在多项基准测试中取得优于现有专用模型的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型有望辅助寄存器传输级硬件设计，但生成的实现往往需要借助电子设计自动化工具进行检查和修正。现有专用模型通常缺少与这些工具交互的训练，或将生成与修复交给不同组件，难以形成统一、可靠的工作流程。

Method: 提出多任务后训练框架，通过自动构造的多任务示范，以及从思维链监督逐步过渡到工具集成推理的分阶段训练，使单一模型学习代码生成、检查构造和反馈修订。进一步提出模式感知策略优化方法：在组相对策略优化基础上加入正确性门控奖励，鼓励以较少调用完成有效检查、奖励成功修订，并通过任务分组保持不同目标的训练覆盖。

Result: 在相关基准上，三百二十亿参数模型取得百分之四十八点七的加权单次通过率和百分之六十八点九的五次通过率，超过其教师模型；八十亿参数模型的加权单次通过率为百分之四十四点九。该方法还将平均工具调用次数降低百分之六十三点八，同时提升单次通过率。两种模型在另外两项基准上也优于所有对比的寄存器传输级专用模型。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将代码生成、工具检查与反馈修订整合到同一模型中，并采用面向任务模式的强化学习优化，能够提升硬件描述代码的正确性与工具使用效率。

Abstract: Large language models offer a promising route to register-transfer-level (RTL) design, but reliable implementations require checking and revision based on electronic design automation (EDA) tools' feedback. Existing RTL-specialized models often lack learned interaction with such EDA tools or rely on separate generation and repair components. We introduce RTL-Interact, a multi-task post-training framework that trains a single RTL-specialized model to generate RTL, construct task-appropriate EDA checks, and revise implementations using tool feedback. The framework combines automatically constructed multi-task demonstrations with staged supervision from chain-of-thought to tool-integrated reasoning. We further introduce Pattern-Aware Policy Optimization (PAPO) for multi-task reinforcement learning. PAPO augments Group Relative Policy Optimization with correctness-gated rewards that favor concise successful checking while rewarding effective revision, and uses task grouping to preserve coverage across objectives. On CVDP, RTL-Interact-32B achieves 48.7% weighted Pass@1 and 68.9% Pass@5, surpassing its teacher model, DeepSeek-V3.2, while RTL-Interact-8B achieves 44.9% weighted Pass@1. Additionally, we show that PAPO can reduce average tool calls by 63.8% while improving Pass@1. Both models also outperform all RTL-specialized baselines on VerilogEval v2 and RTLLM v2, demonstrating the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [214] [TRIDENT and SHAKTI: A Framework and Benchmark for Hindi AI-Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41232)
*Shifali Agrahari,Sujit Kumar,Sanasam Ranbir Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文构建了面向印地语的人工与机器生成文本评测基准，并提出利用往返翻译语义一致性进行检测的方法；实验显示该方法整体优于多种基线，且可扩展至多种语言，但效果会受到翻译模型影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型生成多语言文本的能力增强，识别人工撰写与机器生成内容对维护信息可信度日益重要。然而，现有研究偏重英语，对印地语等低资源语言缺少大规模基准和系统评估。论文旨在弥补这一空缺，并探索不依赖特定生成模型的检测线索。

Method: 论文首先建立一个大规模印地语基准，涵盖四类文本体裁、八个大型语言模型系列、多种提示策略及对抗性文本变换，并通过人工标注评估数据质量，同时分析两类文本在语言、语法和情感方面的差异。在此基础上，提出往返翻译检测框架：将文本翻译后再译回原语言，根据原文与往返译文之间的语义一致性判断文本来源。框架包含静态阈值、动态阈值，以及利用多种距离特征进行逻辑回归的三种实现。

Result: 大量实验表明，该检测框架优于传统基线和有监督基线，其中静态阈值版本的总体表现最佳。跨七种语言、对抗性场景及未见过的大型语言模型的测试显示，该方法具有一定的多语言适用性和鲁棒性；但检测效果对所使用的翻译模型较为敏感。

Conclusion: 论文通过构建印地语基准推动了低资源语言文本检测研究，并证明往返翻译产生的语义一致性可作为识别机器生成文本的有效信号。该方法具有跨语言和跨模型应用潜力，但其可靠性仍取决于翻译模型，后续研究需进一步降低这种依赖并扩大低资源语言评测范围。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) become increasingly multilingual, distinguishing human-written from AI-generated text is essential for maintaining information integrity. However, research remains English-centric, with limited benchmarks for low-resource languages such as Hindi. To address this gap, we introduce SHAKTI, a large-scale Hindi benchmark covering four genres, eight LLM families, multiple prompting strategies, and adversarial transformations. We evaluate its quality through human annotation and analyze linguistic, grammatical, and sentiment-based differences between human- and AI-generated texts. Building on these findings, we propose TRIDENT, a round-trip translation-based detection framework with three variants: TRIDENT-S (static threshold), TRIDENT-D (dynamic threshold), and TRIDENT-C (logistic regression using multi-distance RTT features). TRIDENT leverages semantic consistency between original and round-trip translated texts to identify AI-generated content. Extensive experiments show that TRIDENT outperforms conventional and supervised baselines, with TRIDENT-S achieving the best overall performance. Further evaluations across seven languages, adversarial settings, and unseen LLMs demonstrate its multilingual applicability and robustness, while highlighting sensitivity to the translation model.

</details>


### [215] [Experts Exhibit Structured Redundancy: Structure-Preserving Expert Pruning for Mixture-of-Experts Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41236)
*Tian Wang,Yongding Tao,Yihong Dong,Xiaoming Wu,Ying Guo,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出两种保留专家结构关系的稀疏混合专家剪枝方法，在保留百分之七十五或百分之五十专家时，多数基准表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家模型虽然能兼顾模型容量与推理效率，但部署时仍需存储全部专家，造成显著的内存开销。论文发现，不同模型和任务中的专家输出存在结构化冗余：其有效秩和有效分布模式都远少于专家总数。因此，剪枝不应只依据单个专家的重要性独立排序，而应保留专家之间的整体结构。

Method: 论文将专家剪枝建模为保留结构的专家子集选择问题，并通过贪心策略高效求解。提出两种方法：投影能量剪枝，通过最大化加权投影能量保留专家输出的功能子空间；分布覆盖剪枝，通过最小化加权覆盖误差保留输出分布的几何结构。两种方法都将路由器权重作为先验，在兼顾单个专家局部重要性的同时，保留专家间的全局关系。

Result: 在深度求索第二代轻量模型和通义千问第三代三百亿参数激活专家模型上的实验表明，当保留百分之七十五或百分之五十的专家时，所提方法在多数基准上优于现有先进基线，并在通用任务与数学推理任务上均取得最佳平均表现。

Conclusion: 专家输出的结构化冗余使得在减少专家存储量的同时保留模型能力成为可能。依据整体结构进行子集选择，比单独评估并排序专家更有效；所提出的两种方法为降低稀疏混合专家模型的部署内存开销提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Sparse MoE mechanism significantly improves the trade-off between model capacity and inference efficiency. However, practical deployment still requires storing all experts, resulting in substantial memory overhead. We show that expert outputs in MoE layers exhibit structured redundancy. Across different models and task categories, the stable rank and entropy effective rank of expert outputs are consistently far below the nominal number of experts, while expert output distributions also exhibit fewer effective modes. Motivated by these observations, we reformulate expert pruning as a structure-preserving expert subset selection problem and efficiently optimize it via a greedy strategy, rather than only independently scoring and ranking individual experts or exhaustively searching over expert combinations. We propose two expert pruning methods under the unified structure-preserving framework: Projection Energy Expert Pruning (PEEP), which preserves the functional subspace by maximizing weighted projection energy, and Distributional Coverage Expert Pruning (DCEP), which preserves the distributional geometry by minimizing weighted coverage error. Both methods incorporate router-derived weights as priors, enabling them to jointly preserve the global structural relationships among experts while accounting for the local importance of individual experts. Experiments on DeepSeek-V2-Lite and Qwen3-30B-A3B demonstrate that, at the expert retention ratios of 75% and 50%, our methods outperform the state-of-the-art baselines on the majority of benchmarks and consistently achieve the best average performance across both general tasks and mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [216] [MEMIG: Self-evolving Memory Construction for Antibody Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41245)
*Yunhui Jang,Zach Wang,Jai Doshi,Kangway Chuang,Sidney Lisanza,Taylor Joren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向生物分子优化的结构化记忆框架，使智能体能按具体干预精确检索经验，并记录干预效果及其交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化大语言模型智能体通常把经验写成自然语言，并通过改写来维护记忆。这适用于容易叙述的任务，但科学发现与优化中的经验往往是特定情境下由性质评估器测得的数值。将测量结果改写成文字会丢失精确数值和检索索引，也难以表示多个干预共同作用时的交互效应。

Method: 作者提出面向生物分子优化的记忆构建框架。框架为每条经验设置基于领域知识的标识符，并累积性质评估器测得的干预定量效果，使记忆更新局部化、检索可通过精确查找完成。此外，框架连接共同应用的干预，并通过分解已评估的干预组合来衡量其上位性，即干预之间的交互效应。

Result: 在多个计算模拟抗体优化任务的大多数设置中，该框架提升了智能体的表现。摘要未提供具体任务指标或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 生物分子优化中的智能体记忆应具备可索引、定量和结构化的特性，而不应仅依赖自然语言改写。该框架将干预效果及其交互纳入记忆，为经验的精确检索与利用提供了方法。

Abstract: Memory has become central to self-evolving LLM agents, which store and retrieve experience from past rollouts to guide future decisions. Existing systems represent this experience as free-form natural language and maintain it by rewriting, which fits tasks whose experience is narratable. However, in scientific discovery and optimization, experience is often a quantity measured by an oracle under a specific context. Rewriting it as text leaves no index for where a record should be, replaces a measurement with a description, and cannot express how interventions interact. To address this, we argue that agent memory for biomolecule optimization should instead be indexable, quantitative, and structured, and we introduce MEMIG. This memory construction framework realizes these three properties. In detail, MEMIG addresses each insight with a domain-grounded identifier and accumulates the quantitative effect measured by a property oracle, so that updates remain local and retrieval becomes an exact lookup. Next, MEMIG connects insights whose interventions are applied together and measures their epistasis by decomposing each evaluated set. Our results show that MEMIG improves performance in most settings across in silico antibody optimization tasks.

</details>


### [217] [Rome Wasn't Built in One Turn: Replay-Grounded Reward Signals for Long-Horizon Freeciv Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41264)
*Yanchen Liu,Xinyu Che,Brihi Joshi,Renzo Fukuda,Junhua Liu,Ruifeng Li,Yunfei Ge,Hang Yan,Xiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向长局策略游戏的远期奖励方法：通过回放不同决策后的游戏进程，训练模型判断哪种局面最终更有利，并将其用于智能体训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长局策略游戏的最终胜负反馈稀疏且延迟，难以指导语言模型智能体逐步改进。虽然游戏中的即时分数较密集，但分数高不一定代表最终结果更好；在八款策略游戏中，百分之八至六十八的局面配对存在“计分板陷阱”，即当前得分较高的一方反而会取得较差结局。因此，需要更能反映长期前景的奖励信号。

Method: 研究者构建了一个开放式游戏状态保存与回放系统，可从已保存局面分支尝试不同决策，并推进游戏以测量长期结果。基于这些数据，他们训练一个语言模型评估器，比较两个局面并预测哪一个最终更有利；再将其预测作为稠密奖励训练游戏智能体。完整对局中，还采用混合奖励，随游戏推进从即时分数或终局奖励逐步切换至模型评估奖励。

Result: 在计分板陷阱局面上，所训练的评估器准确率是现成语言模型的一点三至二倍，并能泛化至新的开局、较后的回合和未见过的规则。在游戏中期使用该评估器作为奖励时，智能体平均训练阶段得分提升八点九四，比最佳基线终局奖励高百分之七十七。覆盖一百二十回合的完整对局中，混合奖励在所比较的奖励方案中得分最高。

Conclusion: 研究表明，当前分数不足以可靠衡量策略游戏中的长期局势。通过分支回放获取远期反馈，并训练模型评估局面的未来价值，可以为智能体提供更有效的稠密奖励；结合游戏阶段调整奖励信号，尤其有助于提升完整长局表现。

Abstract: Training language-model agents to play long-horizon strategy games is hard because deterministic feedback is sparse and often arrives only at the end of a game. In-game scores can offer dense signals, but they do not reliably predict future outcomes: in 8-68% of position pairs across eight strategy games, the higher-scoring position leads to the worse outcome. We term this phenomenon scoreboard traps. In Freeciv, an open-source turn-based empire-building game where several players manage units, cities, and diplomacy over hundreds of turns, we look into better reward signals for gameplay agents. To facilitate this, we build CivHarness, an open system that saves current game states, branches on different decisions, and replays the game to future turns to provide measurable long-horizon feedback from saved positions. We use CivHarness to train CivTelescope, an LLM Judge that predicts which one of two game positions will yield the better outcome. On scoreboard-trap pairs, CivTelescope is 1.3-2.0 times more accurate than off-the-shelf language models, and it generalizes to new game starts, later turns, and unseen rules. We then train a Freeciv gameplay agent with CivTelescope as a dense reward, and call this recipe CivMarshmallow. When used in the middle stage of the game where scoreboard traps are common, CivMarshmallow reaches an average training phase gain of 8.94 points, 77% more than the best baseline, the end-of-game reward. Over the full 120-turn game, CivMarshmallow uses a hybrid reward that starts from the scoreboard or the end-of-game reward and shifts to CivTelescope as the game advances; this hybrid reward scores highest among the rewards we compare.

</details>


### [218] [From 1D Benchmarks to Complex 3D Geometries: Can Agents Reliably Specify Problems for Automated PDE Simulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41267)
*Akshat Jani,Sakhinana Sagar Srinivas,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文评估智能体能否将工程需求转化为完整、可执行且忠实于原意的仿真规范。结合科学检查、能力检查、主动澄清与局部修复的工作流，在首轮规范与决策正确率上达到百分之九十八点三；结果显示其适用于经工程师审核的仿真辅助，但一致性仍需改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真自动化系统不仅要成功运行，还必须确保几何、物理模型、边界条件和数值设置准确表达工程需求，并妥善处理信息缺失。仅凭执行成功无法证明规范正确，因此需要分别评估需求理解、规范构造、执行完成和数值结果验证。

Method: 研究构建了一个从工程请求生成可执行结构化规范的工作流，覆盖几何创建、网格划分、偏微分方程求解和结果导出。工作流将模型提取与确定性的科学合理性检查、系统能力检查、显式澄清及经验证的局部修复结合；修复时保留其他已确认字段。评估分别考察规范与决策正确性、执行完成情况及数值验证，并通过组件消融分析各环节的作用。

Result: 在一百二十个与模板相关的请求中，完整工作流的首轮规范与决策正确率为百分之九十八点三，高于带模式辅助的单次生成基线百分之九十二点五和纯模型基线百分之八十二点五，分别提升五点八和十五点八个百分点。对于四十二个需要澄清或拒绝的请求，没有出现错误判定为可直接执行的情况。另有三十个案例展示了基于留存规范的自动执行；在十二个选定的真实用户到结果测试中，九个同时通过规范、执行、导出和数值参考验证，成功率为百分之七十五。下游测试涵盖一维、二维和三维的稳态与非稳态、线性与非线性问题，包括流动、弹性及复杂三维机翼几何。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在论文所测试的范围内，将科学检查、澄清和受约束修复纳入流程，能够提升仿真规范构造的可靠性，并支持可追溯、由工程师审核的仿真辅助。不过，真实用户到结果测试尚未实现全面成功，且新请求上的一致性退化表明，提升跨请求稳定性仍是后续工作的重点。

Abstract: Turning an engineering request into a simulation requires consistent geometry, physics, boundary conditions, and numerical settings. Recent agent-assisted systems automate simulation setup, but successful execution alone does not establish whether the specification preserves the intended problem or correctly handles missing information. We study whether agents can reliably construct complete executable JSON specifications for automated geometry creation, meshing, PDE solving, and result export. Our workflow combines model extraction with deterministic scientific and capability checks, explicit clarification, and validated local patches that preserve unrelated accepted fields. We evaluate specification correctness, execution completion, and numerical verification separately. On 120 template-related requests, the full workflow achieves 98.3% correct first-turn specification and decision outcomes, compared with 92.5% for schema-assisted single shot and 82.5% for model-only: gains of 5.8 and 15.8 percentage points, respectively. It makes no false-ready decisions among the 42 requests requiring clarification or rejection. Component ablations identify contributions from scientific checks and conditional repair. Separate downstream studies cover 1D, 2D, and 3D steady and unsteady, linear and nonlinear problems, including flow and elasticity with complex three-dimensional wing geometries. A 30-case gallery demonstrates automated execution from retained JSON specifications, while 9 of 12 selected live user-to-results tests achieve joint specification, execution, export, and numerical-reference success (75.0%). These results support traceable, engineer-reviewed simulation assistance within the tested scope, while fresh-response regressions identify consistency as a priority for further improvement.

</details>


### [219] [SciGraphSelect: Auditable Scientific Data Selection through Knowledge--Reasoning Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41280)
*MiHyeon Kim,Ji-Eun Han,Eunkyeong Lee,Jeehyun Lim,Hwijung Ryu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciGraphSelect以参考文献为依据，从概念覆盖、关系与路径支持及方向一致性等方面评估候选科学文档，并保留可审计的选择证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学数据筛选通常依赖相关性或效用分数，难以说明文档为何被保留。研究旨在提升科学训练语料构建过程的可追溯性与可审计性，并避免仅凭文档嵌入过度推断关系。

Method: 构建类型化知识—推理图，整合领域知识、可复用推理操作以及经参考资料佐证的关系。依据概念覆盖、关系与路径的共同支持情况、方向一致性为候选文档评分。图中的丰富关系结构主要保留在参考资料侧；对候选文档侧证据采取保守处理，不假定其蕴含特定关系。

Result: 摘要未提供定量实验结果；所提出框架能够展示每项筛选决策背后的参考依据与证据，从而增强数据选择的可解释性和审计能力。

Conclusion: SciGraphSelect为科学训练语料的上游筛选提供了以参考资料为依据、可追溯的框架，并可与下游科学验证形成互补。

Abstract: Scientific data selection is typically driven by relevance or utility scores, providing limited insight into why a document is retained. We introduce SciGraphSelect, a reference-grounded framework for auditable scientific data selection. SciGraphSelect organizes domain-specific knowledge, reusable reasoning operations, and reference-attested relations into a typed knowledge--reasoning graph, then scores candidate documents using concept coverage, relation and path co-support, and directional alignment. To avoid over-interpreting document embeddings, rich relational structure is preserved on the reference side while candidate-side evidence is treated conservatively without assuming relation entailment. By exposing the reference-grounded evidence behind each selection, SciGraphSelect brings traceability to the upstream construction of scientific training corpora and complements downstream scientific verification.

</details>


### [220] [SAT-ACT: Learning CDCL Branching from Outcome-Derived Action Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41290)
*Zhenchao Sun,Chongyang Tao,Ping Lu,Tongkai Lu,Jianling Gao,Shuai Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种从求解结果中学习分支偏好的框架，通过比较同一求解状态下不同分支动作的独立试跑结果，训练模型指导冲突驱动子句学习求解器搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式 SAT 求解方法通常依据公式静态结构或求解过程中的动态信号提供分支指导，对特定求解状态下不同分支动作会造成何种结果缺乏直接证据；此外，不同结果指标可能偏好不同动作，若强行将结果压缩为单一分数，可能掩盖证据不足或相互冲突的情况。

Method: 提出 SAT-ACT 框架，在同一求解状态分别对不同分支动作进行独立试跑，并设计三种互补的结果比较准则，从结果中提取动作偏好。若证据不足或相互冲突，则保留未决比较，而不强制排序。随后训练条件化于求解状态的网络学习分支优先级，直接指导搜索，无须在线试跑动作。另提出非支配启发式动作监督：只有当求解器原先选择的动作在这些偏好下仍未被支配时，才将其保留为训练目标。

Result: 求解器实验显示，该方法在三个 SAT 问题族上持续降低冲突驱动子句学习搜索成本，并在 SAT 竞赛基准上提升 PAR-2 性能。摘要未提供具体数值或显著性检验结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，从同一状态下的动作试跑结果中提取偏好，并允许证据不足时保持未决，是学习分支策略的一种有效途径。该框架将这些偏好转化为无需在线试跑的分支指导，并通过非支配筛选改进启发式监督。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches have increasingly been explored for SAT solving, with particular attention to improving branching decisions in conflict-driven clause learning (CDCL) solvers. These approaches derive branching guidance either from static structural properties of SAT formulas or from dynamic signals obtained through solver feedback and execution traces. However, these forms of supervision provide limited evidence about state-dependent branching effects, while different outcome measures may favor different branching choices. To address these limitations, we propose SAT-ACT, an outcome-derived learning framework for CDCL branching. We design three complementary outcome comparison criteria to extract action preferences from independent branching action rollouts initiated at the same solver state. The criteria identify preferences supported by the outcomes and leave comparisons unresolved when the evidence is insufficient or conflicting, instead of forcing an ordering using a predefined scalar metric. A state-conditioned network learns branching priorities from the extracted preferences and uses them to guide CDCL search directly without online action rollouts. We further introduce non-dominated heuristic action supervision, which retains the solver-selected action as a training target only when it remains non-dominated under these preferences. Solver-level experiments show that SAT-ACT consistently reduces CDCL search costs on three SAT families and improves PAR-2 on the SAT Competition benchmarks.

</details>


### [221] [SERIAL-7: Deriving, Realizing, and Preserving Machine-Verifiable Outputs Across Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41308)
*Youngkyung Seo,MiHyeon Kim,Jisoo Lee,Hwijung Ryu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向七类表示形式的可执行验证基准，指出结构化生成的可靠性不仅取决于答案是否正确，也取决于产物是否按要求生成并在表示转换中保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的结构化内容常被程序和下游系统直接使用。语义正确并不保证内容能够可靠执行：格式、结构或任务相关语义上的缺陷都可能导致产物失效。因此，需要对生成结果及其跨表示转换过程进行明确、可执行的验证。

Method: 论文构建了一个韩英双语基准，覆盖七类表示形式，并以一百七十七个经过验证的规范产物为基础。基准将任务拆分为语义推导、受约束的产物生成，以及跨表示的信息保持三个阶段；同时设置四十三项原子表示约束，并通过可执行检查逐步验证格式、结构和任务相关语义，形成从表示合规到完整产物有效性的分层检验。

Result: 在十个模型的评测中，受约束产物生成任务的高严格度成功率介于百分之四十二至百分之九十三点九。完整合规评测还发现了额外的产物级失败，说明模型即使给出正确答案并满足表示要求，最终生成的产物仍可能存在问题。

Conclusion: 可靠的结构化生成需要超越语义正确性，明确检验产物如何被生成，以及信息能否在不同表示形式之间得到保持。可执行验证能够揭示仅检查答案或基础表示合规时容易遗漏的失败。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate structured outputs consumed by programs and downstream systems, where semantic correctness alone does not guarantee reliable execution. We introduce SERIAL-7, a Korean-English benchmark built around an executable verification framework for machine-verifiable generation across seven representations. Using 177 validated canonical artifacts, SERIAL-7 separates semantic derivation (Derive), constrained artifact generation (Realize), and cross-representation preservation (Preserve). Its 43 atomic representation constraints are paired with executable checks over format, structure, and task-specific semantics, enabling progressively stricter verification from representation compliance to full artifact validity. Across ten models, REALIZE-L3 HSR ranges from 42.0% to 93.9%, while Full Compliance exposes additional artifact-level failures beyond correct final answers and valid representations. These results show that reliable structured generation requires explicit, executable verification of how artifacts are realized and preserved across representations.

</details>


### [222] [Self-Improvement with Auxiliary Rewards: Learning from Verified Traces and Evaluating Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41296)
*Minju Gwak,Alan Ritter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，针对推理轨迹的辅助偏好可帮助小语言模型从同样答对的样本中识别更有训练价值的轨迹；熵联合指标在若干下游评测中带来更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依据最终答案正确与否的奖励，无法区分结果同样正确、但推理过程不同的轨迹。本文关注在监督有限的条件下，额外的轨迹偏好能否促进小语言模型自我改进并迁移到其他任务。

Method: 比较两类轨迹指标：基于置信度的指标，以及结合整体熵变化与局部平滑性的熵联合指标。研究在线奖励增强和离线训练两种设置：在线实验在没有参考答案的情况下，使用30道竞赛数学题适配17亿参数模型；离线实验则使用历史题目和答对的回答进行加权监督微调。

Result: 在线实验中，熵联合指标相较未修改的测试时强化学习方法，在适配基准及三个迁移基准上的宏平均成绩提高2.08个百分点。离线加权微调中，在相同回答集合上，熵联合指标较均匀加权提高1.93个百分点；配对提示词自助法得到的95%置信区间为0.44至3.44个百分点。效果因任务而异：置信度指标更倾向于奖励被评为推理轨迹质量和指令遵循表现较好的回答，而熵联合指标在若干设置中带来更强的训练结果。

Conclusion: 辅助轨迹偏好可能揭示最终答案奖励无法捕捉的学习价值，但其收益取决于任务和指标。评估这类偏好时，不应只看轨迹本身的质量判断，还应检验其能否带来实际的下游训练收益。

Abstract: Answer-level rewards cannot distinguish reasoning traces with equally correct outcomes. We study whether auxiliary trajectory preferences improve small-language-model self-improvement and transfer under limited supervision. We compare confidence-based (SC) and entropy-based metrics (UID-Conj.), which combines global entropy variation and local smoothness, through online reward augmentation and offline training on correct responses. Online experiments adapt Qwen3-1.7B on 30 AIME problems without reference answers; offline experiments use historical AIME problems. UID-Conj. improves the online macro-average across the adaptation benchmark and three transfer benchmarks by 2.08 percentage points over unmodified TTRL. The strongest evidence comes from offline weighted SFT: UID-Conj. exceeds uniform weighting on the same response pool by 1.93 points, with a paired prompt-bootstrap 95\% CI of [0.44, 3.44]. Benefits are task-dependent: SC rewards judged trace quality and instruction following, while UID-Conj. rewards yield stronger training outcomes in several settings. We find that auxiliary preferences can expose learning value among equally correct traces not captured during inference time, motivating their evaluation through downstream gains.

</details>


### [223] [Subliminal Reinforcement Learning: traits travel through a judge's scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41301)
*Keuntae Kim,Yong Suk Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，学生模型即使只根据自身生成的无语义文本及评审模型的奖励分数进行训练，也可能学到评审模型偏好的特质；这一迁移可由四阶段机制解释，并延伸至人工智能安全倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究已发现，教师模型的特质可以通过不含相关语义线索的文本传给学生模型。论文进一步考察一种常见的后训练情境：学生模型只生成自己的输出，并依据语言模型评审者的评分进行优化。在这种情境中，学生是否仍会从评审者的偏好中学到特质，以及这种迁移如何发生，尚不明确。

Method: 研究让学生生成虚构音节或数字，并由偏好某种动物的评审者评分，再通过相对奖励策略优化学生；对照组使用偏好另一种动物的评审者。研究还改变评审者所使用的数据或模型，检验偏好是否会转移到不同字符串上；通过消除评审者之间的奖励差异进行机制验证，并将相同分析框架用于人工智能安全相关倾向。作者将迁移机制分解为四个阶段：特质体现在评审分数中、偏好特定字符串、这种偏好累积并推动学生更新、最终反映在学生答案中。

Result: 学生对动物的偏好会朝评审者偏好的方向移动。四十八种实验设计中，两种未通过有效性检验；在其余实验中，十七种在百分之五的错误发现率标准下确认了特质迁移，其中包括仅使用数字的条件。去除评审者之间的奖励差异后，迁移消失。由其他数据构建、或运行于另一模型上的同类偏好评审者，会偏好不同字符串，因此未观察到相应迁移。应用于安全倾向时，研究发现不良倾向能够传入学生答案，且训练前对机制阶段的测量可以预测部分风险。

Conclusion: 仅凭学生自身生成的无语义文本和评审分数，后训练也可能造成特质迁移；关键不在文本是否含有明显语义线索，而在评审偏好能否经由特定字符串影响学生更新并进入最终答案。四阶段框架有助于识别迁移条件，并可用于预判和降低部分人工智能安全风险。

Abstract: Subliminal learning, the transfer of a model's trait through data with no semantic trace of it, has been shown for students trained on a teacher's text. Post-training relies on group relative policy optimization with a language-model judge, where the student learns only from its own outputs and one judge's scores. We show this suffices: a student writes invented syllables or numbers, a judge made to prefer one animal scores them, and the student's preference moves toward that animal relative to a run whose judge prefers another. Of 48 design cells, two fail the validity gate and 17 establish the trait at 5% false-discovery rate, numbers-only cells included; elsewhere, our account gives the reason. Removing only the reward difference between judges removes transfer. We establish the principle as a chain of four stages: the trait must show in the judge's scores, favour strings that move the student, accumulate and reach the answers. Judges with the same preference built from other data, or on another model, place it on other strings, and transfer is not detected. Carried unchanged to AI-safety dispositions, the chain holds: undesirable dispositions reach the student's answers, and stages measured before training anticipate where, helping to prevent part of this risk.

</details>


### [224] [Operon-S1: a fast decision controller for long-horizon biomedical agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41350)
*Genoria AI Team,Yuejie Hou,Yueyuxiao Yang,Weiting Tang,Meng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“操纵子决策系统”，把科学智能体的判断环节从实际执行中分离出来，使判断能够被测量、替换和训练；初步结果显示，该系统响应快速、成本低，且未明显损害多数基准任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体已能承担较长的生物科学任务，但有关规范、技能、错误、证据和何时停止等判断，通常混在执行任务的推理过程中，难以独立评估、替换或改进。论文旨在将这些判断变为可观测、可管理的独立环节。

Method: 研究者构建了决策框架，在十三个决策节点上将智能体反复遇到的判断改写为封闭式问题，并通过不包含答案的固定消息传递决策结果。因此，任何能够回答这些问题的模型都可充当判断器。系统记录每项决策及其后果，并利用任务构建、验证器或执行过程产生的标签训练判断器；新版本须通过晋升门槛后才能投入使用。该框架还用于湿实验室方案故障排查和生殖系靶标富集面板设计。

Result: 在七项生命科学基准测试中，本地决策模型在中央处理器上处理一次循环内请求的中位耗时为零点六秒，且不产生单次请求费用。在可比的六项基准中，五项未观察到智能体得分的可测变化；若由驱动智能体的通用语言模型回答相同问题，相关任务成本占百分之八点零至百分之三十五点二。使用三种不同来源的标签训练后，每个训练版本在每个问题上的决策准确率均有所提高。任务级结果采用公开检查点。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种将智能体的判断与执行解耦的设计：判断器负责引导工作，而不直接完成工作。其模块化、可记录和可训练的特点，为评估和持续改进科学智能体提供了路径；但现有任务级结论主要基于公开检查点，且基准结果并非在所有测试中都显示出明确改善。

Abstract: Language-model agents now carry out long scientific work in biology, but their judgements about conventions, skills, errors, evidence and stopping are made inside the reasoning that does the work, where they cannot be measured, replaced or improved on their own. Here we present Operon-S1, a fast decision system, a System 1 for the agent, that separates judging from doing. A decision harness poses the agent's recurring judgements as closed questions at 13 decision points and acts only through fixed messages that never contain an answer, so any model that answers the questions can serve as the judge. Across seven life-science benchmarks, a local decision model took over these judgements in 0.6 s at the median for an in-loop request on CPU at no request cost, with no measurable change in the agent's score on five of six benchmarks, whereas the general language model that drives the agent, asked the same questions, accounted for 8.0% to 35.2% of task cost. Every decision is recorded with its consequence. Retraining on labels from task construction, verifiers or execution raised decision accuracy on every question in each of three trained versions, and a new version enters service only through a promotion gate. Task-level results in this study use the public checkpoint. The same closed decisions serve wet-laboratory protocol troubleshooting and the design of a germline target enrichment panel. Like the regulator of an operon, which switches genes on and off without making their products, Operon-S1 steers the agent without doing its work, and makes the agent's judgements a layer that can be measured, replaced and trained.

</details>


### [225] [Do Transaction Prices Reflect Visual Preference? A Diagnostic Study with Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41358)
*Fengxian Ji,Jingpu Yang,Fan Zhang,Junhong Liang,Qian Jiang,Yuke Li,Jinri Guo,Rongxin Zhu,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出市场图像诊断框架，检验交易价格何时能反映人类视觉偏好，并发现价格只能在有限情况下提供可靠线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉偏好评估通常依赖昂贵的人类标注或偏好奖励模型。市场交易价格容易获得，也体现真实购买行为，但同时受来源、需求和竞争等非视觉因素影响。因此，仅看总体准确率，无法判断误差究竟来自模型、价格信号不可靠，还是评估流程受到干扰。

Method: 研究构建了由三部分组成的诊断框架：整理包含交易价格和盲于价格的人类排序的艺术图像比较集；将图像对划分为价格与人类判断一致、冲突及价格持平三类，并单独检查展示顺序效应；再通过直接比较、独立评分、预算约束和多智能体汇总等六种询问方式，评估视觉评分模型和多模态语言模型能否恢复人类支持的偏好信号。

Result: 在全部图像对中，只有百分之二十四点二呈现非持平的价格方向；在这些图像对中，价格方向与多数人类判断一致的比例为百分之三十四点七。在该有限范围内，一种偏好评分模型的可恢复准确率最高，为百分之六十六点四。某多模态模型的预算约束与汇总版本表现低于基线；另一模型通过界面调整取得的提升尚无统计学定论。

Conclusion: 交易价格并非视觉偏好的可靠真值，只在有限条件下包含可恢复的人类偏好信息。研究建议将价格视为带有不确定性的代理信号，并通过区分价格持平、价格与人类判断冲突及流程效应，谨慎评估模型能力。

Abstract: Existing visual preference evaluation relies on costly human annotations or preference reward models, whereas online marketplace transaction prices are readily available and reflect real purchasing behavior, offering potential low-cost supervision. However, transaction prices conflate visual preferences with non-visual factors such as provenance, demand, and competition, making aggregate accuracy insufficient to distinguish model errors, unreliable price signals, and protocol confounds. To address this identifiability challenge, we introduce MarketImage, a diagnostic framework that identifies when transaction prices reflect visual preferences, where they fail, and whether multimodal language models can recover human-supported visual preference signals. MarketImage comprises three components: (i) MarketImageArt, 266 six-image comparison sets with transaction prices and price-blind human rankings; (ii) MarketImageLens, a tie-preserving protocol that partitions 3,990 within-set image pairs into price-human agreement, conflict, and price-tie regions while separately auditing presentation-order effects; and (iii) MarketValuator, six elicitation methods spanning direct comparison, independent scoring, budget constraints, and multi-agent aggregation to evaluate signal recovery by static visual scorers and three multimodal language models. Experiments show that only 24.2% of image pairs have a non-tied price direction, of which 34.7% agree with the human majority. Within this region, HPSv2 leads at 66.4% Recoverable Accuracy; Qwen's budgeted and aggregated variants trail baselines, while GPT interface gains remain statistically unresolved. Thus, transaction prices encode recoverable visual preferences only under limited conditions and should be treated as uncertain proxies rather than ground truth.

</details>


### [226] [LEAP: Efficient LLM Inference Scheduling with Generalizable Length Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41359)
*Kwan Yan,Jialun Zhong,Yanzeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可在线快速适应、由中央处理器异步执行的请求长度预测系统，在降低预测开销的同时改善调度效果与服务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 按请求长度优先处理较短请求能够降低大语言模型服务延迟，但现有长度预测器在领域或任务变化时排名性能可能下降，重新训练成本高；预测本身的开销也可能抵消调度收益。

Method: 该系统复用服务模型预填充阶段的隐藏状态，以线性读出提取输出长度信息；利用已完成请求作为监督，通过递归最小二乘法在测试时更新读出层，无需梯度计算或回放历史数据。预测与更新异步运行于中央处理器，预测和初始批次排序则与剩余的图形处理器预填充过程并行。

Result: 在评估的跨领域迁移任务中，该系统保持了较强的长度排序能力；面对较大的领域变化，在线适应使斯皮尔曼相关系数提高百分之六十四点七。服务实验显示，与所评估的长度预测器相比，其平均每令牌延迟降低百分之十五点九至四十点八；相较于表现最强的基线，吞吐量提高百分之一点九，而预测器参数量减少约三万零五百六十九倍。

Conclusion: 研究表明，复用模型已有隐藏状态并采用轻量级在线更新，可以兼顾长度预测的跨领域适应能力与执行效率，从而提升长度感知调度的实际服务收益。

Abstract: Length-aware scheduling reduces request latency in LLM serving by prioritizing shorter requests. However, despite their large model sizes, length predictors can suffer degraded ranking performance under domain and task shifts, and adapting them can require costly retraining. Their prediction overhead can further offset the benefits of scheduling. To improve the generalization and efficiency of length-aware scheduling, we propose \textbf{LEAP}, a length prediction system with fast online adaptation and asynchronous CPU execution. LEAP reuses the serving model's prefill hidden states, which contain information about the output length, and extracts this signal through a linear readout. To adapt to substantial domain and task shifts, it updates the readout at test time through recursive least squares, using completed requests as supervision without gradient computation or historical-data replay. Prediction and updates run asynchronously on CPU, while prediction and initial batch ordering overlap the remaining GPU prefill. Across the evaluated cross-domain transfers, LEAP retains strong ranking performance, and online adaptation improves Spearman correlation by 64.7\% under a larger domain shift. Serving experiments show that LEAP reduces average per-token latency by 15.9--40.8\% compared with the evaluated length predictors, while achieving 1.9\% higher throughput with $30{,}569\times$ fewer predictor parameters than the strongest evaluated baseline.

</details>


### [227] [TSBPO: Tree-Structured Branching for Policy Optimization in Interactive Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41697)
*Rui Yu,Chong Mu,Jinchuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向交互式语言智能体的树结构采样与更新方法，通过有针对性的任务采样、分层优势估计和完整回合的策略损失，在两个交互基准上取得较高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭任务终局结果训练智能体面临三个相互关联的难题：如何选择任务及状态后续、如何将延迟反馈归因到具体决策，以及如何汇总多词元动作的损失。论文旨在将这些环节整合进同一训练流程，并在固定的轨迹采样预算下提高训练效率。

Method: 方法先使用基于似然拟合的根节点／分支分配器，依据保留的任务与状态证据，优先采样更可能产生不同终局结果的后续状态。随后在重放得到的树结构轨迹上，分别计算相邻分支片段之间的轨迹优势，以及在相同任务和观测状态下比较结果的分组步级优势。策略更新则对每个完整响应中的裁剪词元替代目标求和，并在有效交互回合间归一化，避免对响应长度取平均而改变策略梯度权重。

Result: 使用十五亿参数级语言模型，在阿尔夫世界基准上取得已见任务百分之九十七点七九、未见任务百分之九十六点六四的随机成功率。在网络商店基准的五百个测试目标上，每个目标进行六次随机评测，逐回合成功率为百分之八十一点零七。网络商店消融实验显示，去除步级优势项后，同一训练步数下成功率降至百分之七十五点九三；阿尔夫世界的损失聚合对比则显示，完整回合加权对应更高的已观测指令有效率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，这套树结构采样与更新配置在所评估的两个交互基准上具有良好表现；消融结果也支持步级优势估计及完整回合损失聚合的潜在作用。不过，摘要仅报告特定模型、任务和评测设置下的结果，尚不足以证明其对其他智能体或环境普遍有效。

Abstract: Training interactive language agents from terminal outcomes poses three coupled problems: which task and state continuations to sample, how to assign delayed feedback to decisions, and how to aggregate token losses for multi-token actions. We present TSBPO, a tree-structured sampling-to-update paradigm that addresses these problems within one training loop. A likelihood-fitted Root/Branch allocator uses retained task and state evidence to target continuations likely to produce contrasting terminal outcomes under fixed rollout budgets. On the resulting replayed physical trees, trajectory advantages compare immediate sibling segments, while observation-group step advantages compare outcomes at matched task/observation states. The actor then sums clipped token surrogates over each complete response and normalizes across active turns, avoiding response-length averaging in the policy-gradient term. With a Qwen2.5-1.5B actor, fixed checkpoints obtain 97.79% seen and 96.64% unseen stochastic success on ALFWorld and an 81.07% per-episode success rate over six stochastic episodes for each of the 500 WebShop test goals. A WebShop step-term ablation yields 75.93% at the same update-100 evaluation; an ALFWorld aggregation comparison shows higher observed command validity with complete-turn weighting. These results support the evaluated training configuration on two interactive benchmarks.

</details>


### [228] [RECITE: Revising Eligibility Criteria Incorporating Textual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41368)
*Russell Ro,Reza Abbasi-Asl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出一套自动化框架，从临床试验方案修订文件中提取并评估资格标准修改建议，并发现大语言模型能够较好地将既有证据转化为可操作的试验设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验的资格标准过于严格，常导致受试者招募失败。尽管已有研究提出放宽具体标准的建议，这些建议仍未能有效融入大规模试验设计流程，因此需要自动化方法来发现并落实有证据支持的标准修订。

Method: 研究构建了“融合文本证据修订资格标准”框架，利用临床试验注册平台上的方案修订文件，整理出涵盖五千七百三十五项试验的一万一千九百一十三条数据，每条将原始资格标准与文件证据及目标修订相配对。研究在三千一百一十六条样本上评测十五种大语言模型配置，比较上下文截断和检索增强生成方式，并通过经十名领域专家验证的模型评审流程评分。此外，研究使用智能体式文献发现系统寻找论文与试验的匹配关系。

Result: 模型评审与人工专家评分的一致性较高，加权一致性系数为零点六九四，标准差为零点零九六。表现最佳的开放权重模型得分为三点零，标准差为零点九，归一化得分为零点七四二；表现最佳的闭源模型得分同为三点零，标准差为零点八，归一化得分为零点七四五。文献发现系统还找到四十四组论文与试验的匹配，涉及五篇提供试验设计及资格标准修改证据的论文。

Conclusion: 研究表明，方案修订记录与相关文献中的临床知识可以通过自动化框架加以整理和应用，为资格标准修订提供可执行的证据支持。该方法有望帮助临床试验设计更有效地采用放宽标准的建议，但其实际效果仍需在真实试验流程中进一步验证。

Abstract: Overly restrictive eligibility criteria contribute to widespread enrollment failure in clinical trials, yet published recommendations for how specific criteria should be broadened remain disconnected from trial design at scale. We introduce RECITE (Revising Eligibility Criteria Incorporating Textual Evidence), a framework for automated discovery, extraction, and implementation of evidence-based eligibility criteria revisions. RECITE uses protocol amendment documents from ClinicalTrials.gov to construct a dataset of 11,913 curated instances across 5,735 trials, each pairing original criteria with documentary evidence and target revisions. We benchmark fifteen LLM configurations under truncated-to-context-window and retrieval-augmented generation conditions, on a 3,116-instance sample under an LLM-as-judge protocol validated against ten domain experts (judge vs. human quadratic-weighted Cohen's $κ_{qw} = 0.694 \pm 0.096$). The best open-weight model, Gemma 2 9B, achieves $κ_{qw}$-aligned ordinal score $3.0 \pm 0.9$ on a 0--4 scale (normalized 0.742) and the best closed-weight model, GPT-4o, reaches $3.0 \pm 0.8$ (normalized 0.745). Using an agentic literature discovery system, we then identified 44 paper--trial matches across five publications providing trial-design and EC-modification evidence. Our findings suggest that existing clinical knowledge can be operationalized into actionable trial design recommendations.

</details>


### [229] [From the Zoo to the BULLPEN: Sample-Efficient LLM Profiling via Multi-Channel Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41375)
*Russell Ro,Qiming Cui,Reza Abbasi-Asl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种从少量模型回答中提取行为特征的模型嵌入框架，可低成本预测多类模型属性，并提升行为、风格与来源相关任务的评估效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型评估通常需要针对不同属性分别运行基准测试，成本高昂。既有模型嵌入方法主要依赖答题正确性和问题特征，往往需要大量探测题目，也难以捕捉冗长程度、安全性和表达风格等体现在回答方式中的重要行为。

Method: 提出“基于行为画像编码的大语言模型理解”框架，扩展既有嵌入方法：整理下游任务并新增二十项有关行为、风格和模型来源的基准；联合编码模型回答文本、回答正确性及问题文本；将模型画像所需的问题数量减少至数百道。

Result: 在二百六十个开放权重模型上，回答文本编码器在新增任务上的平均得分为零点四八，优于最佳既有编码器的零点三五。仅使用四百道问题时，回答文本编码器保留了至少百分之九十一的完整题库表现，推理计算量仅为使用完整题库的百分之一点三；仅依赖正确性的基线则保留百分之八十三。

Conclusion: 结合回答内容与正确性能够更全面地刻画模型行为，并以较少探测题实现有效画像。该方法只需模型输出，适用于行为审计和低成本模型筛选，也有望扩展至仅通过应用程序接口调用的模型。

Abstract: Choosing among the hundreds of thousands of available large language models (LLMs) requires a thorough understanding of each candidate model's properties. In practice, each property is measured by its own benchmark suite, so profiling a model on many properties means paying for many separate evaluations. To address this, model embeddings compress each model into a fixed-length vector from which many properties can be predicted. Existing methods, such as EmbedLLM, build these vectors from correctness patterns and question features, and often need large probe batteries to do so. However, many behaviors that are important when choosing a model, such as its verbosity, safety and style, show up more in how it answers than in whether it is correct. We introduce BULLPEN (Behavioral Understanding of Large Language models via Profile ENcodings), a framework that extends EmbedLLM in three ways. First, BULLPEN organizes the downstream tasks that prior work uses and adds 20 task benchmarks we built on behavior, style and provenance. Second, it is the first framework to read a model's own answer text jointly with its correctness and the question text. Third, it encodes models from a few hundred questions instead of the full 24,118-question pool. Across 260 open-weight models, our answer-text encoder scores 0.48 +/- 0.10 on the new tasks against 0.35 +/- 0.10 for the best prior encoder. Our encoders that read answer text keep at least 91% of their full-pool skill from only 400 questions (1.3% of the inference compute of the full question pool), while a correctness-only baseline keeps 83% with the same 400 questions. Because these embeddings need only model outputs, they support behavioral auditing and low-cost profiling, and could extend to API-only models. Code and data: https://github.com/wcquesera/bullpen

</details>


### [230] [Ask on Demand: Targeted Peer Communication for Parallel Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41378)
*Haofu Yang,Hongyang Yin,Ran Bi,Faguo Wu,Peng Xue,Feng Zhou,Han Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种按需通信框架，让并行大语言模型智能体在遇到具体信息需求时向同伴求助，从而提升任务表现并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行智能体虽能探索多条任务轨迹，但有价值的发现往往只留在各自的执行记录中。现有方法通常提前筛选或总结信息，难以预知其他智能体之后的需求，因此可能遗漏届时关键的细节。论文旨在让信息共享与智能体不断变化的需求相匹配。

Method: 框架允许智能体在出现明确的信息需求时发起定向求助。请求获准后，系统从同伴的执行历史中检索相关证据，选择合适的同伴，并生成针对请求的回复。此外，研究者使用成对的团队后续执行结果训练轻量级决策门控器，依据最终团队表现与推理成本，判断是否值得求助。

Result: 在应用世界和网络商店任务上的实验表明，该方法相较于彼此独立的并行执行，能够稳定提升任务表现；与已有信息共享基线相比，则能以更低的推理成本实现协作。

Conclusion: 将同伴通信与智能体实际出现的信息需求对齐，有助于更有效、经济地利用分散在执行历史中的知识。按需求助为提升多智能体协作效果提供了一种兼顾性能与成本的方案。

Abstract: Parallel LLM agents tackle complex tasks by exploring multiple trajectories, yet useful discoveries remain isolated in individual execution histories. Existing sharing methods often select or summarize information before other agents' needs become clear, potentially overlooking details that become important later. We propose Ask on Demand (AOD), a communication framework that enables agents to seek targeted peer assistance when specific information needs arise. Upon an approved request, AOD retrieves relevant evidence from peer execution histories, selects a suitable peer, and generates a response tailored to the request. To determine when such assistance is worthwhile, we train a lightweight gate using paired team continuations, with supervision derived from final team outcomes and inference cost. Experiments on AppWorld and WebShop demonstrate that AOD consistently improves task performance over independent parallel execution while achieving lower inference cost than existing information-sharing baselines. These findings highlight the benefits of aligning peer communication with agents' evolving information needs to achieve more effective and efficient collaboration.

</details>


### [231] [Online Evidence-Containment Prediction over Text Spans under Distinct Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41384)
*Xiang Liu,Haochen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将嵌套证据区域的在线验证建模为包含关系预测，并据此减少验证调用；实验显示该方法能提升证据定位效率，并在检索层级任务中改善固定阅读器的问答表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证据驱动的自然语言处理系统常需逐个核验嵌套文本区域，而先前的核验结果会约束后续区域。若忽略这种包含关系，系统可能浪费验证调用。论文希望形式化利用区域间的结构信息，并研究在在线、每个候选至多查询一次的条件下，预测性能能够达到什么水平。

Method: 论文提出在线证据包含关系预测框架：在精确核验候选区域之前，先预测其是否包含目标证据。文本跨度的包含关系可表示为端点秩构成的三角形二维乘积序，因此该问题属于具有不同查询点的在线保序回归。作者分析了多种目标设定下的遗憾界，并为单一固定目标设计了多项式时间学习算法。此外，作者提出期望搜索空间缩减指标，将预测概率转化为验证顺序决策。

Result: 理论上，当允许多个目标时，最坏情形遗憾随有效规模近线性增长；当目标固定时，算法可达到对数级且渐近最优的遗憾。在自然问题问答数据的真实交互过程中，包含关系带来显著的逻辑推断收益，学习方法还进一步改善了尚未解决候选的预测；结果在两种模型骨干和不同检索层级上得到复现。在科学事实核查、证据推理及检索层级实验中，该指标帮助系统以更少的核验调用定位证据；在检索层级的调用预算限制下，方法优于四种近期外部选择器，并使固定阅读器的问答分数高于学习式调度和生成器贪心调度。

Conclusion: 显式建模文本区域的包含结构，能够把逻辑推断与在线学习结合起来，减少证据核验成本。该研究既给出了不同目标设定下的理论性能界，也表明基于期望搜索空间缩减的决策策略可提升实际证据定位与问答效果。

Abstract: Evidence-grounded NLP systems often verify nested text regions sequentially, so verifying one region constrains others. We formalize this as Online Evidence-Containment Prediction (OECP): each candidate is predicted before exact verification and queried at most once. Span containment is exactly a triangular 2D product order, making OECP distinct-query online isotonic regression. With multiple admissible targets, worst-case regret is near-linear, $\widetildeΘ(m_{\mathrm{eff}})$, in the effective scale given by endpoint-rank compression; a single fixed target, as in standard QA, admits an explicit polynomial-time learner with optimal $Θ(\log m_{\mathrm{eff}})$ regret, run on real NQ episodes. On Natural Questions, containment yields a large deductive gain plus a smaller learned gain on unresolved candidates, reproduced across two backbones and DPR hierarchies. Expected Search-space Reduction (ESR) turns these probabilities into decisions, localizing evidence in fewer verifier calls on SciFact, ERASER, and DPR hierarchies; on DPR hierarchies under call budgets it outperforms four recent external selectors and raises fixed-reader F1 over learned and generator-greedy scheduling.

</details>


### [232] [DAMI: Dual-Axis Memory Initialization for Enhanced Long-Context Retrieval in Gated Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41994)
*Zhangchi Feng,Harry Jiang,Yangyifei Luo,Ray Zhang,Richong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出双轴记忆初始化方法，在训练前联合设置门控线性递归模型的状态衰减与输入敏感度，提升长上下文检索能力，且不增加可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力随序列长度线性扩展，但有限状态记忆可能限制长距离信息检索。记忆保留能力受状态衰减影响，而衰减由门控参数和输入激活共同决定。受控实验发现，相关参数训练后仍与初始值高度相关，说明初始化对最终记忆行为十分重要。

Method: 双轴记忆初始化在训练前联合配置两类参数：在不同注意力头之间设置几何分布的记忆时间尺度，以覆盖不同检索距离；在不同网络层之间设置由低到高再到低的输入敏感度分布。该方法利用少量激活样本校准模型实现，使其符合预设行为目标，且不引入额外可训练参数。

Result: 在多种门控线性递归模型及相关架构上，该方法使类基准长文本检索的平均准确率较随机初始化基线提高5.29至16.71个百分点。语言建模、常识推理和两千词元真实场景检索的表现总体与基线相当。

Conclusion: 合理的记忆初始化能够在不增加模型参数的情况下，显著改善多类线性递归架构的长距离检索能力，同时基本维持其他任务表现。研究结果表明，训练前协同设计记忆时间尺度与输入敏感度，是提升有限状态记忆利用效率的一种有效途径。

Abstract: Linear attention scales linearly with sequence length, but its finite-state memory can limit long-context retrieval. In gated linear recurrences, memory retention depends on state decay, which is shaped jointly by gate parameters A_log and dt_bias and by input activations. In controlled Gated DeltaNet (GDN) experiments, both parameters remain highly correlated with their initial values after training, highlighting the importance of initialization. Therefore, we introduce Dual-Axis Memory Initialization (DAMI), which jointly configures these parameters before training. DAMI assigns geometric timescales across heads for varied retrieval distances and a low-high-low input-sensitivity profile across layers. A small activation sample calibrates the GDN-family implementation to match both behavioral targets without adding trainable parameters. Across GDN, KDA, GDN2, Mamba-2, and RWKV-7, DAMI improves average RULER-style retrieval accuracy by 5.29 to 16.71 percentage points over the corresponding random-initialization baselines. Performance on language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and 2K real-world retrieval remains broadly comparable to those baselines. Our code is available at https://speedai.com/research/dami.

</details>


### [233] [Cross-Interface Authorization for AI Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41397)
*Genliang Zhu,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种受配置边界约束的计算机使用授权协议：在执行前后分别核验操作路径、规范化后的效果、授权与权威状态，并要求持久化结果由接收回执证实。实验与审计支持其在声明配置及假设范围内的安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理可通过图形界面、命令行、应用程序接口、脚本及上下文协议服务器等多条路径执行操作。即使策略判断与界面无关，实际授权仍可能被其他路径绕过、被改变的安全默认值影响，或因授权后状态变化而失效。因此，需要确保所有产生效果的路径都受到执行约束，并在操作提交时重新确认授权条件。

Method: 针对固定且版本化的部署配置，枚举会产生效果的操作路径与持久化结果接收端，并检查路径到接收端之间是否存在完整的强制执行关口。将具体请求归一化为规范效果，签发绑定操作路径的准备票据；提交时再次核验路径、效果、授权、执行轨迹、配置、清单及权威状态。只有接收端回执与预期一致，才认定操作已提交。论文在明确假设下证明配置范围内的细化正确性、相同效果的决策一致性、对替代路径绕过及声明范围内跨界面重组的抵抗能力、提交时状态安全性，以及结果由回执证实。

Result: 参考配置覆盖十二类效果、五种接口和六十条操作路径。两种结构不同的运行时映射在各自的一千七百三十二个场景中共生成三千四百六十四条轨迹，其中九百六十次提交、二千三百一十二次拒绝、以及一百九十二次重新准备后的提交。独立检查器接受全部保留轨迹与配对结果，并拒绝二十六项语义回归；覆盖率和规范化测试分别拒绝二十四项与七百二十项变异。全部一百六十八项提交探测均符合声明结果。对十二个文件的冻结源码审计通过五十二项检查；适配器验证提交了四十八次受测调用，并拒绝十五项未绑定调用。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该协议可在受测部署配置中把授权约束延伸到不同操作路径，并以提交时状态核验和接收端回执提升执行结果的可信度。但其保证明确受配置清单、效果语义及证明假设限制，不能据此推断对未声明路径、其他部署或未建模效果同样安全。

Abstract: Computer-use agents act through graphical interfaces, command lines, APIs, scripts, and Model Context Protocol servers. A policy decision can be interface-invariant while its executable authorization is not: another route can bypass mediation, alter a security-relevant default, or commit after the authorized state has changed. We present a profile-bounded protocol for a fixed, versioned computer-use deployment. It enumerates effect-bearing routes and durable sinks, checks a route-to-sink enforcement cut, normalizes each concrete request to a canonical effect, issues a route-bound prepare ticket, and verifies the route, effect, grant, trace, profile, inventory, and authoritative state again at commit. A committed result requires an agreeing sink receipt. Under explicit assumptions, we prove profile-bounded refinement, matched-effect decision invariance, resistance to alternate-route laundering and declared cross-surface recomposition, commit-time state safety, and receipt-grounded outcomes.
  The reference profile registers 12 effect families, five interfaces, and 60 routes. Two structurally distinct runtime mappings produce 3,464 traces over 1,732 scenarios each: 960 commits, 2,312 denials, and 192 commits after re-preparation. An independent checker accepts every retained trace and pair and rejects 26/26 semantic regressions; coverage and normalizer suites reject 24/24 and 720/720 mutants; and 168/168 commit probes match their declared outcomes. A frozen-source audit passes 52 checks over 12 files from two independent hybrid runtimes and one public authorization substrate. Source-shaped adapter refinements commit 48/48 instrumented calls and reject 15/15 unbound cases. The resulting guarantees bind the declared profile and its effect semantics.

</details>


### [234] [scAnyOmics: Foundation-Model Distillation for Any-to-Any Completion of Multi-omics Virtual Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41399)
*Zhaoqi Song,Ye Du,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Qian Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出统一的单细胞多组学生成框架，可整合不同分子模态、补全缺失测量，并迁移预训练模型中的生物学知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞技术能够同时测量转录、蛋白和染色质可及性等信息，但不同模态数据异质、配对样本有限，导致联合分析和缺失模态预测困难。研究旨在利用预训练模型知识，提升不完整多组学数据的整合与预测能力。

Method: 研究构建生成式框架，将冻结的单细胞基础模型中的知识蒸馏到多模态学生模型中。模型把单细胞转录组、蛋白标记物测量和染色质可及性数据映射到共享表征空间，用于跨模态预测及缺失模态重建。研究汇集六项公开研究中的六十九万余个细胞，包含配对和部分配对数据，并在留出供体数据上评估模型。

Result: 在留出供体测试中，该框架在多项评价指标上优于基线方法。除跨模态重建外，研究还探索了其在细胞类型注释、扰动响应预测和生物年龄估计中的应用。

Conclusion: 该框架为不完整单细胞多组学数据提供了统一整合与预测方案，并展示了将预训练生物学知识迁移至不同细胞背景和实验平台的潜力，可为虚拟细胞模型的构建提供支持。

Abstract: Recent advances in single-cell technologies have enabled the profiling of multiple molecular modalities, including transcriptomics, proteomics, and chromatin accessibility, providing a more comprehensive understanding of cellular states. However, integrating heterogeneous modalities and predicting missing molecular information remain challenging, particularly when paired measurements are limited. Here, we introduce scAnyOmics, a generative framework that distills knowledge from a frozen single-cell foundation model into a multimodal student model. scAnyOmics integrates scRNA-seq, protein-marker measurements, and scATAC-seq into a shared representation, enabling cross-modal prediction and reconstruction of missing modalities. We assembled 690,880 cells from six publicly available studies, covering both paired and partially paired multi-omics measurements, to systematically evaluate its predictive performance. We further evaluated scAnyOmics on held-out donor data, where it outperformed baseline methods across multiple evaluation metrics. Beyond cross-modal reconstruction, we explored its applications in cell-type annotation, perturbation-response prediction, and biological age estimation. Overall, scAnyOmics provides a unified framework for integrating incomplete single-cell multi-omics data and transferring pretrained biological knowledge, supporting the development of virtual cell models across diverse cellular contexts and experimental platforms.

</details>


### [235] [The Knowledge Layer Model (KLM): Evidence-Visible AI Output, Routing Diagnostics, and Negative Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42066)
*Omer Asim Ozturk,Cihan Aydiner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于记录人工智能推理依据的开放规范，并报告若干彼此独立的内部实验。结果显示，部分记录与文本保留检查可行，但证据不足以证明答案正确、模型确实依赖所记录的依据，或系统具备安全性与合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭答案看起来正确，审查者无法判断系统当时依据了什么。论文旨在让推理所依据的证据、认知判断及明确的未知项可被检查，并通过版本化记录提升审查的可追溯性。

Method: 提出知识层模型规范，定义推理记录中的版本化认知评估、证据关系和明确未知项；并以某人工智能操作系统的知识与证据架构作为参考实现。实证部分包含多项分开的内部实验，涉及领域路由、模型权重路径配置、容量增长、诊断指标、文本打包保留，以及两种语言验证器对合成样例的检查结果。

Result: 历史领域路由基准报告在15个类别中正确分类137/150例，即91.3%；名义上的95%威尔逊区间为85.7%至94.9%，但计数是根据四舍五入摘要恢复的，历史样例归属也未核实。原生模型权重路径配置在修正后的双来源测试中15例均未成功。容量增长实验因旧版评分器有缺陷且缺少原始输出，无法得出可靠效果。两种诊断的曲线下面积均为0.008，远低于预设的0.65最低标准。旧版文本打包在221个选定案例中有118例丢失含金文本，修复版则全部保留；这只衡量文本是否存在，不代表答案质量。两种语言的验证器据报在合成样例的六项检查上结果一致，但另一次生产环境披露未获接受。

Conclusion: 该规范提供了记录和审查推理依据的一种实现中立框架，但现有实验仅属内部报告，且并非端到端验证。恢复与重新推导能力、人工评分支持、边界隔离及有界响应等评估仍待开展。证据可见不等于答案正确，也不能证明因果依赖、安全性或符合法规；独立复现和完整公开审计材料仍是后续工作。

Abstract: AI answers require more than apparent correctness: reviewers need to inspect their recorded basis. We introduce the Knowledge Layer Model (KLM), an open, implementation-neutral specification for inference records with versioned epistemic assessments, evidence relations, and explicit unknowns. SEDIM, the knowledge and evidence architecture of Nage AI OS, is a reference implementation. We reference KLM v0.3 (repository tag v0.3.0). Empirical material consists of separate internal experiments, not end-to-end validation. A historical platform-domain routing benchmark reports 137/150 classifications across 15 classes (91.3%; nominal 95% Wilson interval 85.7-94.9%); the numerator is recovered from a rounded summary, and historical example membership is unverified. A native-model weight-path configuration yielded 0/15 successful answers in a corrected two-source test. A capacity-growth comparison could not establish a reliable effect because of a defective legacy scorer and missing original outputs. Lexical and scoped diagnostics each yielded AUC 0.008 for silent-versus-contradicted examples, below the registered minimum of 0.65. Legacy packing lost gold-containing text in 118/221 selected cases; repaired packing retained it in 221/221. These measure text presence, not answer quality. Python and TypeScript verifiers reportedly agreed on six checks per synthetic fixture; a separate production disclosure was not accepted. Restoration, re-derivation, human-rubric support, boundary isolation, and bounded-response evaluations remain proposed. Findings are internal and author-reported; historical observations are distinguished from later reproductions. Evidence visibility does not establish correctness, causal reliance, security, or regulatory compliance. Independent replication and a complete public audit package remain future work.

</details>


### [236] [CryoAtlas: A Large Curated Dataset and Unified Benchmark for Cryo-EM Atomic Model Building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41411)
*Mingrui Li,Minzhang Li,Weichen Qin,Yufan Xie,Zhangzhi Xiong,Sixian Shen,Jiakai Zhang,Yuan Pei,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文构建了高质量冷冻电镜数据集与统一评测基准，用于推动蛋白质链的密度引导原子模型构建研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已推动冷冻电镜密度图中的自动原子建模，但研究进展受限于大型、高质量数据集不足，以及评测标准不统一。这使不同方法难以公平比较，也限制了模型训练与改进。

Method: 作者整合并筛选截至二〇二六年三月的电镜数据库和蛋白质结构数据库条目，建立包含一万七千八百零九个“密度图—模型—序列”条目的数据集，并整理出一万六千六百五十七条训练数据。另构建包含六百一十八个目标的基准，采用统一协议共同评估结构准确性、模型与密度图的吻合度及立体化学有效性。研究还比较了当前学习型方法、晶体学与电镜结构解析软件以及第三代蛋白质结构预测模型，并分析分辨率、序列长度、蛋白质类别和序列同一性对建模准确性的影响。

Result: 该数据集与基准为相关方法提供了较大规模、可复现的训练和评测资源。作为案例，在新数据集上重新训练一种模型的卷积神经网络组件后，其基准测试表现有所提升。摘要未提供具体提升幅度或各方法的详细排名。

Conclusion: 这项工作通过系统整理数据和建立统一评测流程，补足了冷冻电镜原子模型构建研究的基础设施短板，为公平比较、模型改进及后续研究提供了开放且可复现的平台。

Abstract: Deep learning has accelerated automated atomic model building from cryo-EM maps, yet progress remains limited by the lack of large, high-quality datasets and standardized evaluation benchmarks. We address this gap by introducing CryoAtlas, a rigorously curated dataset and unified benchmark for density-guided atomic model building of protein chains. By integrating and filtering EMDB and PDB entries (up to March 2026), CryoAtlas provides 17,809 map-model-sequence entries, including a curated 16,657-entry training split. From this resource, we construct a 618-target benchmark with a shared evaluation protocol that jointly assesses structural accuracy, model-to-map fit, and stereochemical validity. Using this framework, we benchmark state-of-the-art learning-based methods against Phenix and AlphaFold3, comprehensively analyzing the impact of map resolution, sequence length, protein category and sequence identity on modeling accuracy. As a case study, retraining the ModelAngelo CNN component on CryoAtlas improves performance on the benchmark. CryoAtlas provides an open, reproducible foundation for future cryo-EM atomic model building research. Project details and resource release updates are available at https://oulling.github.io/cryoatlas/.

</details>


### [237] [Evidence-Guided Recovery for AI Agents with Ambiguous Tool Outcomes and Partial Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41453)
*Genliang Zhu,Xiaobo Chen,Ziyuan Wang,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究工具超时后，智能体如何依据公开证据安全恢复任务。规划建议在受控比较中明显优于证据提示，但结论仅适用于所评估的建议接口及其能力假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用超时可能留下模糊结果：操作或许失败、已经提交，或仍在执行，而其他步骤可能已完成。若直接重试，可能造成重复操作或残留副作用；若仅凭表面结果判断，也可能把未获证据支持的状态误认为任务完成。因此，需要一种结合证据、服务能力和状态不确定性的恢复方法，并明确区分实际达成目标与公开证据足以支持的安全完成。

Method: 研究将恢复问题表述为：在已批准义务、服务提供方能力以及与公开证据相容的状态范围内，寻找可接受的任务完成方案。通过统一的可接受性检查处理密钥过期、授权时机、步骤依赖和残留影响，并用终止证书区分目标是否实际满足、是否有公开证据支持安全结束。已知模型实现将标准的基于观察分支规划，与独立实现的效果历史判定器结合。随后进行预先登记、按任务划分留出的比较，在相同安全约束下，对比规划建议与证据提示，并使用两套本地模型配置。

Result: 有限研究包含三十七项任务：规划方法和三种主动对照方法均为二十八项任务出具完成证书，规划方法额外耗费四个合成费用单位的计算成本。留出比较涵盖十九个既有任务家族中的五十八组配对样本：规划建议认证三十项完成，证据提示认证九项，差距为三十六点二个百分点；按任务家族进行的精确符号翻转检验结果为零点零零七八。规划建议提出二百七十二个操作提案，少于证据提示的三百九十九个；全部一百一十六条执行轨迹均通过独立重放。两套模型配置的完成率差异均为正。

Conclusion: 在该实验设置和共同安全约束下，规划建议比证据提示更能支持安全、可认证的任务完成，同时减少操作提案。不过，结果衡量的是所评估的建议接口，不能直接证明搜索式建议普遍优于规则式建议；后者仍需匹配条件下的独立比较。未知结果和交接状态仍被显式保留，任何保证也仅在所声明的服务提供方能力范围内成立，并不意味着任意第三方系统都能保证操作恰好执行一次。

Abstract: Tool timeouts can leave AI agents facing ambiguous tool outcomes and partial execution: an action may have failed, committed or remained in flight while other workflow steps have already completed. We formulate evidence-guided task recovery over approved obligations, provider capabilities and states compatible with public evidence. A shared admissibility check handles key expiry, authorization timing, dependencies and residual effects; a terminal certificate separates actual goal satisfaction from publicly supported safe completion. The known-model implementation combines standard observation-contingent planning with a separately implemented effect-history adjudicator. In a finite study of 37 assignments, planning and three active controls each certify 28 completions; four saved synthetic fee units incur additional computation. A preregistered assignment-held-out comparison then evaluates planning advice against evidence prompting with two local model bundles. Across 58 paired units in 19 previously developed task families, advice certifies 30 completions versus 9, a 36.2-percentage-point difference (exact family sign-flip p=0.0078), using 272 proposals versus 399. All 116 trajectories pass independent replay. Both models exhibit a positive completion difference. The comparison measures the evaluated advice interface under a common shield; search-versus-rule advice requires a separate matched comparison. Unknown and handoff outcomes remain explicit, with guarantees tied to the declared provider capabilities rather than arbitrary third-party exactly-once execution.

</details>


### [238] [EvoMCP: Co-evolving MCP agents and training data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41479)
*Xuanang Gao,Kepeng Lin,Xinyan Shi,Yong Deng,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng,Xiaofeng Wu,Jie Yang,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种智能体与数据相互演进框架，通过持续发现能力短板、生成任务并改进示范轨迹，形成闭环微调流程；在三个基准测试上均显著提升了模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型上下文协议智能体的监督微调通常依赖静态数据，无法随学生模型能力变化而调整，因而难以及时覆盖新暴露的弱点。研究旨在让训练任务和示范数据能够依据模型反馈持续演进。

Method: 该框架构建由任务演进、轨迹演进和监督微调组成的闭环。首先，根据模型执行反馈评估其已有能力与能力缺口，并据此确定学习目标；随后，在保持验证器一致的前提下，改编经过验证的种子任务中的指令和可观察环境状态，生成可执行的新任务；再利用可复用技能修复教师模型失败的执行轨迹，并优化成功轨迹，以获得可靠示范。最后，使用任务与轨迹配对数据微调学生模型，将通过验收的任务作为后续演进的种子，并根据更新后模型的反馈启动下一轮迭代。

Result: 在工具智能体、模型上下文协议基准和模型上下文协议智能体基准上，该方法相较初始学生模型分别取得百分之十六点五、百分之十点二和百分之十二点六的绝对提升，三个基准均呈现稳定改进。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将能力评估、任务生成、执行轨迹改进与微调整合为反馈闭环，有助于缓解静态训练数据与模型能力变化之间的不匹配，并持续提升模型上下文协议智能体的能力。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of Model Context Protocol (MCP) agents is typically conducted on a static data distribution, which fails to adapt to the student's evolving capabilities and leaves newly exposed weaknesses insufficiently addressed. To address these challenges, we propose \method, an agent--data mutual evolution framework that enables MCP agents to improve through a closed loop of task evolution, trajectory evolution, and supervised fine-tuning. Specifically, \method\ assesses the student's established capabilities and capability gaps from rollout feedback to identify learning targets. Task evolution then translates these targets into executable tasks by adapting the instructions and observable environment states of compatible verified seed tasks while keeping their verifiers aligned. To provide reliable and efficient demonstrations for these tasks, trajectory evolution uses reusable skills to recover failed teacher rollouts and refine successful ones. The resulting task--trajectory pairs are used to fine-tune the student, while accepted tasks seed subsequent evolution. To close the loop, we re-evaluate the updated student and use its feedback to guide the next round of data evolution. Experiments on Toolathlon, MCPMark, and MCP-Atlas demonstrate consistent improvements over the initial student, yielding absolute gains of 16.5\%, 10.2\%, and 12.6\%, respectively. Project page: https://github.com/xagao/EvoMCP.

</details>


### [239] [Multi-Test Nation Bias Benchmarking of LLMs with Explicit Unbiased Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41486)
*Jonghyeon Choi,Yeonjun Choi,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究以联合国安理会记录为依据，构建具有明确参照基准的多维评测，发现四种大语言模型对中国、俄罗斯的输出相对偏向反对立场，对英国、美国、法国的输出相对偏向支持立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中的国家层面系统性偏差可能给国际关系应用带来实际风险，但既有评测往往没有清楚界定何为无偏的真实参照，因而难以进行充分量化，也难以跨场景比较。研究旨在建立扎根于可观察证据、并能覆盖多种偏差表达方式的评测框架。

Method: 研究扩展了包含722份联合国安理会正式记录的语料，并以五项互补测试衡量国家层面偏差，分别考察观点排序、关联排序、投票模拟、反对立场归因和标题框架。研究为每项测试明确设定无偏参照；对于难以直接确定真实答案的观点排序和关联排序，则依据可观察的投票行为构造行动型伪无偏基准。随后，对四种大语言模型围绕安理会五个常任理事国进行评测。

Result: 跨测试的总体模式显示，相对于各项测试设定的参照基准，模型对中国和俄罗斯的输出更倾向于反对立场，对英国、美国和法国的输出则更倾向于支持立场。该模式并非局限于某一种测试，而是在多种偏差表达渠道中均可观察到。

Conclusion: 面向国际关系的模型部署需要接受系统审计。评测不应只依赖单一任务或模糊的无偏假设，而应采用多维测试，并为每项测试设定明确、基于证据的参照点，以提升偏差测量的可量化性和跨场景可比性。

Abstract: While prior work has highlighted that systematic nation-level bias in large language models (LLMs) can pose operational risks for international relations (IR) applications, many existing evaluations still lack a clearly specified unbiased reference (ground truth), limiting fully quantitative and cross-setting measurement. Using an expanded corpus of 722 official UN Security Council (UNSC) records, we measure nation-level bias with five complementary tests, thereby extending prior approaches to cover a broader set of bias expression channels: opinion ranking (WOW), association sorting (AK+-), vote simulation (PVS), opposition attribution (F-Oppose), and framing (Headliner). To enable grounded bias evaluation, we explicitly define an unbiased status for each test; in particular, for WOW and AK+- where ground truth is difficult to specify, we propose an action-based pseudo unbiased status derived from observable voting behavior to construct a quantifiable baseline. Benchmarking 4 LLMs (GPT-4o-mini, Llama 3.3-70B, Mistral-22B-Small, and Qwen3-30B) across the five permanent members of the UNSC, we find a test-agnostic macro-pattern: relative to the reference baselines defined for each test, model outputs lean toward the opposition (red) side for China and Russia and toward the supportive (blue) side for the U.K., the U.S., and France. These results underscore the need to audit IR-facing LLM deployments with grounded, multi-test benchmarks tied to explicit reference points.

</details>


### [240] [Specification-Guided Generation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41511)
*Alessandro Previti,Konstantinos Vandikas,Mario Alviano,Mats Carlsson,Albin Larsson Forsberg,Alexandros Nikou,Athanasios Karapantelakis,Aneta Vulgarakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种结合大语言模型生成与符号推理的规范引导解码方法，使结构化输出不仅符合语法，也满足形式化规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有受约束解码主要保证输出语法正确，无法直接确保输出满足形式化规范；拒绝采样等方式还可能带来重复推理开销。因此，论文旨在将规范检查融入生成过程，提前排除无法导向合规结果的选择。

Method: 该框架在每一步解码时利用符号推理细化语法约束，过滤那些无法扩展为满足规范的完整输出的取值。框架包含两种变体：仅向前解码通过构造保证规范满足；回溯解码则在后续选择受限时修改先前可行的选择，避免模型被迫选取其自身认为不合适的值。该方法与模型无关，无需重新训练，并可建立在现有语法约束解码机制之上。

Result: 在配置生成和约束求解基准上，仅向前变体能够稳定生成符合规范的输出，相较拒绝采样减少了重复的大语言模型推理，额外符号推理开销较小。回溯变体进一步减少模型被迫采用其判定为不合适取值的情况，从而显著提升输出对模型自身提示理解的遵循程度。

Conclusion: 将符号推理与大语言模型解码结合，可以在生成过程中直接落实形式化规范。仅向前解码提供构造性合规保证，回溯机制则进一步改善模型偏好与最终输出之间的一致性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate structured outputs, but existing constrained-decoding methods mainly enforce syntax and cannot directly enforce satisfaction of a formal specification. We introduce a neuro-symbolic, specification-guided decoding framework that combines LLM generation with symbolic reasoning. At each step, the framework refines the decoding grammar by filtering out values that cannot be extended to an output satisfying the specification. We present a forward-only variant that guarantees specification satisfaction by construction, and a backtracking variant that revises earlier feasible choices when their downstream consequences leave only values that the LLM itself judges unsuitable. The framework is model-agnostic, requires no retraining, and builds on existing grammar-constrained decoding mechanisms. Experiments on configuration and constraint-solving benchmarks show that the forward-only variant reliably produces specification-compliant outputs while reducing repeated LLM inference compared with rejection sampling and incurring modest symbolic-reasoning overhead. The backtracking variant further reduces cases in which the model is forced to select values that it has itself judged unsuitable, thereby substantially improving adherence to the LLM's stated interpretation of the prompt.

</details>


### [241] [Verify--Backtrack Loop: Structured Precondition Recovery for Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41564)
*Mingyang Liu,Qingcan Kang,Shixiong Kai,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Xin Li,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“验证—回溯循环”，通过明确更新目标层级与失败原因，提升语言智能体执行长程交互任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务的难点不只是步骤多，更在于行动依赖隐含前提：当前提缺失或任务分解出错时，盲目尝试其他行动往往无济于事。既有方法常以自由文本描述纠错过程，缺少连接最终目标、当前目标与失败来源的明确控制状态。

Method: 提出一种无需训练、可扩展于反思—行动范式的控制方法。它以有界目标栈维护任务分解，并在目标启动、执行失败或环境状态显著变化时，同时判断当前目标是否有助于最终目标、以及在当前状态下是否可行；再结合类型化失败归因，明确执行压入、弹出、重试或回溯至父目标等操作。

Result: 在三种语言模型与应用世界、人工智能家庭、科学世界三个交互环境上的所有测试组合中，该方法均优于反思—行动基线及基于反思的控制方法。在较具挑战性的应用世界环境中，任务成功率最高提升二十二点零七个百分点。消融实验显示，移除验证或失败诊断都会降低表现，部分设置下甚至不如基线。

Conclusion: 提升长程交互可靠性的关键，不应局限于修正下一步行动，还要显式修订规划状态。对目标相关性、当前可行性及失败来源进行联合判断，有助于智能体在前提缺失或分解失败时进行有效恢复。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks in interactive environments, where actions often depend on latent preconditions. The core difficulty is not trajectory length but recovery: when a prerequisite is missing or an earlier decomposition fails, trying another plausible action rarely helps. ReAct and later reflection or adaptive-planning methods support local correction, but express recovery as free-form text, with no explicit control state linking the terminal goal, the active goal, and the failure source. We introduce the Verify--Backtrack Loop (VBL), a training-free extension of ReAct that turns precondition discovery and failure diagnosis into structured planning-state updates. VBL maintains the task decomposition as a bounded goal stack and, on goal activation, execution failure, or material state change, jointly verifies two horizons---whether the active goal contributes to the terminal goal and whether it is feasible in the current state. These dual-horizon judgments, combined with typed failure attribution, trigger explicit operations---Push, Pop, retry, or BacktrackTo the parent.
