We evaluate VBL with Qwen3.5-9B, GPT-4o, and DeepSeek-V3.2 on AppWorld, ALFWorld, and ScienceWorld. VBL improves over ReAct and reflection-based control on every backbone--benchmark pair. On the challenging AppWorld setting, it improves task success by up to 22.07 percentage points over ReAct. Ablations show that removing either verification or diagnosis degrades performance, in several settings below ReAct, confirming that the two mechanisms are complementary. These results show that reliable long-horizon interaction benefits from revising the planning state, not merely the next action.

</details>


### [242] [Connectivity-Aware Client Selection in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41570)
*Usevalad Milasheuski,Monica Nicoli,Stefano Savazzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文利用图拉普拉斯矩阵的谱信息，帮助去中心化联邦学习客户端挑选更有价值的邻居，在减少一半通信消息的同时，保持接近全邻居广播的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习虽能避免中心服务器带来的单点故障和通信瓶颈，但现有方法通常要求每个客户端向所有邻居广播模型更新，通信开销和能耗较高。论文旨在减少通信量，同时尽量不损害模型收敛与准确率。

Method: 针对固定通信图，利用图拉普拉斯矩阵的谱性质设计轻量级邻居选择方法。具体而言，结合特征向量嵌入以及热核或扩散核相似度，评估邻居在图结构上的相关性，并引导每个客户端仅选择部分邻居进行通信。

Result: 实验表明，基于拓扑信息的随机化选择效果不逊于均匀邻居抽样，并且准确率接近全邻居广播。数据异质性较强、图规模较大且结构较丰富时，最大准确率提升约三个百分点；相较于直接广播，传输消息总数减少百分之五十。

Conclusion: 利用图谱结构进行邻居筛选，可以在去中心化联邦学习中有效权衡通信成本与模型性能。该方法显著减少通信量，并在多种条件下维持接近全量广播的准确率，尤其适用于数据异质性较强且拓扑结构丰富的场景。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) has emerged as a scalable alternative to centralized federated learning, removing the single point of failure and communication bottleneck at the server. However, most existing decentralized approaches rely on full-neighborhood broadcasting, where each client transmits its model update to all of its neighbors in each communication round. While effective, this results in significant communication overhead and high energy consumption. This paper applies spectral graph tools as lightweight client selection methods that leverage the graph topology by analyzing the spectral properties of the graph Laplacian over a fixed communication graph. Specifically, we exploit eigenvector embeddings and heat/diffusion kernel similarities to guide each client in selecting only a subset of structurally relevant neighbors, i.e., those that are most beneficial for the convergence of the federation process. This topology aware randomization matches or outperforms uniform neighborhood subsampling and approaches the accuracy of full broadcasting. The largest gains, about $3$ percentage points, emerge under strong data heterogeneity on larger, structurally rich graphs, while the total number of transmitted messages is reduced by $50\%$ compared to naive broadcasting.

</details>


### [243] [Patch-Based Deep Kernel Learning for Data Assimilation and Bias Correction with Extremely Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41596)
*Emanuele Mele,Angelo Coluccia,Massimo Cafaro,Italo Epicoco,Paola Nassisi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将卷积神经网络与可学习核高斯过程结合的混合框架，从极稀疏观测中同时重建系统状态并校正模型偏差，还能提供不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分资料同化依赖人工设计且通常假设误差无偏的背景误差协方差，难以表示随状态变化的相关性和系统性模型偏差，计算成本也会随空间范围扩大。深度学习方法虽能进行非线性同化与偏差校正，但往往可解释性有限，也缺乏经过校准的不确定性估计。因此，需要兼具表达能力、效率与不确定性量化能力的方法。

Method: 将卷积神经网络与可学习核高斯过程端到端结合，通过最大化边际对数似然，从数据中学习覆盖整个网格的协方差，并联合完成资料同化和偏差校正。模型直接给出最大后验状态估计及其不确定性。训练时采用分块策略，在固定块大小下将精确高斯过程的训练复杂度从网格点数的三次方降至线性；推理时精确求解一个线性系统，复杂度取决于观测数而非网格规模。学习得到的协方差还可替代三维变分方法中的人工协方差，而不改变其变分形式。

Result: 在海表温度和盐度实验中，涵盖合成数据与真实的极稀疏原位观测，该方法优于三维变分、经典高斯过程及一种对比深度学习模型。相较三维变分，其推理时间减少超过百分之九十八。

Conclusion: 该混合框架以数据驱动的协方差学习弥补了传统资料同化对人工误差模型的依赖，同时保留高斯过程的不确定性量化能力，并通过分块训练和稀疏观测推理降低计算成本。实验结果表明，它有望高效提升极稀疏观测条件下的状态重建与模型偏差校正效果。

Abstract: Data Assimilation (DA) reconstructs the state of a spatio-temporal system by combining sensor observations with the output of a numerical model. Since model states are high-dimensional while observations cover only a small fraction of the domain, DA is a challenging large-scale estimation problem, central to weather forecasting and oceanography. Classical variational methods such as 3D-Var rely on a hand-crafted background error covariance that assumes unbiased errors, and therefore cannot capture state-dependent correlations or systematic model biases. Moreover, their cost grows with the size of the domain. Deep learning (DL) methods can perform DA and bias correction (BC) non-linearly, but typically offer limited interpretability and no calibrated uncertainty estimates. We propose a hybrid framework that couples a convolutional neural network (CNN) with a learnable-kernel Gaussian Process (GP) to jointly perform DA and BC from extremely sparse observations. The model is trained end-to-end by maximizing the marginal log-likelihood, learns a data-driven covariance over the whole grid, and provides both the Maximum A Posteriori analysis and its uncertainty by design. A patch-based strategy reduces the $O(N^3)$ training cost of exact GPs over $N$ grid points to $O(N)$ for a fixed patch size. Inference remains exact and only requires solving a linear system whose $O(m^3)$ cost depends on the number of observations $m \ll N$, not on the domain. The learned covariance can also replace the hand-crafted one in 3D-Var, improving its accuracy without changing the variational formulation. Experiments on sea surface temperature and salinity, with both synthetic and real, extremely sparse in-situ observations, show that the framework outperforms 3D-Var, a classical GP and a competing DL model, while reducing inference time by more than 98% with respect to 3D-Var.

</details>


### [244] [Real Computation or Merely Naming? A Pre-Registered Mechanism Audit of 25 Cognitive-Architecture Modules in an LLM-Based Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41604)
*Peter Chen,Ni Zhang,Ying Xu,Siyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一套可复用的静态审计方法，用于核实认知架构模块是否真正参与系统计算；对两个系统的审计发现，许多模块名义存在却未影响生产输出，而针对性修复能显著改善性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地大语言模型智能体常以“全局工作空间”“心智理论”“因果推理”等认知机制命名模块，但模块名称并不能证明相应计算实际发生。论文聚焦于这一“宣称与实现落差”，主张必须分别核验模块名称所表达的功能主张，以及代码是否真正实现并使用了该功能。

Method: 作者提出包含四个问题的静态审计流程，以代码行号作为证据并追踪调用链；同时针对模块预先登记可证伪预测，再通过干预检验。该流程先用于一个宣称具备二十五种认知机制的系统，其中十一种模块接受预测与干预检验；随后在另一独立系统的整合信息理论核心上复现，并预先设定该系统的证伪标准。此外，作者进行两项修复实验作为正向对照。

Result: 在首个系统的二十五个模块中，有十八个模块的计算结果从未进入任何生产输出。研究还识别出三种此前未见的闲置连线形态：已连接但从未调用、已调用但不起作用、已调用但不读取内容。在十二项干预的六十一条预先冻结标准中，五十三条得到确认，两条部分裁定，五条被证伪，一条因裁定标准不明确而无法定论。修复后，规划器任务成功率从百分之二点八升至百分之百，状态控制器错误率下降百分之五十二。

Conclusion: 模块被命名或接入代码，并不意味着其计算真正影响系统行为。论文表明，结合代码证据、调用链追踪、预先登记的可证伪标准和干预实验，可以系统检验这种落差；其审计流程与失效分类具有迁移价值，但现有证据主要来自两个案例，尚不能直接代表所有智能体系统。

Abstract: Agent systems built on local large language models are increasingly marketed through the vocabulary of cognitive architecture: modules named global workspace, theory of mind, causal reasoning, or active inference are listed as features, and the presence of the name is taken as the presence of the mechanism. We ask the prior question -- does the named computation actually happen -- and contribute a transferable verification method for the claim-implementation gap: a four-question static audit protocol with line-number evidence and call-chain tracing, demonstrated on two systems. We audit a system claiming 25 cognitive mechanisms, pre-register falsifiable predictions for 11 modules and execute the interventions, replicate the protocol on the IIT-phi core of a second, independent system with its own pre-committed falsification criteria, and run two repair experiments as positive controls. In 18 of 25 modules the computed value never reaches any production output; three idle-wiring shapes new to the dead-code and code-smell literatures emerge (connected-but-never-called, called-but-inert, called-but-content-blind). Of 61 frozen criteria across 12 interventions, 53 were confirmed, two partially adjudicated, five falsified, one adjudication-ambiguous. Repairs raised planner task success from 0.028 to 1.000 and cut state-controller error by 52%. Module names and module computations must be audited as independent claims; the protocol and the five-class failure taxonomy are reusable for the claim-implementation gap at two-system, case-level depth.

</details>


### [245] [What Should LLMs Forget: Reinforcement Unlearning of Knowledge Associations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41616)
*Wutong Xu,Yun Qu,Haiyan Feng,Qi Wang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出以“知识关联”为遗忘目标的强化学习框架，在更精细地抑制指定知识的同时，尽量保留其他知识与模型通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型遗忘方法通常直接优化完整回答，却没有明确定位需要遗忘的知识，因而容易造成遗忘粗糙、控制不足，并损害无关能力。论文希望将遗忘目标从整段回答细化为与特定上下文相关的目标知识。

Method: 论文将“知识关联”定义为查询上下文与目标知识之间的联系，并用相应的条件预测分布加以刻画。在此基础上提出知识关联强化遗忘框架：通过词元级奖励提供细粒度遗忘监督，通过分布级正则化精准压制目标知识关联，并采用邻近感知的保留样本采样，减少对无关知识和能力的连带损害。

Result: 在多种大语言模型和代表性基准上的实验表明，该方法相较现有基线能稳定提升遗忘效果，同时保持相近的知识保留表现和通用能力。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将知识关联而非完整回答作为遗忘对象，有助于实现更精确、可控的模型遗忘。所提出的统一强化学习框架兼顾目标知识抑制与其他知识及通用能力保留。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) acquire extensive factual knowledge during pre-training, which may include sensitive, private, or copyrighted information, motivating the development of unlearning methods that selectively remove specific knowledge while preserving general capabilities. However, existing approaches typically optimize entire responses without explicitly identifying the knowledge to be forgotten, resulting in coarse and uncontrolled forgetting. To address this, we revisit the forgetting target of LLM unlearning and introduce Knowledge Association (KA), which characterizes the association between a query context and its target knowledge and is instantiated by the corresponding conditional predictive distribution, as the fundamental target of forgetting. Based on KA, we propose Knowledge Association Reinforcement Unlearning (KARU), a unified reinforcement learning framework that suppresses forgotten knowledge associations while preserving retained ones. Specifically, KARU derives complementary mechanisms from the KA-based formulation: token-level reward construction for finer-grained forgetting supervision, distribution-level regularization for precise knowledge suppression, and proximity-aware retain sampling to mitigate collateral degradation. Experiments across diverse LLMs on representative benchmarks demonstrate that KARU consistently improves forgetting effectiveness over existing baselines while maintaining comparable retention performance and general utility.

</details>


### [246] [Chance-Constrained Inspection Viewpoint Sequencing for On-Orbit Servicing via Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41639)
*Chloé Carer,Adam Abdin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将航天器近距离观测视点规划建模为带约束的马尔可夫决策过程，并用带拉格朗日惩罚的近端策略优化学习策略；在满足风险上限和燃料预算的同时，实现接近最大值的目标覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨服务通常需要先自主检查目标航天器，但观测视点规划同时受覆盖范围、燃料消耗和碰撞安全约束，属于复杂的组合优化问题。传统方法常对禁入区半径设置固定安全裕度并进行确定性规划，难以灵活控制实际碰撞风险，也可能需要针对不同任务反复调整参数。

Method: 将视点选择建模为带约束的马尔可夫决策过程，由高层规划器在目标周围的观测球面上安排相机视点，低层模型则用克洛西—威尔特希尔相对运动方程评估视点间转移。单次脉冲可达性和速度增量上限直接用于筛除不可行动作；针对导航与机动误差导致的随机安全间隔，设置轨迹概率约束，并通过比例—积分—微分控制调节拉格朗日乘子。目标卫星以点云表示，视点可见性由视场范围和视线遮挡共同确定。

Result: 在中地球轨道的伽利略级导航卫星案例中，策略在不同风险预算下均将禁入区违规率控制在规定上限内，同时实现接近最大值的覆盖并遵守任务速度增量预算。与采用固定安全裕度的确定性规划相比，在覆盖水平相近时，该方法的违规率不到其一半。学习得到的策略可直接选择视点，无须像逐步贪心的概率约束规划器那样在每个决策点反复进行蒙特卡洛风险估计。

Conclusion: 该方法把碰撞风险作为可显式设定的设计参数，并将随机安全约束融入视点规划与策略训练，能够兼顾观测覆盖、燃料预算和安全要求。它减少了固定裕度调参及在线风险估计的需求，为自主近距离航天器检查提供了一种风险可控的规划方案。

Abstract: Autonomous inspection of a target spacecraft is typically the initial step in on-orbit servicing. Planning close-proximity observation viewpoints involves a complex combinatorial problem constrained by coverage, fuel, and safety considerations. This paper models the viewpoint selection as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP) and applies Proximal Policy Optimization with Lagrangian relaxation to solve it. The system combines a high-level planner, which sequences camera viewpoints on an observation sphere around the target, with a low-level model that assesses each transfer using the Clohessy--Wiltshire (CW) equations for relative motion. The deterministic constraints on single-impulse reachability and $Δv$ cap are enforced directly on the action set. Safety margins are affected by navigation and manoeuvre errors, making the actual clearance stochastic. To address this, a chance constraint on the realised trajectory is implemented, with a Lagrangian multiplier governed by PID control to adapt the penalty during training. The approach is tested on a Galileo-class navigation satellite in medium Earth orbit, modelled as a point cloud where visibility from each viewpoint considers field-of-view and line-of-sight occlusion. Across various risk budgets, the trained policy maintains the keep-out violation rate within the specified limit, achieving coverage close to its maximum and staying within the mission $Δv$ budget. Compared to the usual practice of applying a fixed margin to the keep-out radius and planning deterministically, the chance-constrained policy attains less than half the violation rate at comparable coverage. This makes the collision risk an explicit design parameter, eliminating the need for re-tuning per case. The learned policy selects a viewpoint without the repeated Monte Carlo risk estimates required by a greedy, chance-constrained planner at every decision point.

</details>


### [247] [Risk-Sensitive Mission Planning for Multi-Target On-Orbit Inspection via Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41647)
*Adam Abdin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向低地球轨道多目标在轨服务的风险敏感自适应规划方法。结果显示，风险规避策略可显著降低任务回报波动和任务失败概率，并在相近方差下接近可达到的最优平均回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标在轨服务任务会受到运行条件变化和结果不确定性的影响，而航天任务具有较高安全要求。由于有限的任务时间通常不足以访问所有目标，规划器既要决定目标访问顺序，也要权衡平均收益与任务风险；传统只优化期望收益的方法难以明确处理这种风险权衡。

Method: 研究采用分布式强化学习，学习任务回报的完整概率分布，并使用基于分位数回归的深度价值网络选择下一个巡检目标。策略通过均值—方差准则权衡预测回报的期望与波动。环境考虑倾角存在差异的圆轨道，并以埃德尔鲍姆低推力联合机动估算转移代价。观测信息包括轨道要素、访问历史、各目标的风险状态及剩余任务时间；目标风险状态会随机变化，因此巡检结果具有不确定性。研究还通过枚举可行访问顺序，精确计算不同回报方差下可实现的最高平均回报前沿，用于验证学习策略。

Result: 在包含五颗目标卫星的数值实验中，调整风险规避权重即可在不改变环境和奖励的情况下，获得不同的平均回报与回报方差组合。与风险中性策略相比，风险规避策略将单次任务回报的方差约降低一半，并降低任务损失概率，但平均回报有所下降。相同方差下，学习策略的平均回报接近精确计算的有效前沿；此外，风险规避策略的任务损失概率低于前沿策略。

Conclusion: 分布式强化学习结合均值—方差决策，可为多目标在轨服务提供可调节风险偏好的自适应规划方案。实验表明，该方法能有效权衡收益与风险，并在接近可达收益前沿的同时降低任务失败风险。

Abstract: Multi-target on-orbit servicing missions require planning tools that can adapt to evolving operational conditions while accounting for the outcome variability inherent to safety-critical spaceflight. This paper presents an adaptive planning approach for multi-target servicing campaigns in low Earth orbit and applies it to inspection missions flown by a low-thrust servicer. The approach is based on Distributional Reinforcement Learning, which learns the full distribution of mission returns and thereby supports explicit risk-sensitive decision-making at each planning step. Specifically, a Quantile-Regression Deep Q-Network (QR-DQN) agent selects which target to inspect next by jointly considering the expectation and the variance of the predicted return through a mean-variance criterion. The mission environment models circular orbits with a spread in inclination and uses Edelbaum's combined-manoeuvre low-thrust transfer estimates. The observation comprises the orbital elements, the visit history, the observed risk state of each target and the remaining mission-time budget. The risk state of each target changes stochastically during the campaign, making the outcome of an inspection uncertain and creating operationally meaningful distinctions between risk-neutral and risk-averse planning strategies. Because the mission-time budget generally admits only a subset of the candidate targets, the planner must also select which targets to inspect. Numerical experiments on scenarios with five target satellites show that one risk-aversion weight moves the policy across a range of mean return and return variance, over an unchanged environment and reward. Risk-averse agents reduce the variance of the episodic return by approximately one half relative to risk-neutral agents and lower the probability of a mission loss, at a cost in mean return. To validate the learned policies, the efficient frontier, which gives the highest mean return attainable at each level of return variance, is computed exactly by enumerating the admissible visit orders. At equal variance, the learned policies lie close to this frontier in mean return, and the risk-averse policies have a lower probability of a mission loss than the frontier policies.

</details>


### [248] [Breaking Information Islands in Sparse Tuning via Small-World Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41665)
*Xianchao Guan,Zijun Xiong,Yifeng Wang,Yubo Cui,Yaowei Wang,Guo-Sen Xie,Xin Li,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出小世界诱导微调，通过固定的特征散射排列增强稀疏参数更新中的跨组信息交互，在不增加可训练参数的情况下改善微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏微调的效果不仅取决于可训练参数数量，也取决于参数更新所连接的特征维度。连接受限或分布不均会形成彼此隔离的“信息孤岛”，削弱任务相关特征之间的直接交互。

Method: 该方法将块对角稀疏更新与固定、确定性的散射排列结合：前者在特征组内进行局部混合，后者将混合结果路由到不同特征组，促进跨组信息流动且不引入额外可训练参数。论文还从理论上分析了随机稀疏更新和块对角更新可能产生信息孤岛，并证明在特定结构条件下，具备充分散射能力的排列可保证正的块级扩张性。

Result: 在常识推理、自然语言生成和图像分类任务上的实验显示，该方法平均优于所评估的参数高效微调基线，并可达到或超过全量微调的表现。

Conclusion: 研究表明，稀疏微调的结构连接方式是影响性能的重要因素。通过在局部特征混合之外引入固定的跨组散射，可以改善信息连通性，在保持参数效率的同时提升任务适应能力。

Abstract: Sparse tuning is widely used to adapt large language models due to its parameter efficiency. However, its effectiveness depends not only on the number of trainable parameters, but also on the feature-interaction topology of the sparse update's structural support. Limited or uneven connectivity in this support may isolate subsets of feature dimensions, forming information islands that restrict direct task-specific interactions through the trainable update. To address this issue, we propose Small-World Induced Fine-Tuning (SWIFT), a sparse tuning framework inspired by small-world connectivity. SWIFT combines block-diagonal sparse updates for local feature mixing with a fixed deterministic scattering permutation that routes the mixed outputs across feature groups without additional trainable parameters. We further show theoretically that certain random sparse-update models and block-diagonal updates can exhibit information islands, whereas a sufficiently scattering permutation guarantees positive block-level expansion when the trainable block-diagonal factor consists of equal-sized, structurally dense square blocks. Extensive experiments on commonsense reasoning, natural language generation, and image classification demonstrate that SWIFT outperforms the evaluated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) baselines on average and can match or surpass full fine-tuning. Implementation is available at https://github.com/xianchaoguan/SWIFT

</details>


### [249] [Radial Kernel Attention for Long-Range Modeling in Polymer Machine Learning Force Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41675)
*Wenbo Fu,Chenghui Mao,Chengwen Hu,Haifeng Lv,Zhou Lv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出径向核注意力机制，以近线性成本实现聚合物原子间的全局信息传递，并改善测试结构上的能量与力预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物中的原子相互作用可能跨越不同链段及其周围环境。仅依赖局部邻居图会限制远距离信息交流，而显式计算所有原子对的注意力则具有随原子数平方增长的计算成本。因此，论文旨在以较低成本捕捉长程依赖。

Method: 径向核注意力根据各原子到簇质心的距离生成可学习的径向特征，并将特征重叠与依赖内容的查询、键相结合。通过因子化聚合与读出，在特征维度固定时以原子数线性增长的成本进行全局特征交换。该机制作为不变量标量更新插入等变局部网络模块之间；模型对总能量求导得到保守力，并将径向坐标依赖纳入求导。

Result: 在聚合物衍生有限簇数据集的官方验证集上，模型训练十二轮后直接评估。参数量匹配的五百万规模模型，相比基线将每原子能量平均绝对误差降低百分之六十七点四，将力平均绝对误差降低百分之三十六点三。二千万规模模型的总能量平均绝对误差为十五点八一毫电子伏特，力平均绝对误差为每埃二点六一九毫电子伏特，每原子能量平均绝对误差为零点一一八二毫电子伏特。

Conclusion: 径向核注意力提供了一条紧凑且计算高效的全局通信路径，在所评估的聚合物衍生簇上提升了能量和力预测表现。结果支持该方法用于聚合物机器学习力场中的长程建模，但结论目前限于论文所评估的数据集与实验设置。

Abstract: We introduce Radial Kernel Attention (RKA) for long-range modeling in polymer machine learning force fields. Local neighbor graphs constrain communication across polymer chains and their surrounding environments, while explicit all-pair attention incurs quadratic cost. RKA maps atomic distances from the cluster centroid to learned radial features and combines their overlap with content-dependent queries and keys. Factorized aggregation and readout enable global feature exchange at linear cost in atom count for fixed feature dimensions. We insert the invariant scalar update between EquiformerV3 local blocks and obtain conservative forces by differentiating the total energy, including its radial coordinate dependence. We evaluate energy and force prediction for finite polymer-derived clusters in OPoly26. Our models are trained on the official training split for 12 epochs and evaluated directly after training on the complete official validation split. A matched 5M comparison shows that RKA reduces per-atom energy and force MAEs by 67.4% and 36.3%, respectively. The 20M RKA model is compared with UMA-S-1.2 and UMA-S-1.2.1 on the same validation structures. The 20M model attains a total-energy MAE of 15.81 meV and a force MAE of 2.619 meV/angstrom; its per-atom energy MAE is 0.1182 meV/atom. RKA provides a compact global communication pathway that improves energy and force prediction on the evaluated polymer-derived clusters.

</details>


### [250] [Advancing Clinical Trial Outcome Prediction with Domain-Specific Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41710)
*Burak Bakkar,Hana Moalla,Mohamed Salem Jedidi,Haifa Ben Messaoud,Nicolas Lopez Carranza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，基于临床试验登记文本的生物医学预训练语言模型，在各试验阶段的结局预测中整体优于既有结构化模型；冻结模型、仅训练轻量分类器的探测方法表现接近微调，且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验失败会增加药物研发成本，并延迟患者获得有效疗法。既有的层次化交互网络通过建模药物、疾病和试验方案之间的关系来预测结局，但对临床试验登记中的自由文本利用有限。因此，论文评估领域预训练语言模型能否从登记文本中提取有助于结局预测的信息，并比较微调与探测两种使用方式。

Method: 研究使用包含一万二千四百七十七项试验的基准数据集，将每项试验的十个临床试验登记字段组织成文本，分别预测第一至第三期试验的结局。研究比较两种生物医学语言模型及两种训练策略：对较小模型进行全参数微调，对较大模型采用低秩适配；探测方法则冻结预训练模型，只用其文本表示训练轻量分类器。评价指标包括受试者工作特征曲线下面积、精确率—召回率曲线下面积和Ｆ１值，并与已发表的层次化交互网络结果比较。

Result: 四种语言模型方案在各阶段的测试集上，受试者工作特征曲线下面积为零点六一三至零点七七二，精确率—召回率曲线下面积为零点六四九至零点九零一，均高于既有层次化交互网络结果；精确率—召回率曲线下面积相对提升约百分之十至二十。第一、二期中，微调模型的曲线下面积指标最高；第三期中，冻结的大型模型表示效果最佳，受试者工作特征曲线下面积为零点七七二，精确率—召回率曲线下面积为零点九零一。探测与微调总体表现相近，多数差异低于零点零三，且未进行显著性检验。各模型的Ｆ１值差异较小且结果不一；包括既有模型在内，没有模型明确超过始终预测试验成功这一简单基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，生物医学预训练语言模型的文本表示已包含与临床试验结局相关的预测信号。对于该任务，冻结模型并训练轻量分类器可在性能接近微调的同时降低计算开销。不过，Ｆ１值未显示明确优势，模型间差异也未经显著性检验，因此仍需进一步验证其稳健性及临床实用价值。

Abstract: Clinical trial failures are a major barrier to efficient drug development, incurring substantial costs and delaying patient access to effective therapies. Structured approaches such as the Hierarchical Interaction Network (HINT) predict trial outcomes by explicitly modelling interactions between drugs, diseases and protocols, but make limited use of the free text in trial registrations. We evaluate two domain-specific language models, BioBERT (108M parameters) and BioMistral (7B parameters), for phase-level outcome prediction on the TOP benchmark (12,477 trials), representing each trial as text built from ten ClinicalTrials.gov fields. We compare fine-tuning (full fine-tuning for BioBERT, low-rank adaptation for BioMistral) with probing, in which the pretrained model is frozen and only a lightweight classifier is trained on its embeddings. On the held-out test set of each phase, all four language-model variants achieved higher ROC-AUC (0.613--0.772) and PR-AUC (0.649--0.901) than the published HINT results, corresponding to relative PR-AUC gains of 10--20\%. F1 differences were small and mixed, and no model, including HINT, clearly exceeded the F1 of a trivial classifier that predicts success for every trial. Under the configurations tested, probing performed comparably to fine-tuning: fine-tuned models scored highest on ROC-AUC and PR-AUC in Phases I and II, and frozen BioMistral embeddings in Phase III (ROC-AUC 0.772; PR-AUC 0.901), although most differences between variants were below 0.03 and were not tested for significance. These results suggest that pretrained biomedical representations already encode signal relevant to trial outcome, and that probing is a computationally cheaper alternative to fine-tuning for this task.

</details>


### [251] [Multimodal In-Context Learning with Explicit Rule Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41754)
*Yuquan Li,Xinyang Jiang,Yuhao Xue,Cairong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出显式规则归纳框架，通过让多模态大语言模型先从示例中归纳潜在规则，再据此完成任务，提升多模态上下文学习的表现与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多多模态上下文学习任务仅凭输入与输出难以定义，模型必须从示范中发现其隐含规则。现有方法主要学习直接的输入—输出映射，忽视了规则归纳与推理，因此表现受限，也难以解释模型如何得出答案。

Method: 该框架构建以规则为中心的多模态上下文学习数据集，并加入规则归纳的显式监督。训练采用两阶段策略：先通过混合冷启动监督微调建立规则归纳能力，再使用强化学习进一步优化模型，使其能够明确归纳示范背后的规则并据此作答。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多种多模态上下文学习设置中均带来稳定的性能提升；正确归纳潜在规则能够直接增强模型表现，并使其结果更加稳定。

Conclusion: 多模态上下文学习不应局限于拟合示范中的输入—输出关系。显式学习和运用潜在规则是一条有效路径，有望同时改善任务性能、稳定性与推理过程的可理解性。

Abstract: A central challenge in multimodal in-context learning (MICL) arises from tasks that are ill-defined without context and require uncovering the underlying rules from demonstrations. Existing methods predominantly treat MICL as direct input-output mapping, overlooking the importance of explicitly inducing and reasoning over such rules, limiting both performance and interpretability. We propose ERIM, Explicit Rule-Induction for Multimodal In-Context Learning, a unified framework that enhances MICL by explicitly inducing the latent rules governing demonstration samples. Leveraging the reasoning ability of multi-modal large language model (MLLM), ERIM constructs rule-centric MICL datasets, introduces explicit supervision for rule induction, and fine-tunes MLLM to perform explicit rule-induction via a two-stage training strategy that combines mixed cold-start supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments show consistent performance improvements across diverse MICL settings and demonstrate that correctly inducing underlying rules directly leads to stronger and more stable performance.

</details>


### [252] [Clinical-Protocol-Aware Cyber Resilience for Connected Clinical Technologies Under AI-Enabled Adversarial Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41784)
*Pranav Patel,Siva Kannathasan,Prerak Patel,Jigar Patel,Vansh Patel,Sheel Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合临床流程的医疗网络韧性框架，通过边界清晰的临床微网络及受控互联，协同设计预防、遏制与事件响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗设备、临床信息系统、云服务及分布式护理日益互联，扩大了网络攻击面。人工智能还可能加快漏洞发现与攻击路径分析，使攻击扩散风险上升。传统上若只衡量技术风险，可能忽略不同临床流程受损后果的差异，因此需要将临床背景纳入安全设计与评估。

Method: 作者提出临床协议感知的网络韧性框架，并以参数化蒙特卡洛模拟进行比较分析。模拟分别衡量技术网络暴露与建模所得的临床影响，考察网络连接架构、攻击者能力、安全控制、临床协议依赖、风险匹配、通信方向、生命周期整合及响应时机等因素。

Result: 在模拟假设下，互联架构的平均网络暴露比分段架构高百分之五十六点三；预防与遏制措施结合后，暴露比未实施控制时低百分之六十点一。协议依赖增强时，临床影响指数从一点零二一升至七点五六七，但平均网络暴露没有变化，说明技术暴露与临床后果可能并不一致。风险匹配实验也显示，当较高技术风险对应较高临床重要性时，建模的临床影响更大。上述结果仅支持架构间的比较，不是现实风险、控制效果或患者伤害的校准估计。

Conclusion: 该框架为互联医疗环境提供了一种纳入临床情境的安全边界设计、控制优先级排序及协同遏制思路。其核心启示是，网络安全评估应同时考虑技术暴露和临床后果，并依据临床协议依赖及风险重要性制定防护与响应策略。

Abstract: Healthcare increasingly relies on connected medical devices, clinical information systems, cloud services, and distributed-care technologies, creating cybersecurity risks across clinical workflows. AI-enabled capabilities can accelerate vulnerability discovery and attack-path analysis, increasing the need to limit compromise propagation. This paper proposes a clinical-protocol-aware cyber-resilience framework based on bounded clinical micro-networks and controlled interconnections. The framework integrates clinical factors into cybersecurity design, management, and evaluation, covering prevention, containment, and incident response. A parameterized Monte Carlo simulation distinguishes technical cyber exposure from modeled clinical consequence. Experiments investigate architectural connectivity, adversarial capability, security controls, protocol dependency, risk alignment, communication directionality, lifecycle integration, and response timing. Under the simulation assumptions, connected architecture increases mean cyber exposure by 56.3% relative to segmented architecture, while combined prevention and containment reduce exposure by 60.1% relative to no controls. Increasing protocol dependency raises the modeled clinical-impact index from 1.021 to 7.567 without changing mean cyber exposure, demonstrating that technical exposure and clinical consequence can diverge. Risk-alignment experiments further indicate higher modeled clinical impact when greater technical risk corresponds to greater clinical significance. The findings support comparative architectural analysis, not calibrated estimates of real-world cyber risk, control effectiveness, or patient harm. The framework provides a clinical-context-aware approach to security-boundary design, control prioritization, and coordinated containment in connected healthcare environments.

</details>


### [253] [Whose Values, Which Contexts, and What Signals? Grounding Cultural Value Alignment with Re3Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41793)
*Jing Yao,Hyunjin Hwang,Weihua Zheng,Bryan Chen Zhengyu Tan,Jia Wang Peh,Dylan Raharja,JinYeong Bak,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Rui Cao,Yuki Arase,Jacki O'Neill,Zhicheng Dou,Xing Xie,Xiaoyuan Yi,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出大规模、以真实人类证据为基础的文化价值数据集，系统研究文化价值对齐目标应代表谁、在哪些情境中观察，以及如何识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化价值对齐不仅取决于模型如何优化，也取决于对齐目标是否准确反映真实人群。现有研究对目标应依据哪些人群、场景和价值信号定义缺乏充分探索，且可能过度依赖抽象问卷、回答偏好或模型模拟，导致文化价值估计失真。

Method: 作者构建“再思文化”数据集，覆盖四种文化的七百余名参与者，并通过文化归属筛选和考虑人口特征的抽样提升代表性。社区成员参与挑选家庭、亲属关系等文化敏感场景。数据包括一千四百余份价值调查、两万余条基于具体主题的行为原则，以及针对六千余个情境问题收集的回答和明确价值优先级。研究进一步比较不同抽样方式、情境信息和价值信号对价值估计的影响。

Result: 分析表明：第一，估计文化价值既要考虑文化归属，也要覆盖群体内部的多样性；以其他文化替代或缩窄子群体覆盖会改变估计，语言模型模拟还会偏向特定子群体。第二，价值应在社区认可的具体情境中观察；抽象问卷对情境化优先级的预测能力较弱，不同社区认定的价值显现情境也不相同。第三，应直接询问价值优先级；回答偏好会混入价值认同之外的因素，而相似答案可能源于不同的价值取舍。

Conclusion: 该研究指出，可靠的文化价值对齐需要以具有代表性的人群为基础，在社区认定的现实情境中观察价值，并明确 eliciting（直接 elicitation）价值优先级，而不能仅凭抽象画像、回答偏好或最终答案推断。所构建的数据集为文化价值对齐目标的定义提供了人类实证依据。

Abstract: Cultural value alignment requires not only model optimization but also a well-specified target grounded in real-human evidence. Yet how such targets should be grounded in human data remains largely unexplored, raising three fundamental questions: (1) whose values should be represented? (2) where should cultural values be observed? and (3) what signals reveal underlying values? We investigate these questions through Re3Culture, a large-scale dataset linking (i) Representative communities: 700+ human participants across four cultures recruited through cultural-affiliation screening and demographic-aware sampling; (ii) Realistic scenario: culturally sensitive scenarios selected by community members, e.g., family and kinship; and (iii) Revealed value signals: 1,400+ value surveys, 20,000+ topic-grounded behavioral principles and explicit value priorities with responses for 6,000+ situated questions. Our analysis shows why these choices matter for defining the target, with three implications. First, estimating cultural values requires both community affiliation and broad internal coverage. Substituting other cultures or restricting subgroup coverage shifts value estimates. LLM simulations also disproportionately reflect particular subgroups. Second, values must be observed in concrete, community-guided situations. Abstract questionnaire profiles weakly recover situated priorities, and communities differ in which scenarios they identify as culturally value-revealing. Third, value priorities should be elicited explicitly, not learned from response preferences or bottom-line answers alone. Preferences conflate value agreement with other response-level qualities, and similar answers can be derived from different value priorities. By addressing these requirements, Re3Culture provides the first human-grounded resource that explicitly informs target definition for cultural value alignment.

</details>


### [254] [RECAST: REconstruction of Chain-of-Thought via Auditable Structured Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41813)
*Qi Wang,Kang Xu,Chengcheng Wan,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出重构式框架「重构」，仅依据问题和闭源模型的最终答案生成结构化推理文本，并整理为可用于监督微调的数据；它重构的是合理解释，不是提取到模型真实的隐藏思维链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源推理模型通常只返回最终答案，现有思维链提取方法依赖可见推理字段、工具调用暂存内容或可重放的加密推理块。作者在标准聊天接口上复现三种代表性方法，发现它们对「克劳德·桑内特五点五」均未提取到任何推理词元；原因在于接口和安全策略从结构上阻断了这些侧信道。因此，论文转而探索不依赖隐藏推理通道的重构方案。

Method: 「重构」以问题和目标模型的最终答案为条件，生成第一人称、结构化的推理说明，再对文本进行清理，最终构建可供监督微调使用的数据。该流程通过一个提示即可作为智能体技能部署并运行。其核心是根据输入与答案重建一份推理文本，而非访问或恢复目标模型内部实际产生的推理过程。

Result: 作者在「克劳德·桑内特五点五」上实现该流程，并报告在仅提供最终答案的协议下，对「吉皮提六点一」和「双子座三」也获得了定性上成功的重构。论文还提供了受控的产物验证。不过，摘要未报告大规模基准测试结果，也未声称提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一条绕开隐藏推理通道的替代路径：利用问题和最终答案合成可用于训练的推理说明。其贡献主要在系统设计、威胁模型与受控验证，而非证明重构文本等同于真实思维链，或证明其能带来普遍的基准性能提升。

Abstract: Large closed-source reasoning models expose only their final answers. The intermediate reasoning that produces those answers is hidden behind API surfaces, tool-call policies, and safety filters. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) extraction methods assume a side channel: a visible reasoning field, a scratchpad carried through tool calls, or a signed/encrypted reasoning block. We reproduce three representative state-of-the-art extraction methods and find that, on Claude-Sonnet-5.5 accessed through the standard chat-completions interface, all three return zero CoT tokens. The failure is structural rather than a bug: the reasoning field is absent, named tool selection is rejected, free-text scratchpad arguments are filtered, and no replayable reasoning block is exposed. We therefore take a different route. We present RECAST, a reconstruction-based framework that conditions a decoder on the question and the target model's final answer, reconstructs a first-person structured rationale, cleans it, and builds SFT-ready data. To the best of our knowledge, RECAST is the first system to reconstruct CoT from frontier closed models such as Claude-Sonnet-5.5, GPT-6.1, and Gemini 3 without using hidden reasoning channels. We instantiate RECAST on Claude-Sonnet-5.5 and additionally obtain successful qualitative reconstructions on GPT-6.1 and Gemini 3 with the same answer-only protocol. The system is open-source and packaged as an agent skill: one prompt deploys it and runs the complete pipeline to produce SFT-ready CoT data. This preprint describes the system, its threat model, and a controlled artifact validation; it does not claim large-scale benchmark improvements.

</details>


### [255] [ReuScope: Auditing Data Reuse and Evaluation Sensitivity in Capsule Vision 2024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41835)
*Eunseob Choi,Kyeonghun Kim,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出一套审计数据再利用与评估敏感性的方案，并以胶囊内镜视觉数据集为例，区分数据重叠事实与性能变化，避免将分数下降直接解释为数据泄漏造成的虚高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集训练集与验证集之间可能存在重复文件或共同来源，但检测到重叠并不等于证明评估分数因此虚高。研究旨在明确区分可观测的数据复用、评估人群变化带来的影响，以及移除相关训练数据后的条件性能变化，并为每项结论界定适用人群、类别支持和不确定性单位。

Method: 提出再利用审计方案，测量训练集与验证集的数据关系，并进行两类比较：固定模型预测、改变评估人群；固定评估集、改变训练数据。案例分析检查文件级重复和来源录像跨集情况，以十组成对随机种子比较移除相关训练样本前后的表现；另设置样本量与类别数量匹配的对照，并开展探索性分析，比较移除近邻训练样本与移除等量远距离训练样本的影响。

Result: 训练集与验证集之间存在字节完全相同的文件；产生验证帧的四十一段相关录像也全部出现在两个分区中，但缺少跨录像的患者标识，无法计算独立重叠患者数。移除归属于相关数据源的全部训练样本及五个近似匹配样本后，公共验证集平衡准确率由百分之八十二点五降至百分之六十一点二；但在其他来源的验证样本上评估相同模型时，移除样本反而使平衡准确率提高一点三八个百分点。样本量和类别数本身不足以解释这些差异。探索性分析得到真实类别概率的差异中之差估计为负零点三五五个百分点，而准确率的配对检验区间包含零。

Conclusion: 该方案强调，公共验证集上的分数下降反映模型对特定来源训练数据移除的敏感性，不能据此量化数据泄漏导致的分数虚高。数据审计应同时报告重叠证据、评估人群、类别支持及不确定性，并谨慎区分观察到的数据复用与其对性能的条件性影响。

Abstract: We develop ReuScope, a protocol for auditing data reuse and evaluation sensitivity, using Capsule Vision 2024 as a case study. It combines measurements of data relationships with two comparisons: changing the evaluation population while holding predictions fixed, and changing training data while holding evaluation fixed. Byte-identical files cross the public training-validation boundary, and all 41 Kvasir-Capsule recordings that contribute frames occur in both partitions. Shared source recordings establish cross-split overlap; missing cross-recording patient identifiers prevent counting unique shared patients. Across ten paired seeds, removing all training rows attributed to Kvasir-Capsule, plus five rows from other sources flagged as near-matches to it, lowers public-validation balanced accuracy from 82.5% to 61.2%. Evaluating the same fitted models on the validation rows of the other sources reverses the comparison: removal improves balanced accuracy by 1.38 percentage points. The public-validation drop measures sensitivity to source removal, not an amount of leakage-induced inflation. Matched controls show that sample size and class counts alone do not explain the observed score differences. An exploratory experiment removes the training neighbors of validation frames that have close training matches, or equally many distant training rows, and yields a difference-in-differences estimate, relative to matched unflagged frames, of -0.355 percentage points in true-class probability; the paired-t interval for accuracy spans zero. The resulting procedure distinguishes observed reuse from conditional performance sensitivity and specifies the population, class support and uncertainty unit for each conclusion.

</details>


### [256] [Dynamical Memory Transformer: Selective Recurrent Memories with Constant Cost Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41850)
*Yannis Bendi-Ouis,Xavier Hinaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出动力学记忆变换器，将序列压缩为固定大小的动态记忆，再通过注意力读取；其语言建模表现接近变换器，但仍落后于曼巴模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变换器能直接访问完整历史表示，但上下文越长，键值缓存和逐词生成的计算成本越高。状态空间模型将历史压缩为固定大小的递归状态，效率更高，却降低了对特定历史信息的寻址能力。论文旨在兼顾高效压缩与灵活读取。

Method: 模型将序列编码与信息读取分开。编码器由多个记忆单元组成，每个单元以具有复数对角递归矩阵的动力系统更新，并由输入相关的泄漏率调节更新幅度。解码器通过注意力读取并融合记忆单元的输出，而不访问完整词元历史。该递归形式支持关联扫描：总计算量随序列长度线性增长，并行深度随序列长度对数增长；自回归推理时，递归状态和卷积缓存的大小保持固定。

Result: 在认知任务和语言建模任务上，动力学记忆变换器在若干认知任务中取得最佳结果，语言建模表现接近变换器，但整体仍不及曼巴模型。在选择性复制任务中，其对更长序列的外推能力强于曼巴模型和变换器。

Conclusion: 并行动力学记忆单元为序列信息的压缩、保留与读取提供了一种有潜力的机制，在效率和信息可访问性之间取得折中。不过，实验显示其语言建模能力仍有提升空间，尤其需要缩小与曼巴模型的差距。

Abstract: Transformers retain an explicit history of the sequence, allowing attention to directly retrieve past representations. However, the size of the KV-Cache and the cost of a new token increase with the context length. State Space Models compress the past into a fixed-size recurrent state, at the cost of reduced addressability. We present the Dynamical Memory Transformer (DMT), a hybrid architecture that separates sequence encoding from information reading. The encoder uses several memory units, each implemented by a dynamical system with a complex diagonal recurrent matrix. An input-dependent leak rate controls their updates. The decoder then uses attention to read and combine their outputs, without going through the token history. The formulation of the recurrence allows an associative scan with O(T ) total work and O(log T ) parallel depth, with fixed dimensions. During autoregressive inference, the recurrent states and the convolution cache remain fixed in size. We compare DMT with Mamba and Transformer on cognitive and language modeling tasks, and show its strengths and limitations: DMT achieves the best results on several cognitive tasks and language modeling performance close to that of Transformer, but remains behind Mamba in language modeling. On selective copy, DMT also shows stronger length extrapolation than Mamba and Transformer. These results demonstrate that parallel dynamical memory units are a promising mechanism for retaining and using information over sequences.

</details>


### [257] [Learning to Interact and Direct: Co-Evolving Video Director Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41865)
*Yingzhao Jian,Zhongan Wang,Yuxuan Hou,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种由导演智能体、模拟用户和元工程师共同驱动的协同进化框架，通过主动学习外部知识，提升视频制作执行与用户交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频导演智能体研究主要关注剧本规划的自我进化，却较少考虑导演能力与用户交互之间的相互促进。交互能帮助系统理解创作意图、改进工作流程；执行更可靠则能使协作从排错转向创意决策。以往方法依赖元智能体被动归因问题，难以适应更广泛的交互模式和制作技巧。

Method: 框架由导演智能体、模拟用户和元工程师构成。模拟用户提出创作任务以探测导演的弱点，元工程师依据任务过程记录调整交互与制作策略。进一步引入协同进化机制，使元工程师主动学习外部知识，以增强问题发现和方案提出能力。三方由此形成持续迭代的闭环，共同优化导演与交互能力。

Result: 在测试集和分布外测试集上，相较初始流程，该框架的制作评分和交互评分分别提升百分之十九点一和百分之三十二。消融实验显示，制作能力与交互能力的提升存在关联；协同进化机制还促进了导演技能和交互方式的探索。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将导演执行、用户交互与主动知识学习纳入同一协同进化闭环，有望同时提升视频制作质量和人机协作能力。

Abstract: Video director agents orchestrate multistage production to realize users' creative visions. Emerging research on their self-evolution advances screenplay planning effectively. However, the mutual reinforcement between directing and user interaction remains largely overlooked. Improved interaction clarifies user intent and informs workflow refinement, while more reliable execution shifts collaboration from troubleshooting to creative decisions. To this end, we introduce LIT (Learning to Interact and DirecT), a framework comprises a director agent, a simulated user, and a meta engineer. Simulated user assigns creative tasks to probe the director's weaknesses, while the meta engineer uses the task traces to revise the director's interaction and production strategy. Although this approach can address discovered interaction and production issues, it still relies on passive attribution by the meta agent. This limits its ability to adapt to broader user interaction patterns and production techniques. To solve this problem, we equip LIT with a co-evolution paradigm. The meta agent proactively learns external knowledge, thereby improving its ability to discover problems and propose solutions. The meta agent, simulated user, and director together form an evolutionary closed loop, collaboratively enhances the system's directing and interaction capabilities. Across the Test and OOD Test splits, LIT improves production and Interaction scores by 19.1% and 32.0%, respectively, relative to the initial pipeline. Controlled ablations reveal the correlation between improvements in production and interaction, where the co-evolution mechanism further encourage directing skill and interaction mode exploration.

</details>


### [258] [Fuzzy selective state model for forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41879)
*P. A. Golovinski,A. A. Drobyshev,A. O. Shatalova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于选择性状态空间模型的消费者偏好预测方法，以三角模糊数表示偏好，并在保持模糊性可解释的同时提升预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售客户的偏好会随时间变化，也可能受到竞争对手促销和客户转移等因素影响。仅预测一个确定数值，无法表达偏好估计本身的不确定程度；而反复进行模糊运算又可能导致不确定性逐渐膨胀。论文旨在兼顾动态预测精度与模糊程度的可解释性，并避免模糊性随时间累积。

Method: 将客户偏好表示为三角模糊数，并以隐藏状态同样为三角模糊数的选择性状态空间模型描述其演化。模型使用马特维耶夫的模糊数二分量代数进行计算；递推参数由确定性特征计算，模糊部分则保持由非负系数组成的线性结构。作者证明，在这些条件下，状态始终是合法的三角模糊数，并且是历史观测的凸组合，因此预测模糊性不会随时间累积，相关代数计算也与扎德扩展原理一致。论文还利用预测结果的语言化表达识别客户是否永久转向竞争对手。

Result: 在包含竞争对手促销和客户转移的合成示例中，一个仅含一百零七个参数的轻量模型，对预测中心的平均绝对误差为零点一五四，优于恒定惯性演化算子的零点一六三和朴素预测的零点一七九。其预测精度与相同结构的确定性模型相当，同时还能提供对预测模糊程度的语言化解释，并用于检测客户永久转向竞争对手的情形。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过对模糊状态演化施加非负线性约束，可以构建兼具动态建模能力、稳定模糊性和可解释性的消费者偏好预测模型。合成实验支持其在预测精度上的竞争力，也展示了表达预测不确定性和识别客户转移的潜在价值；不过，摘要所述验证基于合成示例，实际零售数据上的效果仍有待检验。

Abstract: We consider forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences of retail chain customers. A customer's preference is described by a triangular fuzzy number. The evolution of the preference over time is governed by a Mamba-type selective state space model whose hidden state is a triangular number, with computations carried out in M.G.~Matveev's two-component algebra of fuzzy numbers. The recurrence parameters are computed from crisp features, while the fuzzy part of the model remains linear with non-negative coefficients. We show that the state then always remains a proper triangular number and is a convex combination of past observations, so that the fuzziness of the forecast does not accumulate over time and the algebraic computations coincide with Zadeh's extension principle. On a synthetic example with competitor promotions and customer switching, a lightweight 107-parameter model reaches a mean absolute error of the forecast centre of 0.154 versus 0.163 for the constant-inertia evolution operator and 0.179 for the naive forecast, matches a crisp model of the same architecture and additionally provides a linguistically interpretable estimate of forecast vagueness. We also show how the linguistic readout of the forecast can be used to detect a permanent switch of a customer to a competitor.

</details>


### [259] [Automated Keyword Generation Using LLMs in a High-Volume Data Virtualization Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41893)
*Saiful Khan,Yiwen Xing,Joyraj Chakraborty,Afrah Farea,Philip Beaucamp,Samuel Jackson,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用大语言模型为数据虚拟化服务中的虚拟数据集自动生成关键词，帮助用户发现已有数据集并减少重复工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据虚拟化服务可根据不同任务对源数据进行选择、整合和预处理，由此产生大量虚拟数据集。用户需要频繁查找可复用的数据集，但缺少关键词会使检索困难，因此有必要自动为虚拟数据集生成关键词。

Method: 研究提出五种策略，从不同集合的文本描述中选取内容交由大语言模型生成关键词，并在五个使用不同源数据集的应用案例中进行实验。实验比较了三种关键词生成模型和两种文本嵌入技术。

Result: 五种策略的表现取决于文本描述的质量。实验结果为在扩展的数据虚拟化服务中采用多策略关键词生成方案提供了依据。

Conclusion: 自动关键词生成有助于提升数据集丰富且访问频繁的数据虚拟化服务的检索能力，是支持用户发现和复用虚拟数据集的重要功能。

Abstract: In modern data infrastructure, a data virtualization service (DVS) allows users to create many versions of source datasets for different processing tasks. For example, a multivariate or multi-modality dataset can be used for different machine learning tasks (e.g., classification, detection, segmentation, forecasting, and so on). Data selection, participation, integration, and preprocessing in different tasks result in many virtual datasets [Khan et al., SSC 2026]. Meanwhile, different users need to discover existing virtual datasets frequently and easily to avoid duplicate efforts. To enable an effective search engine in a DVS, it is desirable to automatically generate keywords for each virtual dataset. In this work, we present a solution for generating keywords for virtual datasets using large language models (LLMs). We propose five strategies for selecting different collections of text data for processing by the LLMs. We applied the five strategies to virtual datasets created in five application case studies with different source datasets. We conducted experiments with three LLM generators and two embedding techniques. The experiments show that the performances of the five strategies depend on the quality of the text descriptions. The results help us adopt a multi-strategy implementation of a new keyword generation service (KGS) as part of an extended DVS. The work demonstrates that the capability of automated keyword generation is indispensable for a high-volume DVS that is dataset-rich and frequently accessed.

</details>


### [260] [Using Machine Learning to Measure Self-Efficacy Belief Through Learners' Interaction Traces in Technology-Enhanced Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41900)
*Maeva Somny,Linda Chouati,Marie Lefevre*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探索利用学习者在技术增强学习系统中的交互轨迹，识别其低自我效能信念，并强调持续监测的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我效能信念会影响学习表现、学业选择及未来职业发展。若能尽早发现学习者自我效能偏低，便可及时采取补救措施；然而，利用技术增强学习系统中的行为数据检测自我效能仍缺乏充分研究。

Method: 研究在两所大学开展实验，收集学习者完成练习时的系统交互轨迹及其自我效能自我报告数据。随后运用数据挖掘与机器学习方法建立分类流程，比较多种分类模型，并分析可指示低自我效能的特征。

Result: 论文报告了初步结果，指出对学习者自我效能进行密切监测有助于提升预测表现。研究还强调，自我效能会在学习过程中动态变化，因此仅依赖静态测量可能不足。摘要未提供具体模型指标或数值结果。

Conclusion: 该研究初步展示了依据学习交互数据检测低自我效能的可行方向，并为在技术增强学习环境中及早支持学习者提供了依据。后续工作应进一步验证模型，并更充分地刻画自我效能随学习过程变化的特征。

Abstract: Self-efficacy beliefs play a critical role in learners' performance, academic choices, and, more broadly, in their future career trajectories. As Technology-Enhanced Learning (TEL) environments have demonstrated strong potential for supporting both performance and motivation, investigating self-efficacy within such environments is of particular interest, especially for implementing remedial practices as early as possible to support learners' self-efficacy beliefs. Our research aims to develop a system capable of detecting low self-efficacy based on learners' interaction traces while they solve exercises in a TEL system. In this paper, we present initial results and future perspectives on a detection approach based on data mining and machine learning techniques to identify features indicative of low self-efficacy beliefs. Because the detection of learners' self-efficacy beliefs through their use of TEL systems remains largely unexplored, this work is exploratory in nature. To this end, we conducted an experiment across two universities to collect interaction traces and self-reported measures of self-efficacy. We then developed a machine learning pipeline comprising multiple classification models, enabling us to highlight the importance of closely monitoring of learners' self-efficacy beliefs for improved prediction performance, given their highly dynamic nature during learning.

</details>


### [261] [ReX-GG: a LLM Ensemble Pipeline for Relation-extraction and Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41901)
*Giacomo Magnifico,Eduard Barbu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种轻量、可定制的多模型协作流程，利用结构化输出与匿名互评筛选关系抽取结果，并将结果呈现为交互式因果图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型集成方案常依赖多阶段流程和额外的答案排序模块，架构较重，且各环节可能失效；较轻量的方案又容易传播幻觉，并缺乏对复杂输出的控制。因此，论文希望在控制成本与复杂度的同时，提高结果可靠性和可控性。

Method: 构建由多个大语言模型协同工作的模块化流程，以JSON格式约束模型输出，并通过匿名同行评审式排序筛选候选结果，以降低幻觉传播和单模型失效的影响。论文将该流程用于英文科普文章的关系抽取，识别强因果、弱因果、对比和组合四类本体约束关系；随后对语义节点进行规范化，并生成带颜色编码、可交互的HTML因果图。该架构可适配基于API或本地部署的大语言模型。

Result: 在针对该系统的用户研究中，平均感知准确率为0.778，评估参照为人工标注的黄金标准。论文摘要未提供与其他方法的直接对比数据或具体实验规模。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了以结构化输出和匿名互评为核心的轻量级多模型集成方案，可用于关系抽取及因果图生成，并兼顾模块化部署与可复现性。作者开放发布了完整框架，以促进复现和协作研究。

Abstract: Current LLM ensemble frameworks focus on multi-step setups with additional modules for answer ranking, often opting for token and span analysis rather than structured outputs, leading to heavyweight architectures with potential fail states along the pipeline. Faster, lighter solutions are more vulnerable to hallucination propagation and can lack output control in more complex pipelines. This paper proposes a customisable, lightweight ensemble workflow of coordinated Large Language Models that leverages JSON-structured outputs and anonymous peer-review ranking to mitigate hallucinatory outputs and single-model failure points. The pipeline is demonstrated on a relation extraction task applied to popular science articles in English, targeting four ontologically-grounded relation types (strong causation, weak causation, contrastive, and compositional), with semantic node canonicalisation and interactive, colour-coded HTML causal graphs as the final output. Performance is evaluated through an anonymous user study, achieving an average perceived accuracy of 0.778 against a human-annotated gold standard. The modular architecture supports flexible deployment across both API-based and in-house LLM setups, and the full framework is released under an open license to foster reproducibility and collaborative research.

</details>


### [262] [Structure-Aware Skill Retrieval and Arbitration for Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41909)
*Anran Liu,Benzhao Hou,Zongsheng Cao,Daichao Wang,Lang Chen,Shitong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以问题原型为中心的优化技能智能体，通过改进技能表示与结构化检索，提升自然语言优化任务在不同表述下的求解稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体虽能将自然语言优化任务转化为可由求解器验证的答案，但面对叙述方式改写时仍较脆弱，且常按具体实例而非可复用的问题类型存储经验，导致技能检索和迁移效果受限。

Method: 作者在既有优化技能框架上加入两个面向检索的模块：一是质心感知表示学习，通过低秩原型残差优化缓存技能表示的排序；二是签名引导检索，为技能附加可扩展维度的结构签名，依据加权杰卡德重叠度筛选候选，并将语义重排限制在前若干名之内。此外，系统采用保留基线的仲裁规则，决定是否以结构化检索路径替代原有选择器。

Result: 在使用相同的大语言模型骨干和已发布技能库的条件下，该方法在五个基准上的微平均首次通过率达到百分之六十五，相比原有优化技能框架提高一点六四个百分点；宏平均提高二点六八个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，围绕问题原型组织经验，并结合结构签名检索与受限语义重排，有助于提升优化技能的复用能力和求解表现。摘要报告了总体基准提升，但未提供各基准的具体结果或消融实验细节。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can turn natural-language optimization tasks into solver-checked answers, but current agents remain brittle under narrative rewrites and often store reusable experience at the instance level. We introduce \textsc{ArchOR}, an archetype-centric skill agent that builds on \textsc{OptSkills} by adding two retrieval-focused modules. \emph{Centroid-Aware Representation Learning} (CARL) refines the ordering of cached skill representations through a low-rank prototype residual. \emph{Signature-Guided Retrieval} (S\textsuperscript{3}R) attaches open-dimensional structural signatures to skills, prioritizes candidates by weighted Jaccard overlap, and bounds semantic re-ranking to a top-$K$ set. A baseline-preserving arbitration rule determines when the structural route should replace the original selector. With the same DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash backbone and released skill library, \textsc{ArchOR} reaches 65.00\% Micro-Avg.\ Pass@1 across five benchmarks, improving over \textsc{OptSkills} by 1.64~pp Micro-Avg.\ and 2.68~pp Macro-Avg.

</details>


### [263] [Sensorium: An Open-Source Multimodal Data Collection and Curation Library for Embodied Surgical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41946)
*Lorenzo Mazza,Ariel Rodriguez,Stefanie Speidel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出开放源代码工具库“森索里姆”，用于采集、处理、标注和整理多模态手术数据，并在多种实验条件下进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身手术人工智能依赖可靠的多模态数据，但手术数据采集成本高、难以重复，且高度依赖硬件配置。因此需要一种灵活、可复用的数据管理工具，以降低采集和整理数据的难度。

Method: 作者开发了一个基于机器人操作系统及其第二代中间件的开放源代码派森工具库，可接入可配置的机器人与传感器数据流。工具库涵盖面向操作者的数据采集、可配置的数据处理、语义标注，以及按操作片段生成数据集等功能。

Result: 研究使用该工具库评估了共一千五百一十六次记录，覆盖仿体、离体和活体猪实验、多种手术任务及传感配置，并收集了初步的可用性反馈。摘要未提供具体性能指标或详细反馈结果。

Conclusion: 该工具库旨在为多样化手术机器人与传感器配置提供统一的数据采集和整理流程，促进可重复的多模态手术数据构建。现有评估显示其已用于多种实验场景，但摘要所述结果主要是初步验证，尚不足以判断其相对其他方案的性能优势。

Abstract: Embodied surgical AI requires reliable multimodal data, yet surgical data collection remains costly, difficult to repeat, and strongly dependent on the hardware setup. We present Sensorium, an open-source Python library for recording, processing, annotating, and curating multimodal surgical data from configurable robot and sensor streams using the Robot Operating System (ROS) and ROS2 middleware. The library combines operator-facing data acquisition, configurable processing, semantic annotation, and episodic dataset generation. We evaluate Sensorium across 1516 recordings of phantom, ex vivo, and in vivo porcine experiments involving multiple surgical tasks and sensing configurations, and report preliminary usability feedback.

</details>


### [264] [Optimizing Stochastic Policy Geometry for Few-Step Flow Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41949)
*Zhuguanyu Wu,Congjia Chen,Ruihao Gong,Yang Yong,Shiqiao Gu,Yi Xu,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向少步流模型的强化学习方法：为每个去噪转移选择几何性质更优的随机策略，并校准不同时间步的训练权重；在四步图像与视频生成实验中取得更高奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步流模型每次去噪跨越较大的噪声范围。将组相对策略优化用于这类模型时，必须在去噪转移中引入随机性，而随机策略的具体选择会显著影响优化。已有方法虽要求策略保持目标流系数，但可行策略并不唯一，也缺少选择准则；此外，不同时间步的优化尺度不一致。

Method: 提出几何优化少步强化学习方法。在固定条件状态和时间步组合下，从保持流系数的各向同性高斯转移策略中，选择使任意非零速度输出更新所引起的条件相对熵最小的策略。该准则可化为最小化一个标量几何因子；此因子同时决定速度空间费舍尔信息和条件转移对数似然比的方差。作者推导出唯一闭式最优策略，其转移系数同时取决于源噪声与目标噪声。针对几何因子随时间步变化的问题，方法还采用梯度尺度匹配，并推导策略梯度权重与参考策略相对熵权重的闭式形式。训练沿用原有少步时间表，推理时仍使用确定性求解器。

Result: 在四步图像生成和视频生成实验中，该方法取得的最终奖励分数均高于流模型组相对策略优化和流模型系数保持策略两种基线。摘要未提供具体分数或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在少步流模型强化学习中，随机转移策略的几何结构是重要设计因素。通过最小化条件相对熵、并校准不同时间步的优化尺度，可以在不改变训练时间表和推理求解器的前提下提升生成奖励表现。

Abstract: Few-step flow models generate samples through a handful of large denoising transitions. Applying GRPO to such models requires introducing stochasticity into the denoising transitions, and when a single transition spans a wide range of noise levels, the choice of the resulting stochastic policy becomes a major factor in optimization. Recent constructions constrain the policy to preserve the target flow coefficients, but this constraint admits a family of policies and offers no criterion for choosing among them. We introduce Geo-FSRL (Geometry-Optimized Few-Step Reinforcement Learning), built on a selection principle: at a fixed conditioning state and timestep pair, choose the admissible policy that minimizes the conditional KL divergence induced by any given nonzero velocity-output update. Within a coefficient-preserving family of isotropic Gaussian transitions, this criterion reduces to minimizing a single scalar geometry factor that determines the conditional KL for every velocity-output update. The same factor governs velocity-space Fisher information and conditional transition log-likelihood-ratio variance. We derive the unique closed-form optimizer within this family, shared across all nonzero update magnitudes and directions, with transition coefficients determined by both the source and target noise levels. Because the minimized geometry factor still varies across timesteps, we further adopt gradient-scale matching from prior work and derive closed-form policy-gradient and KL weights that normalize explicit score scales and reference-KL curvature. Geo-FSRL trains on the original few-step schedule and retains the deterministic solver at inference. Our method achieves higher final reward scores than Flow-GRPO and Flow-CPS on both image generation and video generation models under the 4-step setting. Code is available at https://github.com/ModelTC/LightX2V/tree/main/lightx2v_rl

</details>


### [265] [Prune Gradually, Adapt Sparsely: Efficient Pruning of Large Language Models at Extreme Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41968)
*Jean Lucien Randrianantenaina,Hung Nguyen,Nasib Ullah,Jinbin Zhang,Nilesh Gupta,Inderjit S. Dhillon,Rohit Babbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种渐进剪枝与稀疏适配方法，在显著降低计算和存储成本的同时，提升模型在高稀疏率下的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型剪枝可降低存储与推理开销，但稀疏率很高时往往难以保持模型质量。一次性剪枝成本低，却容易造成质量大幅下降；直接从稠密模型优化剪枝掩码虽效果较好，却需要接近模型规模的优化器状态和辅助变量，代价高昂。

Method: 该方法分阶段逐步收紧全局稀疏约束，并在每个阶段只训练一个预算较小、规模固定的稀疏增量。其核心思路是：逐步移除权重，使每次剪枝造成的变化足够小，让稀疏适配能够补偿损失；适配后的模型再提供端到端梯度，用于指导下一阶段的剪枝决策。

Result: 在参数规模从一亿二千五百万到一百三十亿的六个模型上，该方法在百分之五十至百分之九十的所有测试稀疏率下，困惑度均低于对比方法，最高降低百分之四十二。在百分之九十稀疏率下，剪枝七十亿参数的拉玛二代模型只需一张英伟达高性能计算卡，而对比方法需要四张；总持久状态缩小至五分之一，图形处理器计算时数减少百分之四十九。

Conclusion: 渐进剪枝与小规模稀疏适配有望兼顾极高稀疏率下的模型质量与计算成本，为低成本压缩大型模型提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Weight pruning can reduce the storage and inference cost of large language models (LLMs), but preserving quality at high sparsity levels remains challenging. Existing methods face a quality-cost trade-off in this regime. One-shot pruning is cheap but degrades sharply as sparsity increases, even when followed by post-pruning recovery. A growing line of work instead optimizes the mask directly from the dense model under the end-to-end objective; among these methods, [Lee et al.,2026], the recent state of the art at extreme sparsity, reaches high quality but at the cost of model-sized optimizer state and auxiliary variables. To address this gap, we propose ETAPS, which prunes gradually and adapts sparsely: it tightens a global sparsity constraint in progressive stages while training a sparse delta with a small, fixed budget. Our key observation is that gradual weight removal keeps each pruning step small enough for sparse adaptation to compensate, allowing the adapted model to provide end-to-end gradients that guide the next pruning decision without model-sized optimizer state. Across six models from 125M to 13B parameters, ETAPS achieves lower perplexity than ELSA at every evaluated sparsity from 50% to 90%, with perplexity reductions of up to 42%. ETAPS is also far cheaper: at 90% sparsity, it prunes LLaMA2-7B on a single H200, where ELSA uses four, with $5.0\times$ smaller total persistent state and 49% fewer GPU-hours. Together, our findings point to a promising direction for achieving high quality in demanding regimes such as extreme sparsity at substantially lower cost.

</details>


### [266] [When Does Test-Time Search Help World Action Models? An Empirical Audit of Candidate Selection and Simulator Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41970)
*Jun Liu,Dajie Sun,Zhenglun Kong,Zhenjiang Fan,Peiyan Dong,Fan Yang,Wenbin Zhangg,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Yanzhi Wang,Dong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文对世界动作模型中的测试时扩展进行前提审计：在所测配置中，候选选择信号很弱，计算削减未转化为目标级别的实际加速，自适应计算分配实验也未得出明确结论；同时发现并修复了规划路径中的静默渲染错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展通过增加推理计算、筛选候选动作及自适应分配计算来提升控制效果。但成功率提升只有在候选包含可利用的信息、名义计算节省能转化为实际速度提升、评估过程可靠时才可信。论文旨在系统检验这些前提，而非只看成功率。

Method: 作者在宇宙策略模型和机器人之家基准上，采用预注册、以检验前提为先的审计方法：比较学习型价值选择器与随机选择及事后最优选择的表现，检查不同候选动作的结果差异；测量令牌裁剪对单次前向计算量和端到端速度的影响；在统计功效门槛下评估自适应计算分配；并检查不同规划路径中的渲染完整性，随后发布错误检测器、修复方案和脚本。

Result: 在干净数据上，学习型价值选择器比随机选择低1.66个百分点；事后最优选择仅提高1.40个百分点，且按回合计算的区间包含零。82%的状态中候选动作结果相同，说明可供选择利用的空间有限。令牌裁剪最多减少47%的单次前向计算量，但部署路径端到端加速最高仅为1.15倍；排除解码的路径最高为1.25倍，均未达到1.5倍目标。自适应分配实验因未通过统计功效门槛而无法得出明确结论。作者还发现，静默渲染错误会随规划计算量增加而显著增多，但不影响官方直接策略结果，并提供了检测与修复工具。

Conclusion: 评估测试时扩展不应仅依据成功率，而应逐环节验证候选决策信号、实际效率收益和评估完整性。本研究在所测试配置中发现，这些前提并未得到充分支持；结论不应外推到其他模型或配置。

Abstract: World-action models increasingly apply test-time scaling, spending extra inference computation on candidate selection and adaptive allocation to improve control. Such gains are credible only when candidates carry exploitable signal, nominal compute savings become real speedup, and evaluation stays reliable. We audit all three on Cosmos Policy and RoboCasa with a premise-first, pre-registered methodology. On clean data, a learned value selector is 1.66 pp worse than random; a hindsight oracle gains only 1.40 pp, with an episode-level interval spanning zero; and 82% of states show identical candidate outcomes, leaving little selection headroom. Token pruning cuts per-forward FLOPs by up to 47% but yields at most a measured 1.15$\times$ end-to-end speedup on the deployed path (1.25$\times$ on a decode-excluded path), short of a 1.5$\times$ target, and our adaptive-allocation experiment is inconclusive under a power gate. We further identify and fix a silent rendering corruption whose rate rises sharply along compute-heavy planning paths while leaving the official direct-policy result unaffected, and release a detector, fix, and scripts. These findings show why test-time scaling should be audited as a chain of premises (decision signal, realized efficiency, evaluationintegrity) rather than by success rate alone. Conclusions are scoped to the tested configuration.

</details>


### [267] [ID2Pattern: Pretraining Generalizable Identity Behavior Patterns for Transaction Risk Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41988)
*Ze Zhao,Haoyu Jiang,Weiyu Han,Ruobing Zuo,Xiao Tan,Shuai Chen,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ID2Pattern 将身份字段在交易序列中的出现与复现模式转化为可学习的离散行为表示，从而提升交易预测效果，并能泛化到训练期间未见过的身份值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线支付行为预训练有望学习可泛化表示，但卡号、设备号和账户号等高基数身份字段虽包含丰富行为信号，却因词表持续增长、单个取值缺乏语义而难以用传统词表编码或语义编码有效建模。其可迁移信息主要体现在身份值如何在交易序列中出现和复现，而非具体取值本身。

Method: 提出身份模式预训练框架。首先，使用局部匿名身份标记器对身份字段编码，只描述每个身份在当前序列中的出现与复现情况，不依赖其具体取值；随后，在无标注序列上预训练多尺度模式码本，将匿名身份序列抽象为离散行为模式，并用于下游预测任务。

Result: 在包含超过一亿条序列的工业数据集和六个公开数据集上，该方法持续优于强基线：工业数据集平均提升百分之四点一三，公开数据集平均提升百分之八点八三。即使训练期间所有身份取值均未出现，方法仍保持有效。

Conclusion: 研究表明，围绕身份值的序列行为模式进行建模，能够补足高基数身份字段的传统表示方法，并增强交易基础模型的预测表现及对新用户、新市场的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large-scale behavioral pretraining offers a promising route to generalizable representations in online payment systems. However, high-cardinality identity fields such as card, device, and account IDs, though rich in behavioral signals, remain underexploited. Their continually growing vocabularies and limited semantic meaning make existing vocabulary-based and semantics-based encodings ill-suited. Their generalizable signal lies not in specific values, but in the identity behavior patterns they exhibit across a transaction sequence. Building on this insight, we propose ID2Pattern, a pretraining framework that brings these patterns into transaction foundation models. ID2Pattern first encodes identity fields with a Local Anonymous ID (LAID) tokenizer, which captures how each identity appears and recurs within the sequence, independent of its specific value. It then pretrains a multi-scale pattern codebook on unlabeled sequences, which abstracts LAID sequences into discrete behavior patterns for downstream prediction. Experiments on an industrial dataset with over 100M sequences and six public datasets show that ID2Pattern consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving industrial by 4.13% and public datasets by 8.83% on average. Notably, ID2Pattern remains equally effective even when all identity values are unseen during training, highlighting its potential to generalize to new users and markets.

</details>


### [268] [TradeWar: A Benchmark for Execution-Grounded Trade Negotiation with Adaptive LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42004)
*Xiangyu Kong,Ruirui Yang,Xia Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出多国贸易谈判基准与跨回合自适应智能体，通过将谈判承诺落实为贸易政策并评估执行结果，提升长期谈判的经济效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型谈判基准主要关注是否达成协议，却较少检验协议执行后能否真正创造价值。现实谈判还受到产能与贸易网络等约束，且执行结果可能延迟显现，因此仅凭协议率或对话能力难以评估谈判质量。

Method: 论文构建多国贸易模拟基准，将谈判内容转化为具体的关税和贸易份额政策，并在结算时施加产能与网络约束，使用经济健康指数评价结果。所提智能体不更新基础模型权重，而是跨回合吸收既往经验、对手行为及结算结果，迭代调整谈判策略。

Result: 零样本思维链提示基线的协议率达到百分之七十五，但最终经济健康指数仅为五十七点一；所提方法达到六十八点八。经过二十轮连续回合后，其指数由五十八点二提升至七十三点二，而不进行跨回合更新的消融版本为六十五点二。在技术管制和能源危机冲击下，所提方法的性能下降也小于对比方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明，思维链推理和对话记忆本身不足以解决执行反馈延迟的问题。将实际结算结果纳入跨回合策略调整，有助于提升长期谈判的执行价值与抗冲击能力；该结论目前是在论文构建的模拟基准中得到验证。

Abstract: Multi-turn negotiation remains a challenging problem for large language model (LLM) agents. While LLMs can conduct natural-language dialogue, existing benchmarks predominantly assess only agreement rates, overlooking whether negotiated commitments actually create value once executed. To address this gap, we introduce TradeWar, a multi-country simulation benchmark that translates negotiation dialogues into concrete tariff and trade-share policies, enforces capacity and network constraints during settlement, and evaluates outcomes via an Economic Health Index (EHI). To solve this execution-grounded task, we propose TACTIC (Trade-aware Adaptive Consolidation and Theory-of-mind for International Commerce), an agent that iteratively refines its negotiation strategy across episodes-without updating base LLM weights-by leveraging prior experience, opponent behavior, and past settlement outcomes. Despite using zero-shot chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting, PromptLLM attains a 75% agreement rate but only 57.1 final EHI (*100), whereas TACTIC reaches 68.8. Across 20 sequential episodes and three seeds, TACTIC's final EHI improves from 58.2 to 73.2, compared with 65.2 for an ablation without cross-episode updates. Under technology-controls and energy-crisis shocks, TACTIC also exhibits smaller performance degradation than PromptLLM and MemoryLLM. These results indicate that CoT deliberation and interaction memory alone do not fully address delayed execution feedback, whereas settlement-grounded adaptation improves long-horizon negotiation in TradeWar.

</details>


### [269] [Same Question, Different Answers: Auditing Persona-Dependent Variation in Six LLMs on Smoking-Cessation Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42039)
*Artin Yousefi,Aizierjiang Aiersilan,Lorien C Abroms,David A Broniatowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究审计了六种广泛使用的大语言模型在戒烟建议上的差异，发现回答会随提问者画像而变；加入“事实底线”提示可缩小差异，但效果因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多人向大语言模型寻求健康建议，但不同用户是否会获得同等、完整的医学信息尚不明确。戒烟领域具有明确的临床指导和重要的公共卫生影响，因此适合用于检验模型建议是否因用户特征而不同。

Method: 研究者对来自四家提供方的六种模型开展审计：将四个戒烟问题分别交给十二种在生命阶段、自述专业程度和吸烟经历上不同的用户画像，共收集并分析二千八百八十条回答。随后测试一种可部署干预：在系统提示中规定相关情境下每位用户都应获得的核心医学事实，同时允许模型调整语气、词汇和表达方式。

Result: 回答内容随用户画像而异。与成人画像相比，各模型向青少年画像提及获批处方戒烟药物的比例低三十二至六十七个百分点；六种模型中有五种向自称具备医疗专业知识的画像提供的事实少于向未自称具备专业知识者提供的事实。事实底线提示缩小了所有模型中的画像差异，但对整体事实覆盖率的影响在不同模型间大小不一、方向也不一致，不能假定在一种模型上有效的提示可直接迁移到其他模型。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的健康建议可能因提问者画像而产生系统性差异，事实底线提示有助于缓解差异，但需针对具体模型验证。临床公平并不等于所有人获得完全相同的信息：例如，未成年人通常不适用处方戒烟药物，因此事实底线应结合年龄制定。此外，用户画像同时改变了多个属性，研究结果反映的是整体情境差异，不能单独归因于年龄或专业程度。研究公开了全部回答及分析代码。

Abstract: Hundreds of millions of people now ask large language model (LLM) chatbots for health advice, which raises a question that is simple to pose and hard to answer: does everyone get the same advice? We audit six widely deployed LLMs from four providers on smoking cessation, a domain with codified clini- cal guidance and substantial public-health stakes, by putting the same four cessation questions to twelve user personas that differ in life stage, self-described expertise, and smoking history, for a total of 2,880 responses. What the models say depends on who appears to be asking. Every model named FDA-approved prescription cessation medications less often to teen personas than to adult personas, by 32 to 67 percentage points, and in five of six models a persona presenting health- care expertise received fewer facts than a persona presenting none. We then evaluate one deployable intervention: a short system prompt specifying a factual floor, the set of core med- ical facts every user should receive when they are relevant, while leaving tone, vocabulary, and framing free to adapt. The prompt narrows persona differences in every model, but its effect on overall fact coverage varies in both magnitude and direction across models, so a prompt validated on one system cannot be assumed to transfer to another. Two cautions frame these results. Equal mention rates are not always the correct clinical target, because prescription cessation pharmacother- apy is not recommended for minors, so a defensible factual floor must be age-aware rather than uniform. And because our personas vary on several attributes at once, we report scenario- level differences rather than isolated effects of age or expertise. We release all 2,880 responses and the analysis code.

</details>


### [270] [CausalWAM: Learning the Causes of Actions in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42096)
*Juntao Cheng,Jinzhou Tang,Fan Feng,Henrik I. Christensen,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出因果世界动作模型，通过对动作生成路径施加稀疏约束，将动作相关因素与仅影响场景预测的无关因素分离，从而提升动作生成的可识别性与闭环效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型需要预测完整场景，但生成动作通常只依赖场景中的一部分因素。仅凭示范数据训练，模型可能把动作相关因素与无关因素纠缠起来，仍能模仿示范，却无法确保动作只由真正相关的潜在因素决定。

Method: 作者在理论上证明：施加稀疏约束后，用于生成动作的潜在变量是动作相关潜在因素的可逆函数，真实结构可在潜变量置换意义下被识别。该结论不要求充分的时间依赖，也不要求对未观测机制的演化作假设，并允许动作相关因素与其他因素存在统计依赖。基于此，作者提出因果世界动作模型，在预训练模型的动作输出头中加入稀疏路由路径，同时保持原有世界建模目标不变。

Result: 合成数据与真实机器人实验验证了理论结论。实验还显示，该方法能够提高闭环任务成功效率。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离动作生成所依赖的因素与场景预测所依赖的因素，并对动作路径施加稀疏约束，可以缓解潜变量纠缠，使动作生成更具因果针对性。该方法在较弱假设下实现结构识别，并得到实验验证。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) learn latent variables from observations and use these to both predict the evolution of the scene and to generate actions. The prediction has to be of the whole scene, whereas only a portion of the scene causes actions. WAMs fitted to demonstrations do not, in general, guarantee that they will generate actions solely from the latent causes of the demonstrated actions (the action-relevant latent variables), as the model could just as well learn to imitate in this way whilst entangling the action-relevant latent variables with the nuisance variables. We therefore separate out what causes action from what prediction depends on. We prove that under a sparsity constraint the latent variables used to generate actions are an invertible function of the action-relevant latent variables alone and the true structure is identified up to a permutation of the latent variables. This requires neither sufficient temporal dependence nor any assumptions on how the unobserved regimes evolve and holds even when the action-relevant latent variables are statistically dependent on the rest. We therefore propose CWAM (Causal WAM), which incorporates the constraint into a pretrained WAM by adding a sparse routing path to its action head, with the world modeling objective remaining unchanged. Experiments on both synthetic data and real-world robots validate the theory as well as showcase the improved efficiency in closed-loop success.

</details>


### [271] [ACCT: Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42116)
*Yuzhe Liu,Haolin Tian,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨模型上下文迁移方法，可将同一模型家族中不同规模模型生成的键值缓存转换为目标模型可直接使用的稀疏状态，减少重复处理上下文的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构模型部署中，同一上下文常被多个模型重复使用，但每次切换目标模型都要重新进行上下文预填充，带来显著计算成本。现有缓存难以直接跨模型复用，因此需要在不同规模模型之间迁移上下文计算结果，并控制缓存开销。

Method: 该方法面向同一模型家族内不同规模模型间的缓存迁移，由三个轻量组件构成：映射器为目标模型的每一层和键值头识别与查询相关的上下文词元；分配器在总缓存预算约束下决定各层、各头保留的词元数量；编译器将源模型缓存转换为目标模型可直接读取的稀疏缓存。由此，目标模型无需重新处理原始上下文即可开展推理。

Result: 实验覆盖五项通用能力基准和六项高度依赖上下文的任务。缓存规模减少百分之七十五时，该方法仍达到或超过目标模型原有推理表现。在约一万九千词元的上下文长度下，缓存转换速度相较目标模型重新进行上下文预填充提升六点五至二十一点二倍。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在有限缓存预算下，不同规模模型之间迁移上下文计算结果具有可行性。该方法兼顾推理质量与转换效率，有望降低异构模型部署中重复处理上下文的成本。

Abstract: In real-world deployments, large language model (LLM) systems are increasingly evolving from single-model invocation toward heterogeneous deployments in which different models are dynamically invoked for inference. When the same context is reused across models, however, each newly selected target model typically has to process it again, repeatedly incurring substantial computational costs. Therefore, we propose ACCT (Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer), which targets KV cache transfer between models of different scales within the same model family. ACCT transforms the KV cache produced by a source model into sparse KV states that can be directly consumed by a target model, thereby enabling cross-model reuse of context computation and decoupling context computation from downstream inference. ACCT consists of three lightweight components: the Cartographer identifies query-relevant context tokens for each target layer and KV head; the Allocator determines how many context tokens each target layer and KV head retains under a shared total KV budget; and the Compiler transforms selected source KV into sparse target KV. The target model can therefore perform inference using the transferred KV without reprocessing the original context. We evaluate ACCT in two complementary settings comprising five general-capability benchmarks and six highly context-dependent tasks. Experiments show that ACCT matches or exceeds the target model's original inference performance even when reducing the KV cache size by 75%. At a context length of approximately 19K tokens, ACCT's KV conversion achieves a 6.5-21.2 times speedup over target context prefill. These results demonstrate the feasibility of transferring context computation across models under a limited KV budget.

</details>


### [272] [Latent Sparse Attention: Towards Subquadratic Ultra-Long Context Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42147)
*Ales Kubicek,Andrea Caciolai,Liangzu Peng,Sukrit Sumant,Benjamin Hoover,Fraser Greenlee,Ruairidh M. Battleday,Radim Urban,Torsten Hoefler,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出潜在稀疏注意力，通过在可学习的潜在空间中对键进行层次聚类，使查询只需评估相关簇，而非逐一检索所有键，从而在超长上下文中显著加速注意力计算并保持准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向开放式科研等长时程任务，模型需要处理超过百万词元的上下文，但全注意力成本过高。现有稀疏注意力索引方法要么仍随上下文长度呈平方级增长，要么在超长上下文下准确率下降，因此需要兼顾效率与准确性的索引机制。

Method: 潜在稀疏注意力将键组织成可学习潜在空间中的层次簇，查询先对簇评分，再从相关簇中选择键，降低预填充、解码和训练阶段的计算开销。论文提出两种变体：蒸馏式潜在稀疏注意力，可通过蒸馏适配预训练模型；原生潜在稀疏注意力，则仅依靠下一词元预测损失进行训练。

Result: 在一千万词元上下文下，蒸馏式方法相较原模型的稀疏注意力，单层预填充最高加速二十二点七倍；相较全注意力，预填充和解码分别最高加速七十二点四倍和四十二点五倍，同时在多类任务上保持相近准确率。原生方法在十二万八千词元上下文下，每层训练速度提升一点六倍，并达到与全注意力相当的准确率。

Conclusion: 层次化潜在空间索引为超长上下文注意力提供了一种实用的降耗方案。蒸馏与原生训练两种路径分别支持已有模型改造和从训练阶段直接采用，有望使模型更高效地利用超长上下文。

Abstract: Agentic workflows are increasingly shifting towards long-horizon tasks, such as open-ended scientific work, where models benefit from long context windows. However, at ultra-long context (above 1M tokens), full attention is prohibitively expensive. A promising approach is sparse attention, in which a dedicated indexer selects a subset of keys for the attention. Yet current methods of indexing either still have quadratic cost in context size or lose accuracy at ultra-long context scale. We address this with Latent Sparse Attention (LSA), an efficient attention method that maintains accuracy at ultra-long context. In LSA, keys are hierarchically clustered in a learned latent space, and queries score the hierarchy of clusters instead of scoring every key, making prefill, decode and training faster at long context. We propose two variants, Distilled-LSA (D-LSA), which can be applied to pre-trained models via distillation, and Native-LSA (N-LSA), which is trained natively on the next-token loss alone. On GLM-5.3-FLASH (QWEN-3.8-27B) at 10M tokens, D-LSA speeds up one attention layer by 22.7x (72.4x) in prefill and 4.6x (42.5x) in decode over its original sparse attention (full attention), while keeping similar accuracy across a variety of tasks. N-LSA matches the accuracy of full attention with a training speedup of 1.6x per layer at 128k tokens. LSA offers practical and efficient attention for models to benefit from ultra-long context.

</details>
